### [1] [TOKI: A Bitemporal Operator Algebra for Contradiction Resolution in LLM-Agent Persistent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06240)
*Ziming Wang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: TOKI 将持久化记忆的冲突消解形式化为写时并发控制，通过双行位时态模式、隔离前置条件和审计行机制提供显式的正确性契约，证明四种生产级启发式方法在隔离性、模式和溯源上的完备性，并揭示现有系统均存在写时异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产系统中广泛使用四种冲突消解启发式方法（最后写入者胜出、证据加权合并、等待确认、按规则策略），但没有任何一个声明其假设的隔离级别或允许的写时异常。冲突消解本质上是写时并发控制，但这一契约从未被明确化。

Method: 将四种启发式方法类型化为一族基于双行模式（dual-row schema）的位时态算子，每个算子附带隔离前置条件和溯源标注（审计行保留失败事实）。通过四条完备性定理在隔离、模式和溯源之间闭合契约，并推广到算子管道和n元冲突集。紧致性证明表明仲裁者的键控日志记录对于重放一致性是必要的。

Result: 异常判定矩阵显示：所有在写路径上保留语言模型裁判的系统都存在至少一种写时异常（重放不一致、信念漂移偏差、审计擦除）；基于内容寻址的引擎层比较器通过移除裁判避免异常；TOKI 在保留裁判的同时排除所有三种异常。在 LoCoMo 子集上审计行防御提升 0.86 分，消融实验下降 0.49 准确率。

Conclusion: 核心贡献是写时正确性规范契约，在隔离性、模式和溯源上被证明完整，明确了生产级启发式方法所假设但从未被显式声明的保证。跨系统比较统计效力不足，不声称优越性。

Abstract: Persistent memory for an LLM agent is a write-heavy substrate: every belief update is a versioned write, and a new claim may contradict a stored one. Production systems use four resolution heuristics (last-writer-wins, evidence-weighted merge, await-confirmation, per-rule policy), yet none declares the isolation level it assumes or the write-time anomalies it admits. We show that contradiction resolution is write-time concurrency control and make the missing contract explicit. TOKI types the four heuristics as one family of bitemporal operators over a dual-row schema, each with an isolation precondition and a provenance annotation that preserves the losing fact in an audit row. Four soundness theorems close the contract across isolation, schema, and provenance, lift the guarantees to operator pipelines, and extend the fold operators to n-ary conflict sets. A tightness companion proves that, within the relational schedule model, keyed logging of the adjudicating judge is necessary for replay consistency, which every audited baseline omits. A verdict matrix over eight systems localizes the gap: every baseline that keeps a language-model judge on the write path admits at least one of three write-time anomalies (replay inconsistency, belief-drift skew, audit erasure); a content-addressed engine-layer comparator avoids them only by removing the judge, and TOKI alone excludes all three while keeping it. On its one natural-workload slice the audit-row defence moves LoCoMo by 0.86, and ablating the typed memory layer removes 0.49 accuracy on 1,444 answerable LoCoMo questions; the cross-system comparison stays underpowered and claims no superiority. The contribution is the contract: a write-time correctness specification, proved sound across isolation, schema, and provenance, pinning the guarantee every production heuristic assumes but no deployed system makes explicit.

</details>

### [2] [Hub-Aware Hybrid Search: Accelerating the Locally Aligned Ant Technique](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06198)
*Simone Vilardi,Reynier Peletier,Felipe Contreras,Kerstin Bunte*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 针对LAAT算法在密集枢纽区域计算开销大的问题，提出两阶段解决方案：预处理阶段定位密集枢纽并用似然模型替代，随后采用混合似然-信息素策略引导蚂蚁高效穿越密集区域，在合成数据和宇宙网N体模拟中验证了检测效率和鲁棒性的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高噪声、高维点云数据中检测流形结构（如宇宙网中的纤维、流、墙和团簇）具有重要科学意义，但现有LAAT算法在处理密集枢纽（如节点或球状星团）时会产生不必要的计算开销，影响检测效率。

Method: 提出两阶段解决方案：1) 快速预处理步骤定位密集枢纽，并将其替换为定制化的似然模型；2) 采用混合似然-信息素策略引导蚂蚁高效穿越密集区域，避免计算浪费。

Result: 在合成数据和大型天文N体模拟（宇宙网）实验中，该方法显著提升了LAAT的检测效率和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的两阶段优化方法有效解决了LAAT算法在密集枢纽区域的计算瓶颈问题，增强了算法在宇宙大尺度结构检测中的实用性和可靠性。

Abstract: Finding manifold structures in noisy and high-dimensional point clouds is a challenging but important problem. In astronomical observation survey and simulation data the detection of filaments, streams (1D), walls (2D) and clusters (3D) gives rise to deeper understanding of the evolution of our universe. The Locally Aligned Ant Technique (LAAT) uses biologically inspired agents to efficiently recover faint and multidimensional structures. However, very dense hubs (e.g. nodes or globular clusters) dominate the ants' activity, creating unnecessary computational overheads. In this paper we propose a two-stage solution. First a fast preprocessing step locates the hubs and replaces them with a tailored likelihood model. Subsequently, a mixed likelihood-pheromone strategy guides the ants to efficiently bridge the dense regions. We demonstrate improvements in detection efficiency and robustness of LAAT with synthetic and a large-scale astronomical N-body simulation of the cosmic web.

</details>

### [3] [AppAgent-Claw: CLI Is All You Need for GUI Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05171)
*Zhixue Song,Zhiheng Zhang,Yi Song,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: AppAgent-Claw通过"一次录制，多次回放"的范式，将GUI工作流转化为无需运行时推理的可复用技能，集成到OpenClaw生态系统中，解决了LLM-based GUI智能体速度慢、成本高、不一致的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OpenClaw平台的技能导向架构虽好，但许多现实任务局限于没有稳定API的GUI界面；现有的LLM-based GUI智能体依赖重复的实时模型推理，速度慢、成本高且结果不一致，无法作为高效的OpenClaw技能使用。

Method: 采用"record-once, replay-many"范式，捕获丰富的上下文元数据以支持稳健执行；使用分层定位策略处理视觉变化；采用验证耦合执行模型确保预期屏幕效果。

Result: AppAgent-Claw提供了一种实用、高效且可诊断的方案，将GUI绑定任务集成到OpenClaw生态系统中。

Conclusion: AppAgent-Claw通过演示驱动的方式，无需运行时推理即可将GUI工作流转化为可靠、可复用的技能，为OpenClaw平台自动化提供了有效的补充。

Abstract: The OpenClaw platform provides a practical foundation for automation through its skill-oriented architecture, organizing external capabilities into lightweight, reusable components that can be invoked efficiently through a command-line interface (CLI). However, a significant bottleneck remains: many real-world tasks are confined to graphical user interfaces (GUIs) with no stable API available. While LLM-based GUI agents offer generality, their reliance on repeated live model inference makes them too slow, costly, and inconsistent to serve as efficient OpenClaw skills. In this paper, we present AppAgent-Claw, a demonstration-driven system that converts GUI workflows into reliable, reusable skills without runtime inference. By following a ``record-once, replay-many'' paradigm, the system captures rich contextual metadata to facilitate robust execution. It employs a layered localization strategy to handle visual shifts and a validation-coupled execution model to ensure intended on-screen effects. AppAgent-Claw provides a practical, efficient, and diagnosable solution for integrating GUI-bound tasks into the OpenClaw ecosystem.

</details>


### [4] [Is This Edit Correct? A Multi-Dimensional Benchmark for Reasoning-Aware Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05172)
*Yixuan Ding,Wei Huang,Ruijie Quan,Xiaojuan Qi,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出RE-Edit基准，从物理、环境、文化、因果和指代五个推理维度评估图像编辑系统的隐含逻辑约束理解能力，发现即使先进系统也常缺乏多维度隐含推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型图像编辑系统在遵循自然语言指令时往往只进行表面指令执行，缺乏对用户请求中隐含上下文约束的推理，导致视觉上合理但逻辑不一致的编辑结果。

Method: 构建RE-Edit基准，包含1000个精心设计的样本，覆盖五个推理维度（物理、环境、文化、因果、指代）；设计维度对齐的评估标准；提出轻量级推理引导的后编辑基线方法。

Result: 对10个开源和2个商业图像编辑模型的全面评估表明，即使先进系统也常因缺乏隐含多维度推理而产生逻辑不一致的编辑，尽管其视觉质量很高。

Conclusion: 提出了一种模型无关的推理引导后编辑基线作为初步探索，证明显式引入推理机制有助于缓解图像编辑中的逻辑不一致问题。

Abstract: Diffusion-based image editing has achieved strong visual fidelity under natural language instructions, yet most existing systems still operate at the level of surface instruction following, without reasoning about the implicit contextual constraints embedded in real user requests. This often leads to visually plausible but logically inconsistent edits. In this work, we introduce RE-Edit, a benchmark for REasoning-aware image Editing that evaluates image editing systems across five complementary reasoning dimensions: physical, environmental, cultural, causal, and referential. RE-Edit comprises 1,000 carefully curated samples, each designed such that visual plausibility alone is insufficient and correct editing requires satisfying implicit logical constraints. To support fine-grained analysis, we establish dimension-aligned evaluation criteria and conduct a comprehensive study of ten open-source and two commercial image editing models. Our results show that even advanced systems frequently struggle with implicit multi-dimensional reasoning despite producing high-quality visuals. We further present a lightweight reasoning-guided post-edit baseline as an initial exploration, illustrating how inserting explicit reasoning can help mitigate such failures in a model-agnostic manner.

</details>


### [5] [The Virtual Roundtable: Multi-Agent Personas Simulating the Dynamics of Human Brainstorming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05178)
*Tim Dorn,Saara A. Khan,Julie Mumford*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多智能体架构，通过发散-收敛两阶段模拟圆桌头脑风暴，利用多样化AI角色和代理主持人自动生成并评估产品创意。案例研究（AI智能眼镜）表明该方法能产生多样、相关的创意，且角色间的观点交流能逐步提升讨论质量和创意深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人类头脑风暴存在群体思维、回音室效应和多样性不足等问题，而AI驱动产品开发的瓶颈正从"如何构建"转向"构建什么"，因此需要一种新的自动化创意生成方法。

Method: 提出多智能体架构，包含两个阶段：(1) 发散思维阶段——多个多样化AI角色参与圆桌讨论，每个角色保留私人想法同时公开发表评论，创意在讨论中自然涌现；(2) 收敛思维阶段——评估并排序最有前景的创意。系统包含代理主持人引导讨论方向，对每个角色设置创意提交和投票配额以促进平衡参与，并全程追踪创意的演化谱系。

Result: 通过AI智能眼镜的案例研究证明：(i) 该方法能产生多样、相关的创意，并能洞察创意的演化过程；(ii) 角色间累积的观点交流培育了共享语境，逐步深化了讨论质量和创意产出。

Conclusion: 该多智能体架构有效地模拟了人类圆桌讨论过程，通过角色间的观点交叉传播和共享语境的构建，能够系统性地生成更高质量和更多样化的产品创意。

Abstract: As AI-driven product development accelerates, the bottleneck is shifting from how we build to what we build. Traditional human brainstorming faces challenges including groupthink, echo chambers, and limited diversity. To address this, we present a multi-agentic architecture that simulates roundtable brainstorming through two phases: divergent thinking to generate diverse ideas, and convergent thinking to evaluate and rank the most promising ones. The system employs diverse AI personas that engage in roundtable discussions, guided by an agentic facilitator that steers the discussion toward productive outcomes. Personas maintain private thoughts while commenting publicly, with ideas emerging organically throughout the discussion. Per-persona quotas on idea submissions and votes promote balanced participation while producing natural rankings. Throughout the session, the system tracks each idea's lineage, capturing how concepts originate and cross-pollinate over time. We demonstrate this approach through a case study generating consumer ideas for AI smart glasses, showing (i) it produces diverse, relevant ideas with insights into their evolution; (ii) the cumulative exchange of perspectives across personas cultivates a shared context that progressively deepens the quality of discussion and the ideas produced.

</details>


### [6] [The Role of Instructional Guidance in Generative AI-Assisted Learning: Empirical Evidence from Construction Engineering Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05509)
*Xiaoyu Hou,Bo Xiao,Hexu Liu,Shane Mueller*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于生成学习理论的五步提示框架，通过控制实验比较了幻灯片学习、无提示AI学习和有提示AI学习三种条件。结果发现，有提示的AI学习在需要解释和推理的开放题任务上显著优于其他条件（提高约2-3分，p<0.01），而在选择题上无显著差异。研究表明AI学习效果取决于交互结构的设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI越来越多地用于支持自主学习，但学生与这类系统的互动往往缺乏结构，限制了对深层认知过程的参与。本研究旨在探究教学指导如何塑造学生与AI在建筑教育中的互动。

Method: 基于生成学习理论提出五步提示框架；进行控制实验，比较幻灯片学习、无提示AI学习和有提示AI学习三种条件；使用多选题和开放题评估学习表现，使用用户体验问卷（UEQ）测量用户体验。

Result: 有提示条件在开放题得分上显著高于其他条件（提高约2-3分，满分18分，p<0.01）；选择题表现无显著差异；无提示条件与幻灯片学习效果相当。

Conclusion: AI支持学习的效果取决于交互的结构化程度；所提出的框架为将学习科学原则整合到建筑教育的生成式AI系统中提供了基础。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly used to support self-directed learning, yet student interaction with such systems often remains unstructured, limiting engagement in deeper cognitive processes. This study examines how instructional guidance shapes student and AI interaction in construction education. A five-step prompting framework grounded in Generative Learning Theory (GLT) is introduced to guide learner interaction during review activities. A controlled experiment compares three learning conditions: slide-based learning, unprompted AI-supported learning, and prompted AI-supported learning. Learning performance is assessed using multiple-choice and open-ended tasks, and user experience is measured using the User Experience Questionnaire (UEQ). Performance differences are concentrated on tasks requiring explanation and reasoning. The prompted condition achieves higher open-ended scores, with an improvement of approximately 2 or 3 points on a scale of 18 (p < 0.01), while no significant differences are observed in multiple-choice performance. The unprompted condition remains comparable to slide-based learning. These findings indicate that the effectiveness of AI-supported learning depends on how interaction is structured. The proposed framework provides a basis for integrating learning science principles into generative AI systems for construction education.

</details>


### [7] [Architecting Strategic Influence: Operationalising the UXR Point of View Framework for Research Function Maturity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05826)
*Rohinin Singh,Renee Barsoum*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过案例研究展示，系统应用UXR POV框架可作为用户体验研究部门从孵化期向成熟期转型的有效操作脚手架，通过整合"进攻性"和"防御性"策略的Playbook，帮助UXR领导者应对利益相关者偏见、被动任务分配和洞察碎片化等组织障碍，从而将研究职能从战术执行提升为战略合作伙伴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UXR部门在从孵化期向成熟期转型过程中面临 stakeholder bias（利益相关者偏见）、reactive tasking（被动任务分配）和 insight fragmentation（洞察碎片化）等系统性组织障碍，需要系统化的操作框架来支撑这一关键转型。

Method: 采用案例研究方法，系统应用UXR POV（Point of View）框架，整合结构化的"Offensive"（进攻性）和"Defensive"（防御性）双轨策略，构建可操作的Playbook工具包。

Result: 所提出的Playbook驱动的成熟度策略能够显著增强研究职能作为战略合作伙伴的可能性，使其能够交付基于证据、可操作的观点，并直接塑造长期业务战略。

Conclusion: 系统化、有目的地应用增长策略，能够使研究职能超越战术执行层面，直接参与并影响长期业务战略的制定，从而完成从执行角色向战略角色的关键跃迁。

Abstract: This case study illustrates that the systematic application of the User Experience Research (UXR) Point of View (POV) framework serves as an effective operational scaffolding for a UXR function undergoing the critical transition from incubation to maturity. By assimilating structured 'Offensive' and 'Defensive' strategies, the presented Playbook equips UXR leaders with an adaptable toolkit to systematically navigate common institutional barriers, such as stakeholder bias, reactive tasking, and insight fragmentation. By pre-emptive and purposeful application of growth strategies, the likelihood of the research function establishing itself as a strategic partner capable of delivering evidence-based, actionable perspectives is significantly enhanced. The analysis demonstrates how this deliberate, Playbook-driven maturity strategy empowers research functions to move beyond tactical execution and directly shape long-term business strategy.

</details>


### [8] [EEGDancer: Dynamic Emotion Latent Space Masked Modeling with Reinforcement Learning for EEG Continuous Emotion Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05855)
*Zhihao Zhou,Weishan Ye,Li Zhang,Gan Huang,Zhen Liang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: EEGDancer提出了一种动态情感潜在空间学习框架，通过向量量化表示学习、掩码时序建模和强化学习轨迹优化，实现了对连续脑电情感演变的有效预测，在多个数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续EEG情感预测方法主要依赖逐点回归和直接建模高噪声的EEG特征，难以捕捉长时间跨度的时序依赖和连贯的情感动态变化。

Method: 1) 因果时空VQ-VAE学习结构化情感原型，构建离散-连续情感潜在空间；2) Transformer掩码动态建模捕获长程情感依赖和时间演变模式；3) 将连续情感预测建模为序列决策问题，引入SAC框架优化序列级情感预测轨迹。

Result: 在SEED、SEED-IV和Long-Term Naturalistic Emotion数据集上，EEGDancer一致优于现有机器学习和深度学习方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的潜在空间学习和基于强化学习的轨迹优化对建模连续EEG情感动态是有效的。

Abstract: Continuous electroencephalography (EEG) emotion prediction aims to model the temporal evolution of human emotional states from EEG signals. Unlike conventional discrete emotion recognition, continuous prediction requires capturing long-range temporal dependencies and coherent emotional dynamics. However, existing methods mainly rely on point-wise regression and directly model noisy high-dimensional EEG features, limiting their ability to characterize continuous emotional evolution.To address these challenges, we propose EEGDancer, a dynamic emotional latent space learning framework for continuous EEG emotion prediction. The framework integrates vector-quantized representation learning, masked temporal modeling, and reinforcement learning-based trajectory optimization into a unified architecture.Specifically, a causal spatiotemporal Vector-Quantization Variational Autoencoder (VQ-VAE) is designed to learn structured emotional prototypes and construct a discrete-continuous emotional latent space from EEG signals. Based on the learned latent representations, a Transformer-based masked dynamic modeling strategy captures long-range emotional dependencies and temporal evolution patterns. Furthermore, continuous emotion prediction is formulated as a sequential decision-making problem, and a Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) framework is introduced to optimize emotional prediction trajectories at the sequence level instead of frame-wise local fitting.Extensive experiments on the SEED, SEED-IV, and Long-Term Naturalistic Emotion datasets demonstrate that EEGDancer consistently outperforms existing machine learning and deep learning methods. Ablation studies further verify the effectiveness of the proposed latent space and reinforcement learning-based trajectory optimization for modeling continuous EEG emotional dynamics.

</details>


### [9] [Empathy on Demand: How Empathic AI Can Scale Emotional Support for Verbal Harassment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05995)
*Anouk Bergner,Philipp Winder,Christian Hildebrand*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究揭示了大语言模型（LLM）在言语骚扰情境下产生的共情倾听语言信号（观点采择、情感验证、行动导向）强于人类非专家和心理健康专业人员，能够增强被倾听感和应对自我效能，表明AI可作为可扩展的情感支持来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 言语骚扰是全球日益普遍的心理压力源，但许多人缺乏及时有效的情感支持，人们越来越多地转向对话型AI寻求支持，需要检验AI能否产生与人类共情类似的心理效益。

Method: 开发了一个识别共情倾听三种关键语言信号（观点采择、情感验证、行动导向）的心理学框架；比较LLM、人类非专家和心理健康专业人员的共情倾听语言标记；通过行为实验检验这些语言信号对被倾听感和应对自我效能的影响。

Result: LLM一致地产生比人类非专家和心理健康专业人员更强的共情倾听语言标记；这些语言信号促进了更多接近导向（而非回避导向）的应对策略；行为实验验证了这些信号能增强被倾听感和应对自我效能。

Conclusion: 特定的语言特征能够在人类与高级对话AI之间建立共情连接，增强心理韧性；AI有潜力在人类支持不可用或不足时作为可扩展的情感支持来源。

Abstract: Verbal harassment is a growing source of psychological stress for people around the world. It occurs both online and offline and relies on language to demean, threaten, or discredit its targets. Unlike other stressors such as loss or uncertainty, verbal harassment aims at silencing its targets by eroding their sense of being heard and weakening their perceived ability to respond. Many individuals lack access to adequate and timely support, however, when they experience such harassment. People increasingly turn to conversational artificial intelligence (AI) such as ChatGPT or dedicated AI companions for emotional support, raising questions about whether it can facilitate the same psychological benefits as actual human empathy. We focus on online contexts as a prevalent application of verbal harassment. We develop and test a psychological framework identifying three key linguistic signals of empathic listening (perspective-taking, emotional validation, and action orientation), that together restore a sense of feeling heard and enhance coping in the context of verbal harassment. We find that LLMs consistently produce language exhibiting stronger empathic-listening markers than human non-experts and trained mental health professionals, promoting more approach-oriented (vs. avoidance-oriented) coping strategies. A subsequent behavioral study shows that these linguistic signals boost recipients' sense of feeling heard and increase their coping self-efficacy. These findings reveal how specific linguistic features create empathic connections between humans and advanced conversational AI and can enhance people's psychological resilience. Our results highlight the potential for AI to serve as a scalable source of emotional support, especially when human support is unavailable or insufficient.

</details>


### [10] [Deterring Searches for Child Sexual Abuse Material on Google Search and Promoting Help-Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06126)
*Rebecca Umbach,Griffin Hunt,John Buckley,Joel Scanlan,Caoilte Ó Ciardha,Ethel Quayle,Ainslie Heasman,Maximlian von Heyden,Elizabeth Letourneau,Donald Findlater,Tegan Insoll,Richard Wortley,Chad Steel,Abhishek Roy*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 谷歌搜索在用户搜索儿童性虐待材料（CSAM）时部署"Onebox"功能。本研究评估了该功能的战略转变——从以举报为重点的干预措施转向以后果警示和治疗资源为重点的新干预措施。基于谷歌内部搜索日志数据的双重差分分析发现，新信息使同一搜索会话中后续CSAM相关查询减少了3.8个百分点。帮助资源超链接按钮的平均点击率为0.73%。研究提供了趋同证据，表明部分个体可以被劝阻继续搜寻CSAM并转向治疗服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估谷歌搜索CSAM时"Onebox"功能从举报导向转向后果警示与治疗资源导向的战略转变的实际效果，检验是否能够有效减少用户的CSAM搜索行为并引导其获取帮助。

Method: 采用双重差分（Difference-in-Differences）分析方法，基于谷歌内部搜索日志数据，比较新旧两种干预信息对用户后续CSAM相关查询行为的影响，同时统计帮助资源链接的点击率。

Result: 新干预信息使同一搜索会话中后续CSAM相关查询降低了3.8个百分点；帮助资源超链接按钮的平均点击率为0.73%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过调整搜索结果页面的干预信息（强调后果和治疗资源而非单纯举报），能够有效劝阻部分用户继续搜寻CSAM，并引导其转向治疗服务，为减少在线CSAM搜索行为提供了实证支持。

Abstract: Google Search deploys a "Onebox" feature at the top of the results page when users conduct searches for Child Sexual Abuse Material. This study evaluates the impact of a strategic shift in this feature, comparing a revised intervention, focused on repercussions and therapeutic resources, to a previous iteration that focused on reporting. Using a difference-in-differences analysis of internal Google Search logs data, we found the new messaging resulted in a 3.8 percentage point reduction as compared to the status quo in subsequent CSAM-related queries within the same Search session. We found an average click through rate of 0.73% on any of the hyperlinked buttons to help-providing resources. Together, this research presents convergent evidence that a subset of individuals can be deterred from ongoing CSAM-seeking and redirected to therapeutic services.

</details>


### [11] [A MATLAB Toolbox for Standardized Reading Speed Assessment: Implementing and Extending the Perrin Sentence Generator for English Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06297)
*Daniel P. Spiegel,Romain Bachy*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文针对静态阅读图表在测量阅读速度时存在的记忆效应和言语运动混淆问题，介绍了一个开源MATLAB工具箱。该系统基于Perrin等人(2014)的句子生成范式，利用语义本体和"原型真理"逻辑自动生成数千个独特的语法简单句子，并引入带通心理语言学过滤和语义消歧逻辑，用于准确测量最大阅读速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统静态阅读图表在测量阅读速度时存在的两个主要方法学障碍：重复测试导致的记忆效应，以及口头朗读引入的言语运动混淆，后者掩盖了真实的信息处理速度。

Method: 基于Perrin, Paillé和Baccino (2014)的句子生成范式，开发开源MATLAB工具箱。采用语义本体和"原型真理"逻辑自动生成数千个独特的语法简单句子，并引入带通心理语言学过滤和针对英语特有语义歧义的消歧逻辑。

Result: 开发了一套完整的开源软件包，能够自主生成大量独特句子，并在最大阅读速度测量基础上实现了带通心理语言学过滤和英语语义消歧功能。

Conclusion: 该开源平台可为科学界提供验证和改进阅读速度测量的工具，有望解决传统方法中的记忆效应和言语运动混淆问题。

Abstract: In the fields of vision science, cognitive psychology, and psycholinguistics, the accurate measurement of reading speed is frequently hampered by the limitations of static reading charts. Repeated testing often leads to memorization effects, while the requirement for oral recitation introduces speech-motor confounds that obscure true information processing speed. To address these methodological hurdles, this paper introduces an open-source MATLAB toolbox that adapts the sentence generation paradigm originally proposed by Perrin, Paillé, and Baccino (2014) for the English language. This system utilizes a semantic ontology and a "proto-truth" logic to autonomously generate thousands of unique, grammatically simple sentences with unambiguous truth values. Beyond the original scope of Maximum Reading Speed (MRS) measurement, this implementation introduces band-pass psycholinguistic filtering and specific logic to resolve semantic ambiguities unique to English. We present this complete software package as an open platform for the scientific community to validate and refine.

</details>


### [12] [Computational Modeling of Human Adaptation in Urban Infrastructure Management under Extreme Conditions: A Case Study of Subway Flood Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06429)
*Jinfeng Lou,Zijie Liang,Pengkun Liu,Yuxin Zhang,Cleotilde Gonzalez,Pingbo Tang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究将实例学习理论(IBLT)整合到土木工程计算中，建立了一个模拟人工操作员决策过程的计算认知架构，通过记忆检索和效用混合的数学机制来分离心理偏差。在模拟地铁洪水道轨中断实验中，该模型揭示了人类操作员的四阶段非线性适应周期（获取、过度自信、过度纠正、重新校准），并证实事故后的过度纠正阶段源于急性心理偏差压倒了稳定的记忆基础适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端事件下城市基础设施管理的决策严重依赖人类操作员，但当前计算支持系统未能考虑非单调的人类适应过程以及过度自信、防御性过度纠正等潜在心理偏差。现有系统无法算法化地隔离这些偏差，需要建立能模拟有界理性适应过程的认知架构作为基线。

Method: 基于实例学习理论(IBLT)构建计算认知架构，通过记忆检索和效用混合的数学机制模拟操作员决策过程。该模型作为计算基线，表示由经验先验驱动的有界理性适应，通过人类参与的微观世界实验（模拟地铁洪水道轨中断场景）验证，分析操作员在乘客安全与服务效率间的平衡决策。

Result: 揭示了人类操作员的四阶段非线性适应周期：获取、过度自信、过度纠正、重新校准。在事故后的"过度纠正"阶段，模型与人类行为出现显著差异：人类操作员表现出即时的防御性风险高估，而模型基于累积经验保持稳定轨迹。证实了操作不稳定性源于急性心理偏差压倒稳定记忆基础适应。

Conclusion: 所建立的IBLT认知架构能有效分离心理偏差与记忆基线的适应动态，证明了失败后操作不稳定性常归因于急性心理偏差压制记忆基础适应。该模式预期在类似高风险环境中具有可泛化性，可通过多模态行为与传感器数据在专业操作员中验证。

Abstract: Decision-making in urban infrastructure management during extreme events relies heavily on human operators, yet current computational support systems often fail to account for non-monotonic human adaptation and latent psychological biases like overconfidence and defensive overcorrection. This study addresses this gap by integrating Instance-Based Learning Theory (IBLT) into the domain of civil engineering computing. We establish a computational cognitive architecture that simulates operator decision processes through the mathematical mechanisms of memory retrieval and utility blending. This model functions as a computational baseline, representing boundedly rational adaptation driven by experiential priors, thus allowing for the algorithmic isolation of latent psychological biases from the baseline dynamics of memory-based learning. We demonstrated this framework using a human-in-the-loop microworld experiment simulating subway flood-induced track suspensions, where dispatchers must balance passenger safety against service efficiency. Analysis revealed a complex, non-linear human adaptation cycle consisting of four phases: acquisition, overconfidence, overcorrection, and recalibration. Specifically, the computational model exposed a significant divergence during the post-accident "overcorrection" phase: while human operators exhibited immediate, defensive risk overestimation, the model maintained a stable trajectory based on accumulated experience. This strategic divergence confirms that operational instability following failure is often attributable to acute psychological bias overriding stable memory-based adaptation, a pattern theoretically expected to recur across analogous high-stakes environments and validatable through multi-modal behavioral and sensor data from professional operators.

</details>

### [13] [Domain-Conditioned Safety in Frontier Computer-Using Agents: A 793-Episode Browser Benchmark, a Coding-Domain Cross-Reference, and a Reproducibility Audit of Recent Red-Teaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05233)
*Nicholas Saban*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 当前前沿CUA模型（Claude Sonnet 4.6和GPT-5.4）对手工制作的提示注入攻击具有高度抵抗力（成功率为0/140），但该安全性具有领域特异性：在浏览器任务中有效，在代码代理任务中失败（成功率高达100%）。文献中高攻击成功率主要来自RL优化的注入文本，而非攻击类别本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前CUA红队论文报告了42-98%的提示注入攻击成功率，但这些数据集中在已退役模型或每个论文面板中最脆弱的模型上。作者质疑这些手工模板技术是否仍对当前前沿CUA有效，并试图评估真实安全性。

Method: 发布了CUA-HandCrafted基准，包含793个episode、24个多步骤Web任务、56个攻击模板、8个攻击家族和4个系统提示配置。在Claude Sonnet 4.6和GPT-5.4上测试，并使用消融实验验证安全性的来源。

Result: 在140个多步攻击中成功率为0（Clopper-Pearson 95%上界2.60%）；消融实验表明安全性来自模型权重而非系统提示；但在代码代理任务上，相同权重对手工技能注入攻击的成功率达100%。

Conclusion: 文献中高ASR主要归因于RL优化的注入文本，而非攻击类别本身；前沿安全加固是领域条件的，特定于浏览器表面；在不发布优化字符串的情况下报告技术或跨模态泛化，会使已发表的ASR数字无法复现。

Abstract: Recent computer-using-agent (CUA) red-teaming papers report prompt-injection attack success rates (ASR) of 42-98%, but these headline numbers cluster on retired models and on the most-vulnerable model in each paper's panel. We ask whether those techniques, reproduced as hand-crafted templates, still work against current frontier CUAs. We release CUA-HandCrafted, a public benchmark of 793 episodes spanning 24 multi-step web tasks, 56 attack templates, 8 attack families, and 4 system-prompt configurations. Against Claude Sonnet 4.6 and GPT-5.4 we measure 0/140 multi-step attack success (Clopper-Pearson 95% upper bound 2.60%); a prompt ablation shows this resistance lives in the model weights. Yet it does not generalize: on a sister coding-agent benchmark (SkillBench), the same weights fall to hand-crafted skill-injection at up to 100%. We argue that the literature's high ASR is largely attributable to RL-optimized injection text rather than the attack categories, and that frontier safety hardening is domain-conditioned, specific to the heavily-targeted browser surface. Reporting techniques without releasing the optimized strings, or extrapolating browser-domain safety to other CUA modalities, makes published ASR numbers unreproducible.

</details>


### [14] [Search-Time Contamination in Deep Research Agents: Measuring Performance Inflation in Public Benchmark Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05241)
*Yongjie Wang,Xinyue Zhang,Kunhong Yao,Zhiwei Zeng,Kaisong Song,Jun Lin,Zhiqi Shen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了深度研究型Agent在推理时通过网页搜索泄露基准测试数据导致的搜索时污染问题，定义了三种污染类型并开发检测算法，发现该污染可使性能虚增高达4%，并提出了防范建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公开基准测试在评估具备联网搜索能力的深度研究型Agent时，可能因Agent主动检索而泄露基准元数据、问题上下文甚至标准答案，导致评测结果失真。目前缺乏对这一问题的系统研究和量化分析。

Method: 定义了三种逐级严重的搜索时污染类型：基准元数据泄露、问题上下文泄露和显式答案泄露；开发了相应的检测算法来识别污染并量化其对Agent性能的影响；在六个公开基准上评估了现代深度研究型Agent。

Result: 搜索时污染普遍存在，可使Agent性能虚增高达4%。现有评测可能高估了Agent的真实推理能力。

Conclusion: 应采纳防污染实践，包括隔离沙箱、透明的搜索轨迹和受控的基准访问。

Abstract: Public benchmarks enable fair and reproducible evaluation of LLM reasoning, but they become fragile for deep research agents that actively search the web during inference. Such agents may retrieve public benchmark metadata, question context, or even ground-truth answers via web search. This gives rise to Search-Time Contamination (STC), where external retrieval bypasses intended reasoning and inflates measured performance. We systematically study STC in deep research agent evaluation. We define three contamination types with increasing severity, namely Benchmark Metadata Leakage, Question-Context Leakage, and Explicit Answer Leakage, and develop detection algorithms to identify them and quantify their impact on agent performance. Evaluating modern deep research agents on six public benchmarks, we find that STC is widespread and can inflate performance by up to 4%. Our findings show that existing evaluations may overestimate true reasoning ability. We therefore advocate contamination-aware practices, including isolated sandboxes, transparent search trajectories, and controlled benchmark access.

</details>


### [15] [Policy-Compliant Cloud Storage Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05423)
*Dimitrios Stavrakakis,Masanori Misono,Julian Pritzi,Harshavardhan Unnibhavi,Nuno Santos,Pramod Bhatotia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GDPRuler是一个可信中间件系统，通过在可信机密虚拟机中部署GDPR监控器，为未修改的键值存储系统提供可验证的GDPR合规性，在保持较低性能开销的同时实现策略执行、元数据管理和审计日志功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GDPR等隐私法规对个人数据的存储、处理和审计提出了严格要求，而键值存储(KVS)因其数据模型简单和部署在不可信云环境中的特点，难以满足合规要求。现有方法要么需要侵入式代码修改，要么性能开销过高，要么忽略了合规机制本身的完整性。

Method: GDPRuler在机密虚拟机(CVM)内部部署可信GDPR监控器，通过声明式策略语言将GDPR义务转化为可执行的运行时规则；将元数据紧凑编码到KV记录中，构建专用元数据索引加速GDPR相关查询，仅记录合规相关事件以节省日志空间；实现为未修改Redis和RocksDB的透明代理。

Result: GDPRuler达到约61%的原生KVS吞吐量，其中CVM环境贡献28%-32%的开销；元数据存储开销低于20%；通过元数据索引实现13-182倍的GDPR查询加速。

Conclusion: 通过将可验证的策略嵌入到可信中间件层，GDPRuler为不可信云基础设施上的KVS实现GDPR合规提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: Privacy regulations such as the General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR) impose strict requirements on how personal data is stored, processed, and audited. While key-value stores (KVS) are widely used in latency-sensitive applications, their simple data model and untrusted cloud deployment environments make GDPR compliance particularly challenging. Existing approaches require invasive code modifications, impose high performance overheads, or overlook the integrity of compliance mechanisms themselves.
  This paper presents GDPRuler, a trusted middleware system that enables verifiable GDPR compliance for KVS on untrusted clouds without modifying their codebase. GDPRuler deploys a trusted GDPR monitor inside a Confidential Virtual Machine (CVM), which enforces GDPR policies, manages compliance metadata, and maintains tamper-evident audit logs. A declarative policy language translates core GDPR obligations into enforceable runtime rules. To ensure efficiency, GDPRuler encodes metadata compactly within KV records, builds dedicated metadata indexes for GDPR-specific queries, and logs only compliance-relevant events in a space-efficient format. We implement GDPRuler as a transparent proxy for unmodified Redis and RocksDB deployments. Evaluation with YCSB and GDPR-inspired workloads shows that GDPRuler enforces core compliance guarantees with low overheads: GDPRuler achieves ~61% of native KVS throughput with the CVM environment contributing 28%-32% of it, metadata storage overhead remains below 20%, and GDPR queries benefit from 13-182x speedup through metadata indexing. By embedding verifiable policy enforcement into a trusted middleware layer, GDPRuler offers a practical path toward GDPR-compliant KVS on untrusted cloud infrastructures.

</details>


### [16] [From Attack Simulation to SIEM Rule: Deterministic Detection-as-Code Synthesis with Probe-Level Traceability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05252)
*Alexandre Cristovão Maiorano*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种确定性合成方法，通过锁定攻击语料库和模板库（N=23），将安全演练中的BAS发现自动转化为可部署的Sigma检测规则，实现从发现到规则的字节级可复现路径。在LLM和Web两个锁定语料库上，所有发现均生成可解析规则，LLM规则在OpenSearch SIEM上对AdvBench和HarmBench子集分别达到30%和14%检出率，误报率7.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前安全团队使用BAS工具进行攻击模拟后，检测结果到SIEM检测规则（Sigma格式）的转化仍需人工手动完成，效率低下且易出错，迫切需要自动化方案。

Method: 基于锁定语料库（每个发现具有稳定标识符），设计确定性合成函数：通过小型模板库（N=23，按OWASP LLM和Web Top 10分类索引），将每个发现映射为起始Sigma规则，并包含指向原始发现和MITRE ATT&CK技术的反向引用。

Result: 在17探针LLM和23探针Web两个锁定语料库上，所有绕过探测的发现均生成起始规则；全部17/17规则可解析并转换为Splunk和Elasticsearch后端格式；在OpenSearch SIEM上回放测试，LLM规则对AdvBench子集检出率30%、HarmBench 14%，误报率7.7%。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了从BAS发现到可部署起始规则的可验证、字节级稳定的路径，仅需公开语料库和模板库即可重新推导；以精确可复现性和类型化追溯能力替代了LLM生成方法的广度优势。

Abstract: Security teams routinely simulate attacks against their own systems to check whether their monitoring would catch a real intruder. These Breach-and-Attack-Simulation (BAS) tools surface findings, but the security information and event management (SIEM) systems that watch production need detection rules -- and today a human bridges that gap by hand, reading each finding and writing the corresponding Sigma rule (a vendor-neutral detection format). We show this translation can be partially automated when probes are drawn from a locked corpus, so each finding carries a stable identifier back to the originating probe. We describe a deterministic synthesis function that maps each finding to a starter Sigma rule through a small template library (N=23, indexed by categories from the OWASP LLM and Web Top 10), with a back-reference to the originating finding and its MITRE ATT&CK technique. On two locked corpora (17-probe LLM, 23-probe Web), every bypassed-probe finding yields a starter rule, and all 17/17 emitted rules parse and convert to Splunk and Elasticsearch backends. Replayed through a live OpenSearch SIEM, the LLM rules fire on 30% of a held-out AdvBench subset and 14% of HarmBench at 7.7% false positives on a benign baseline; the Web side is validated structurally, not against a held-out attack set. The contribution is a verifiable, byte-stable path from BAS finding to operator-deployable starter rule, re-derivable from the published corpus and template library alone -- trading the breadth of LLM-generative methods for exact reproducibility and a typed traceback from any fired alert to the originating probe.

</details>


### [17] [Willing but Unable: Separating Refusal from Capability in Code LLMs via Abliteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05396)
*Cristina Carleo,Pietro Liguori,Naghmeh Ivaki,Domenico Cotroneo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究探索了abliteration（低秩权重编辑，正交投影去除拒绝方向）作为一种消除安全对齐代码LLM拒绝生成漏洞代码障碍的方法。以Python和CWE-89（SQL注入）为案例，评估Qwen2.5-Coder-Instruct系列（3B、7B、14B），发现：(i)拒绝率与模型规模和提示上下文强相关；(ii)abliteration几乎完全消除拒绝，同时保持93%以上的语法有效性；(iii)注入率受限于模型能力（14B达88-97%，7B达89-90%，3B仅25-48%），表明abliteration解锁的是意愿而非能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的漏洞检测面临标注噪声问题，现有方法依赖有缺陷种子转换，无法从规范合成漏洞。若从安全代码出发，用指令微调LLM注入指定CWE可转移标注负担，但安全对齐的代码LLM系统性地拒绝此类提示。需要一种方法移除这一障碍。

Method: 采用abliteration技术——一种低秩权重编辑，正交投影去除残差流中的拒绝方向。以Python和CWE-89（SQL注入）为案例，使用Qwen2.5-Coder-Instruct系列模型（3B、7B、14B），在PromSec和SafeCoder数据集的安全样本上进行评估，每个条件重复三次。漏洞判定采用三个工具（CodeQL、Semgrep、Bandit）组成的检测器集成，随后由两位作者对检测器阳性输出进行人工裁决。

Result: (i)14B模型100%拒绝注入提示；7B对PromSec拒绝率为73%，对SafeCoder仅5%；3B几乎从不拒绝。(ii)abliteration将拒绝率降至零或接近零，语法有效性保持在93%以上。(iii)注入率受模型能力限制：14B为88-97%，7B为89-90%，3B仅为25-48%。

Conclusion: abliteration可以有效解除安全对齐代码LLM的拒绝机制，且不损害代码生成能力。但模型成功注入漏洞的能力仍受参数规模限制，表明意愿（abliteration解锁）与能力（随参数规模扩展）是可分离的。该工作为从安全代码出发合成漏洞数据集提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Producing a labeled vulnerable code at scale is a recurring obstacle for learning-based vulnerability detection: mined corpora carry substantial label noise, and existing LLM-based augmentation propagates these inaccuracies because it transforms vulnerable seeds rather than synthesising vulnerabilities from a specification. A complementary route is to start from safe code and ask an instruction-tuned LLM to inject a specified CWE (which would shift the labeling burden from open-ended detection to bounded binary confirmation) but safety-aligned code LLMs systematically refuse such prompts. This paper is a preliminary feasibility study of abliteration, a low-rank weight edit that orthogonally projects out the refusal direction in the residual stream, as a tool to remove this barrier. We use Python and CWE-89 (SQL injection) as a case study, evaluating the Qwen2.5-Coder-Instruct family at 3B, 7B, and 14B parameters on safe samples drawn from PromSec and SafeCoder, replicated three times per condition. We find that (i) refusal on injection prompts is strongly size- and prompt-context-dependent: the 14B refuses 100% of prompts, the 7B refuses 73% of PromSec but only 5% of SafeCoder, whereas the 3B is essentially never blocked; (ii) abliteration reduces refusal to zero or near-zero across all sizes while leaving syntactic validity above 93%, supporting the view that, in this setting, refusal can be detached from measured code-generation capability; and (iii) the post-abliteration injection rate remains capacity-bound (88-97% on the 14B, 89-90% on the 7B, and 25-48% on the 3B) separating willingness, which abliteration unlocks, from capability, which scales with parameters. Vulnerability verdicts are produced by a three-tool detector ensemble (CodeQL, Semgrep, Bandit) followed by manual adjudication by two authors on detector-positive outputs.

</details>


### [18] [A formal framework for the economic security of DeFi compositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05418)
*Massimo Bartoletti,Riccado Marchesin,Roberto Zunino*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MEV非干扰性概念，一种针对DeFi可组合性的形式化安全属性，通过定义局部MEV度量、研究有界/无界财富两种攻击模型，建立模块化推理安全可组合性的充分条件和局部性原则，并应用于多种DeFi组合（交易所、AMM、期权、借贷池、路由器和套利合约）以区分安全与脆弱组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeFi服务通常通过组合多种智能合约构建，虽然可组合性是DeFi成功的关键驱动因素，但也带来了安全风险：攻击者可能利用新部署合约与已有合约之间的交互造成经济损失。现有缺乏形式化框架来推理DeFi组合的经济安全性。

Method: 提出MEV非干扰性（MEV non-interference）形式化安全概念，要求新部署合约集合的最大可提取价值不因与现有区块链状态的交互而增加；定义局部MEV（local MEV）作为关注特定受害者合约集合经济损失的新度量；研究有界财富和无界财富两种对抗模型；建立充分条件和局部性原则以实现模块化推理。

Result: 将框架应用于代表性DeFi组合（交易所、AMM、期权、借贷池、路由器和套利合约），成功区分了安全组合与脆弱组合。

Conclusion: 为推理DeFi组合的经济安全性提供了形式化基础。

Abstract: Decentralized Finance (DeFi) services are usually constructed by composing a variety of smart contracts. While composability is a key driver of the success of DeFi, it also creates security risks: adversaries may exploit interactions between newly deployed contracts and the pre-existing ones to inflict economic losses. We introduce MEV non-interference, a formal security notion for DeFi composability requiring that the maximal extractable value from a set of newly deployed contracts is not increased by interactions with the existing blockchain state. To support this notion, we define local MEV, a novel measure of economic attacks that focusses on the loss of a given set of victim contracts. We study two adversarial models, with bounded and unbounded wealth, and establish sufficient conditions and locality principles that enable modular reasoning about secure composability. We apply the framework to representative DeFi compositions, including exchanges, AMMs, options, lending pools, routers, and arbitrage contracts, showing how it distinguishes secure compositions from vulnerable ones. Our results provide a formal foundation for reasoning about the economic security of DeFi compositions.

</details>


### [19] [CRESS: Quantifying Vulnerabilities of Attack Scenarios in Hardware Reverse Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05459)
*Alexander Hepp,Matthias Ludwig,Michaela Brunner,Johanna Baehr,Georg Sigl*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了CRESS（通用逆向工程评分系统）的定量扩展，通过专家访谈推导出权重，建立量化方程来计算硬件逆向工程攻击场景的严重性评分，相比行业标准CVSS更具表现力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微电子系统的安全性依赖于可信的供应链和设计流程，但全球分布的供应链或设计漏洞为硬件层面的攻击（如伪造、硬件木马、设备攻击）提供了可乘之机。硬件逆向工程（RE）在这些攻击中扮演关键角色，但缺乏一个通用的、一致的评分系统来分类和评估RE相关场景的严重性。

Method: 通过专家访谈研究获取领域专家意见，从访谈结果中推导出不同RE攻击类别的权重，基于这些权重构建量化方程，将原本定性的CRESS系统扩展为定量系统，生成可衡量严重性的CRESS评分。

Result: 形成了量化的CRESS评分体系，并通过六个案例研究的定性与定量评估进行了验证。结果表明CRESS评分比行业标准的通用漏洞评分系统（CVSS）更具表达力。

Conclusion: 量化的CRESS评分能够对新型RE攻击场景进行一致性评级，使不同场景可比，支持开发有效的防御措施，在表达力和适用性上优于CVSS。

Abstract: The safety, security, and reliability of microelectronic systems depend on a trustworthy, secured supply chain and design flow. Globally distributed supply chains or unintentional design weaknesses leave the door open for attacks on the hardware level. These scenarios encompass counterfeiting, hardware trojans, or on-device attacks. For these, hardware reverse engineering (RE) results play a pivotal role. The ongoing publication of new RE-involved attacks motivated the development of the common RE scoring system (CRESS). The system enables a general classification of RE-involved scenarios for a common, consistent rating. In this work, the originally qualitative system is extended to a quantitative system. We performed an extensive interview study with experts in the field. The interview results allowed us to derive weights that measure the severity of different RE-involved attack categories. The weights form an equation that quantifies scenarios, resulting in the severity-indicating CRESS score. The score enables the coherent rating of novel scenarios, renders them comparable, and supports the development of effective countermeasures. To showcase the effectiveness of the quantitative CRESS Score, six selected case studies are rated qualitatively and quantitatively. The CRESS Score proves to be significantly more expressive than the industry-standard Common Vulnerability Scoring System (CVSS).

</details>


### [20] [SHIELDS: Automating OS Hardening with Iterative Multi-Agent Remediation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05476)
*Andrew Hamara,Dwight Horne,Aldehir Rojas,Timothy Kurniawan,Sophie Lamothe,Vishal Suresh,Nicholas Turoci,Lawrence Wong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SHIELDS是一个基于多智能体和大语言模型的系统，通过迭代反馈驱动的方式自动化修复操作系统安全配置偏差，成功修复率高达73%，且发现模型效果更依赖于工具使用和信息收集能力而非参数规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全配置错误是操作系统被攻破的主要原因，手动维护系统符合DISA STIG等安全标准繁琐且昂贵，现有自动化工具依赖静态预写的修正措施，缺乏动态适应性。

Method: 提出SHIELDS多智能体系统，利用大语言模型将OS加固视为迭代、反馈驱动的过程，持续提出修复方案并根据目标系统执行反馈和验证扫描结果进行优化。

Result: 在多个虚拟机配置上使用6种当代LLM（20B-400B参数）评估，SHIELDS成功修复高达73%的扫描发现项。

Conclusion: 该场景下成功修复的关键更依赖于有效的工具使用和信息收集，而非模型参数规模，为计算资源受限或安全隐私需求驱动的本地模型使用提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Security misconfigurations remain a leading cause of OS-level compromise, and manually keeping systems compliant with standards like Defense Information Systems Agency (DISA) Security Technical Implementation Guides (STIGs) is a tedious and expensive process. Existing compliance automation tools can reduce some of this burden, but they depend on static, pre-written corrective actions. In this paper, we introduce SHIELDS, a multi-agent system that uses large language models (LLMs) to approach OS hardening as an iterative, feedback-driven process. Instead of applying fixed remediations, SHIELDS continuously proposes fixes and refines them based on feedback from target system execution and validation scans. We evaluate the system across multiple virtual machine configurations using six contemporary LLMs ranging from 20B to 400B parameters, and find that SHIELDS successfully remediates up to 73% of scan findings. Our results also suggest that success in this setting depends less on model size (parameter count) than on effective tool use and information gathering, paving a practical path toward reducing the burden of security compliance in environments where compute is limited or security and privacy needs drive local model use.

</details>


### [21] [Bitcoin After Block Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05503)
*Junhyuk Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了比特币区块奖励归零后，矿工仅依赖交易手续费时网络的安全性。通过分析诚实与偏离矿工的收益，识别出诚实挖矿不再最优的偏离阈值 $G_t$，并评估了三种协议级机制（基础费、手续费下限、自适应最大区块大小规则）的组合效应，证明其能提高偏离阈值并缓解手续费模式下激励崩溃的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比特币区块奖励将逐步降至零，届时矿工仅靠交易手续费维持，这引发了对网络安全性的担忧。本文旨在识别大规模、持续性偏离诚实挖矿行为的条件。

Method: 采用序贯决策模型分析并比较诚实矿工与偏离矿工的收益，识别出诚实挖矿不再具有私人最优性的偏离阈值 $G_t$。结合2024年比特币减半事件验证当前行为，并在区块奖励归零后进行阈值分析。进一步评估基础费、手续费下限和自适应最大区块大小规则三种协议级机制的效果。

Result: 2024年减半附近，当前挖矿行为未呈现大规模或结构性偏离。但当区块奖励移除后，即使交易手续费占比极小，$G_t$ 准则也显示偏离可能发生。三种机制的组合能有效提高偏离阈值，缓解手续费模式下激励崩溃问题。

Conclusion: 该研究为评估比特币在区块奖励消失后的安全性提供了实用的基准，并指出协议级机制的组合设计是维持网络安全的关键。

Abstract: Bitcoin's block reward is scheduled to decline to zero, raising concerns about whether the network can remain secure once miners rely solely on transaction fees. This paper seeks to identify the conditions under which large-scale and persistent deviation from honest mining can arise.
  We analyze and compare the payoffs of honest and deviating miners in a sequential decision model, and identify a deviation threshold $G_t$ at which honest mining ceases to be privately optimal. Around the 2024 Bitcoin halving, we show that current mining behavior does not exhibit large-scale or structural deviation. However, when the block reward is removed, the $G_t$ criterion implies that deviation can arise even with a very small fraction of transaction fees.
  Finally, we evaluate three protocol-level mechanisms: Base Fee, Fee Floor, and an adaptive maximum block size rule, and show that their combination raises the deviation threshold and mitigates incentive breakdown in a fee-only regime. These results provide a practical benchmark for assessing Bitcoin's security as block rewards disappear.

</details>


### [22] [ZERO-APT: A Closed-Loop Adversarial Framework for LLM-Driven Automated Penetration Testing under Intelligent Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05567)
*Anlan Zheng,Tiantian Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ZERO-APT提出一个攻击者-防御者-裁判三位一体的回合制框架，通过在架构层面注入可配置的LLM防御者、因果一致性机制和专用裁判智能体，同时解决了自动化渗透测试智能体在 realism（真实感）、consistency（一致性）和 auditability（可审计性）三大缺陷。实验表明，在Windows Server 2022后渗透场景下，攻击成功率79%，因果一致性得分0.860，并生成结构化CTI报告实现全链路决策可追溯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的自动化渗透测试智能体通常在静态目标上评估，目标既不检测也不响应攻击，导致其对智能防御的行为未被测试。此外，多步攻击链的因果一致性依赖于不稳定的LLM推理，智能体决策对人工分析师不透明。现有工作通常孤立地修补这些不足，缺乏统一解决方案。

Method: ZERO-APT采用回合制 attacker-defender-judge 架构：(1) 真实性：嵌入可配置的LLM Defender，消费Sysmon遥测数据实时检测攻击；(2) 一致性：通过规划与执行分离、多维ReAct反馈、硬约束过滤的动作库三种架构机制，将因果一致性从不稳定的LLM推理迁移到强制系统架构中；(3) 可审计性：专用Judge智能体裁决每轮行动、维护全局状态并生成结构化CTI报告。

Result: 在Windows Server 2022后渗透原型上，跨越5个场景和3种防御者配置进行实验。ZERO-APT达到79%攻击成功率（Aurora 22%，PentestGPT 39%），因果一致性得分0.860（Aurora 0.930，Claude Code 0.520），并通过结构化CTI报告实现端到端决策可审计。

Conclusion: ZERO-APT通过统一架构同时解决了自动化渗透测试中的真实性、一致性和可审计性问题，显著优于现有基线。作者发布了基准以支持在智能防御下评估渗透智能体。

Abstract: LLM-driven automated penetration testing agents are typically evaluated against static targets that neither detect nor respond to attacks, so their behavior under intelligent defense remains untested. The causal consistency of multi-step attack chains likewise hinges on unstable LLM reasoning, and agent decisions remain opaque to human analysts. These three shortcomings, in realism, consistency, and auditability, are usually patched in isolation. We present ZERO-APT, a turn-based attacker-defender-judge framework that addresses them within a single architecture. For realism, ZERO-APT embeds a configurable LLM Defender that consumes Sysmon telemetry and detects attacks in real time, exposing the attacker to a live opponent rather than a passive target. For consistency, three architectural mechanisms move causal consistency from unstable LLM reasoning into enforced system architecture: separation of planning from execution, multi-dimensional ReAct feedback, and a hard-constraint-filtered action library. For auditability, a dedicated Judge agent adjudicates each round, maintains global state, and emits structured post-hoc CTI reports that make every decision traceable. We evaluate a Windows Server 2022 post-exploitation prototype across five scenarios with three Defender configurations. ZERO-APT reaches 79\% attack success rate (Aurora 22\%, PentestGPT 39\%), a Causal Consistency Score of 0.860 (Aurora 0.930, Claude Code 0.520), and end-to-end decision auditability through structured CTI reports. We release the benchmark to support evaluation of penetration agents under intelligent defense.

</details>


### [23] [Dimensionality Reduction for Cyberattack Classification: A Comparative Evaluation of PCA and Linear Predictive Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05584)
*Nelly Elsayed,Zag ElSayed,Navid Asadizanjani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了两种特征压缩技术（PCA和LPC）在网络攻击检测中的应用，实验表明PCA在强压缩下仍能保持分类性能，LPC性能略有下降，但两者均可大幅降低特征维度且对分类精度影响较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维特征表示在基于机器学习的网络攻击检测系统中被广泛使用，但其增加了计算复杂度，阻碍了在资源受限环境中的部署，因此需要探索特征压缩技术。

Method: 对比了主成分分析（PCA）和线性预测编码（LPC）两种降维方法，生成不同维度的压缩特征表示，并在多种分类模型上进行评估。

Result: PCA在强压缩下仍能保持分类性能；LPC提供了有竞争力的预测表示但性能下降略大；大幅降低特征维度对分类精度影响很小。

Conclusion: 轻量级特征压缩在高效网络安全分析中具有潜力，可以在资源受限环境下实现有效的网络攻击检测。

Abstract: High-dimensional feature representations are widely used in machine learning-based cyberattack detection systems. However, they increase computational complexity and may hinder deployment in resource-constrained environments. In this paper, we investigate feature compression techniques for cyberattack classification by comparing two dimensionality reduction approaches: Principal Component Analysis (PCA) and Linear Predictive Coding (LPC). Compressed feature representations with varying dimensionalities are generated and evaluated across several classification models. Experimental analysis demonstrates that PCA preserves classification performance even under aggressive compression. On the other hand, LPC provides competitive predictive representations with slightly larger performance degradation. The results show that substantial reductions in feature dimensionality can be achieved with minimal impact on classification accuracy, highlighting the potential of lightweight feature compression for efficient cybersecurity analytics.

</details>


### [24] [The Coverage Gap: Chile's Cyber Disclosure Framework versus the USA, EU and UK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05594)
*David Mellafe Z*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"覆盖缺口"（Coverage Gap）概念，用于量化关键基础设施运营商在漏洞披露协调能力方面的可观测差距。通过对智利915家"重要运营商"（OIV）进行全量普查，发现仅1.7%发布可验证的RFC 9116披露渠道，84%存在电子邮件认证配置错误，约23.5%使用已知漏洞组件。与美国联邦文职部门99%的合规率形成鲜明对比。智利在电子邮件认证强制要求方面落后美英荷约8年、落后丹麦约3年。文章提出四阶段路线图并开源分析工具anci-oiv-resolver。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 衡量关键基础设施运营商漏洞披露协调能力的实际可见差距，填补现有研究对披露能力可观测性与合规性之间系统性差异的量化空白。

Method: 基于OSINT的被动方法，遵循ISO/IEC 29147:2018标准和智利计算机犯罪安全港法律（Ley 21.459），对915家OIV的全量官方目录进行约98.7%覆盖率的基础披露能力层（Layer 1，可验证的披露联系渠道）普查。通过RFC 9116、DMARC/SPF/DKIM等电子邮件认证配置和已知漏洞组件检测进行多维度测量。开发开源工具anci-oiv-resolver实现独立复现。

Result: 915家OIV中仅16家（1.7%）发布可验证的RFC 9116披露渠道；物理世界基础设施运营商（能源、医疗、银行、电信等）中不足10家符合要求；四大银行和两大电信运营商均无披露渠道。84%的OIV存在电子邮件认证配置错误；约23.5%使用生命周期结束或已知漏洞组件（Wilson 95% CI [12%, 38%]）。智利在电子邮件认证强制方面落后美国、英国、荷兰约8年，落后丹麦约3年。

Conclusion: 智利关键基础设施运营商在漏洞披露协调能力方面存在严重覆盖缺口，与美国等国家的合规水平差距显著。提出参照CISA BOD 18-01和英国公共部门DMARC工具包的四阶段路线图作为改进路径。开源工具可支持此类全量审计的独立复现和持续监测。

Abstract: We introduce the Coverage Gap as a measurable distance between the observable public exposure of critical-infrastructure operators and their declared capability to coordinate vulnerability disclosure. We instantiate it against the 915 Chilean Operadores de Importancia Vital (OIVs -- Operators of Vital Importance) designated by the National Cybersecurity Agency (ANCI) under Ley 21.663 (Resolucion Exenta No. 87, 16 December 2025). Using a passive-only, OSINT-based method consistent with the principles of ISO/IEC 29147:2018 and Chile's computer-crimes safe harbour (Ley 21.459), we conduct a full-universe census of the foundational disclosure-capability layer (Layer 1, verifiable disclosure contact) across approximately 98.7% of the official catalogue. Only 16 of 915 OIVs (1.7%) publish a verifiable RFC 9116 disclosure channel; among operators of physical-world infrastructure -- energy, health, banking, telecommunications, fuel, water, transport, and state administration -- fewer than ten do so, and all four major banks and both telecommunications incumbents lack one entirely. This compares with over 99% adherence in the U.S. federal civilian branch under CISA Binding Operational Directive 18-01. Email-authentication misconfiguration affects 766 of 915 (84%) OIVs, and end-of-life or known-vulnerable stack components an estimated 23.5% (Wilson 95% CI [12%, 38%]). Cross-jurisdictional benchmarking situates Chile roughly eight years behind the USA, the UK, and the Netherlands on email-authentication mandates, and three years behind Denmark. We propose a four-stage roadmap modelled on BOD 18-01 and the UK Public-Sector DMARC Toolkit, and release the open-source tool anci-oiv-resolver (Apache 2.0) to enable independent reproduction of the OIV-domain mapping that underpins universe-scale auditing.

</details>


### [25] [SlotGCG: Exploiting the Positional Vulnerability in LLMs for Jailbreak Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05609)
*Seungwon Jeong,Jiwoo Jeong,Hyeonjin Kim,Yunseok Lee,Woojin Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SlotGCG方法，通过量化提示词中不同插入位置（slots）的脆弱性分数（VSS），选择最脆弱的槽位进行对抗性令牌插入，相比固定后缀攻击（GCG）在多个模型上实现了14%-42%更高的攻击成功率，且仅增加200ms预处理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化型越狱攻击（如GCG）将对抗性令牌固定在提示词末尾插入，忽视了其他位置插入令牌的效果。本文旨在探究不同插入位置对越狱攻击成功率的影响，并利用这一发现提升攻击效果。

Method: 提出脆弱槽位分数（VSS）量化不同插入位置的越狱脆弱性；基于VSS设计SlotGCG方法，先评估所有候选槽位的VSS，选择最脆弱的槽位插入令牌，再运行针对性优化攻击；该方法与攻击算法无关，可作为插件集成到任何优化型攻击中。

Result: 在多个模型上，SlotGCG相比GCG基线攻击成功率提升14%；收敛速度更快；对抗防御方法时攻击成功率比基线高42%；预处理开销仅增加200ms。

Conclusion: 提示词中不同位置的越狱脆弱性存在显著差异，通过VSS量化并选择最脆弱位置进行攻击可大幅提升越狱效果，SlotGCG是一种高效、通用且轻量的位置搜索机制。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are widely deployed, identifying their vulnerability through jailbreak attacks becomes increasingly critical. Optimization-based attacks like Greedy Coordinate Gradient (GCG) have focused on inserting adversarial tokens to the end of prompts. However, GCG restricts adversarial tokens to a fixed insertion point (typically the prompt suffix), leaving the effect of inserting tokens at other positions unexplored. In this paper, we empirically investigate \emph{slots}, i.e., candidate positions within a prompt where tokens can be inserted. We find that vulnerability to jailbreaking is highly related to the selection of the \emph{slots}. Based on these findings, we introduce the \textit{Vulnerable Slot Score} (VSS) to quantify the positional vulnerability to jailbreaking. We then propose SlotGCG, which evaluates all slots with VSS, selects the most vulnerable slots for insertion, and runs a targeted optimization attack at those slots. Our approach provides a position-search mechanism that is attack-agnostic and can be plugged into any optimization-based attack, adding only 200ms of preprocessing time. Experiments across multiple models demonstrate that SlotGCG significantly outperforms existing methods. Specifically, it achieves 14\% higher Attack Success Rates (ASR) over GCG-based attacks, converges faster, and shows superior robustness against defense methods with 42\% higher ASR than baseline approaches. Our implementation is available at \href{https://github.com/youai058/SlotGCG}{https://github.com/youai058/SlotGCG}

</details>


### [26] [Protecting K-Nearest Neighbor Queries from Location Inference Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05648)
*Zhiyu Sun,Jie Fu,Xinpeng Ling,Huifa Li,Zhili Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次揭示k近邻查询(kNNQ)在LBS中的位置隐私风险，提出两种位置推断攻击(GI-LIA和ZO-LIA)，并设计了基于差分隐私的DPRS框架，通过带约束扰动区间的拒绝采样机制实现隐私保护与查询效用的更好权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: kNNQ在LBS中广泛应用（如"附近的人"），但其潜在的位置隐私风险长期被忽视，现有工作缺乏对此类攻击的系统性研究。

Method: 提出两种攻击方法：几何交点位置推断攻击(GI-LIA)和零阶优化位置推断攻击(ZO-LIA)；提出DPRS差分隐私框架，核心是带约束扰动区间的拒绝采样机制，并设计了私有区间构建算法以优化隐私-效用权衡。

Result: 在真实空间数据集上的实验表明，DPRS在隐私保护和查询效用方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: kNNQ存在固有的位置隐私风险，但通过DPRS框架可有效缓解；代码已开源。

Abstract: The k-nearest neighbor query (kNNQ) is a core component of modern location-based services (LBS) and has been widely adopted in popular features such as ``people nearby''. However, its potential privacy risks have long been overlooked. In this work, we present the first two attacks against kNNQ, namely the geometric intersection location inference attack (GI-LIA) and the zero-order optimization location inference attack (ZO-LIA), revealing the inherent location privacy risks posed by kNNQ. To mitigate these privacy risks, we further propose DPRS, a differential privacy framework for kNNQ protection. The core idea of DPRS is to incorporate a rejection sampling mechanism within a constrained perturbation interval, thereby mitigating the distance distortion caused by excessive noise injection. In addition, we design a private interval construction algorithm to construct the perturbation interval, enabling the rejection sampling mechanism to achieve a more favorable trade-off between privacy protection and query utility in kNNQ. Extensive experiments on real-world spatial datasets demonstrate that DPRS outperforms existing methods in both privacy protection and query utility. Our code is available at https://github.com/reanatom/DPRS.

</details>

Result: 该分散式学习架构提升了隐私保护能力，降低了通信依赖和集中式安全风险，增强了智能威胁分析能力。

Conclusion: 该框架通过联邦学习与可解释AI的结合，有效解决了分布式系统中集中式入侵检测的可扩展性、数据隐私、通信开销和透明度问题。

Abstract: The increasing adoption of distributed infrastructure systems, cloud computing, Internet of Things (IoT) technologies, and edge-based architectures has significantly expanded the cybersecurity attack surface and introduced increasingly sophisticated cyber threats. Conventional centralized intrusion detection approaches often face challenges related to scalability, data privacy, communication overhead, and limited transparency in artificial intelligence-driven decision-making processes. To address these limitations, this study proposes a Cognitive Threat Intelligence and Explainable Federated Security Analytics framework for distributed infrastructure systems. The proposed framework integrates Federated Learning (FL), Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI), and cognitive cybersecurity analytics to enable collaborative and privacy-preserving cyber threat detection across distributed network environments. Instead of transmitting sensitive raw network traffic data to centralized servers, local security models are independently trained at distributed nodes, where only encrypted model parameters and updates are shared through a federated aggregation mechanism. This decentralized learning architecture improves privacy protection while reducing communication dependency and centralized security risks. To enhance intelligent threat analysis, the framework incorporates machine learning and deep learning algorithms including Random Forest, XGBoost, Autoencoder

</details>

*B. M. Taslimul Haque,Md. Arifur Rahman,Md. Serajul Kabir Chowdhury Rubel,Md. Iqbal Hossan*

Method: 基于CICIDS2017数据集，使用XGBoost、随机森林、决策树等机器学习分类器构建入侵检测和网络风险预测模型；集成可解释人工智能（XAI）技术增强决策透明度和可解释性；通过准确率、精确率、召回率、F1分数、ROC-AUC和假阳性率等指标评估模型可靠性和韧性。

Result: 提出了一种用于美国关键基础设施环境的韧性网络风险分析与模型可靠性评估框架，该框架能够有效检测DDoS、僵尸网络、勒索软件、APT等恶意活动，并通过多维度性能指标验证了模型的可靠性和韧性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过结合机器学习和XAI技术，为关键基础设施的智能治理和网络风险决策提供了一种透明、可信且可靠的支撑方案，有望提升关键基础设施在面对高级网络威胁时的安全韧性。

Abstract: The increasing penetrations of the critical infrastructure sector in the United States with intelligent digital technologies have greatly increased exposure to advanced cyber adversaries and operational vulnerabilities. AI-powered governance and automated decision-making systems are becoming a key part of the operation of critical infrastructure systems, including energy, healthcare, transportation, financial services, and communication infrastructure, in order to improve efficiency and strategic management. The growing cyber threat environment, such as Distributed Denial of Service (DDos) attacks, botnets, ransomware, and Advanced Persistent Threats (APTs) pose significant challenges to infrastructure resilience, cyber security reliability, and governance trustworthiness. In a changing attack landscape and dynamic network environment, traditional cybersecurity mechanisms can often fall short of meeting the evolving needs and protecting critical systems. This study will develop a resilient cyber risk analytics and model reliability assessment framework to support intelligent governance and decision support for cyber risk exposure in the U.S. critical infrastructure environment. This study is based on the CICIDS2017 dataset for the development and testing of intrusion detection system models and cyber risk prediction models based on machine learning. Various classifiers like XGBoost, Random Forest, and Decision Tree are used to detect malicious activities on the network and determine the level of cyber risk. Furthermore, the Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) techniques are integrated to enhance transparency, interpretability, and trust in cybersecurity decision-making processes. The proposed framework presents the reliability and resilience of the model by having various performance measures such as accuracy, precision, recall, F1 score, ROC-AUC, and false positive rate.

</details>

Abstract: Digital infrastructure is growing at a rapid pace in the United States, and as a result, exposure to advanced cyber threats to critical sectors including healthcare, finance, transportation, energy and government systems is growing. The traditional cybersecurity approaches, including signature-based intrusion detection systems, have become less effective against today's cyber attacks, as they are unable to detect unknown and changing attacks in real time. To overcome these constraints, this research suggests a smart cyber-defense system, which utilizes Artificial Intelligence (AI) and Machine Learning (ML) algorithms in the detection and prevention of cyber attacks in the U.S. digital infrastructure. This study uses the CSE-CIC-IDS2018 dataset, which is a realistic network traffic dataset, along with various cyber attack scenarios, including Distributed Denial of Service (DDoS), brute force attacks, botnets, infiltration attacks, and web-based attacks. A number of machine learning and deep learning models such as Random Forest, XGBoost, Convolutional Neural Networks (CNN) and Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks are implemented and evaluated to be used in identifying malicious network behavior and boosting the accuracy of intrusion detection. The framework proposed combines data preprocessing, feature engineering, real-time traffic monitoring, intelligent threat classification with automated prevention mechanisms to build cybersecurity resilience. ### [30] [An Embarrassingly Simple Detector for Model Extraction Attacks in Large Language Model API Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05725)
*Shuze Liu,Qianwen Guo,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于良性校准的流量窗口分布测试方法用于LLM模型提取检测，使用最大均值差异（MMD）在语义空间中比较查询分布与历史良性流量，在14个攻击场景中实现了0.3%误报率、100%纯攻击检测率和95.1%平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM通过托管API部署时，模型提取攻击威胁模型所有权和服务安全，而现有检测方法多聚焦于单查询异常评分或纯良性vs攻击者的简单设定，缺乏对混合多用户场景下模型提取监控的有效方案。

Method: 将模型提取监控形式化为良性校准的流量窗口分布测试：将输入查询嵌入语义空间，使用MMD检验其聚合分布是否偏离历史良性流量分布，仅通过良性vs良性比较设定决策阈值。

Result: 在14个攻击者-正常查询对（涵盖4种提取场景）上，与PRADA、SEAT、CAP、DATE、边际马氏距离等基线相比，MMD方法实现0.3%良性误报率、100.0%纯攻击者检测率、90.5%平均攻击者检测率、95.1%平衡准确率。

Conclusion: 良性校准的分布测试是LLM API流量中模型提取检测的强经验基线，在用户级和混合多用户场景下均有效。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed through hosted APIs, making model extraction a practical threat to model ownership and service security. However, individual extraction queries often resemble benign requests, and existing evaluations often focus on single-query anomaly scoring or pure benign-versus-attacker user settings. We formulate model extraction monitoring as benign-calibrated traffic-window distribution testing and show that an embarrassingly simple detector is effective: embed incoming queries into a semantic space and test whether their aggregate distribution deviates from historical benign traffic. We instantiate the detector with maximum mean discrepancy (MMD), using only benign-vs-benign comparisons to set the decision threshold. We evaluate on fourteen attacker-normal query pairs from four extraction scenarios and compare with adapted PRADA, SEAT, CAP, DATE, and marginal Mahalanobis baselines. Across three random seeds, MMD achieves 0.3% benign FPR, 100.0% pure-attacker TPR, 90.5% average TPR over attacker fractions, and 95.1% balanced accuracy. These results show that benign-calibrated distribution testing is a strong empirical baseline for model extraction detection in both user-level and mixed multi-user LLM API traffic. Code is released at: https://github.com/LabRAI/mmd-llm-mea-detection.

</details>


### [31] [Membrane: A Self-Evolving Contrastive Safety Memory for LLM Agent Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05743)
*Minseok Choi,Seungbin Yang,Dongjin Kim,Subin Kim,Jungmin Son,Yunseung Lee,Jaegul Choo,Youngjun Kwak*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Membrane，一种基于对比安全记忆(CSM)的自进化护栏，通过蒸馏有害交互及其良性对应物构建对比单元，在不重训练的情况下持续适应新的越狱攻击，在HarmBench和AgentHarm上取得最高F1分数，同时将良性拒绝率降至7-14%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全微调分类器无法适应不断演变的越狱攻击，而基于记忆的护栏容易过度拒绝与存储攻击表面相似的良性查询，缺乏精确区分有害与良性请求的能力。

Method: 构建对比安全记忆(CSM)，每个单元配对阻塞有害查询的条件与允许表面相似良性请求的条件；通过蒸馏每次有害交互及其良性对应物形成对比单元，按攻击策略索引以实现跨主题泛化；推理时检索相关单元作为决策的上下文基础。

Result: 在HarmBench和AgentHarm上，针对六种越狱攻击均取得最高F1分数；AgentHarm上良性拒绝率仅7-14%，远低于现有方法的28-85%；跨攻击迁移下记忆单元保持87-88% F1，且对记忆投毒攻击保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: Membrane通过对比记忆机制有效平衡了安全防护的精确性与泛化能力，在不重训练条件下持续适应新型攻击，同时显著降低良性查询的误拒率。

Abstract: Despite advances in safety alignment, large language models remain vulnerable to continuously evolving jailbreaks. Existing fine-tuned safety classifiers cannot adapt to these evolving attacks, while adaptive memory-based guardrails tend to over-refuse benign queries that resemble stored attacks. We propose Membrane, a self-evolving guardrail built on Contrastive Safety Memory (CSM): each cell pairs the conditions for blocking a harmful query with those for permitting a superficially similar benign request. Without retraining, Membrane evolves CSM by distilling each harmful interaction and its benign counterpart into a contrastive cell indexed by the underlying attack strategy, so that one cell generalizes across topical variants of the same mechanism. At inference, retrieved cells serve as grounding context for precise safety decisions. Across model-level safety on HarmBench and agent-level safety on AgentHarm, Membrane achieves the highest F1 on all six jailbreak attacks. Notably, benign refusal on AgentHarm stays at 7-14%, well below the 28-85% range of prior guards. Memory cells also retain 87-88% F1 under cross-attack transfer and remain stable under memory poisoning.

</details>

Abstract: With the rapid proliferation of IoT devices, security concerns have dramatically escalated and intrusion detection systems have become critical for protecting networked environments. This paper presents an improved CNN-LSTM based intrusion detection model that combines multi-class classification, dataset integration, and temporal feature learning to enhance detection performance in IoT networks. Using network traffic data, the proposed approach is evaluated on intrusion detection tasks and achieves an accuracy of approximately 97%. Experimental results demonstrate that the model effectively detects multiple attack categories while maintaining stable training and validation performance. The integration of convolutional and recurrent neural network components enables the framework to capture both spatial and temporal characteristics of network traffic, improving overall intrusion detection capability in IoT environments.
