### [51] [Where Do Large Language Models Fail on Competitive Programming? A Taxonomy of Failures by Algorithm Type and Difficulty Rating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05228)
*Ayush Kumar Jha,Shalini Jha*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 系统研究了LLM在竞赛编程中的失败模式，发现Chain-of-Thought推理会显著降低GPT-4o（46.0%→36.8%）和Claude（指令遵循严重退化，编译错误增加244%）的表现，标准提示工程无法弥补算法推理差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前技术报告仅发布聚合通过率，掩盖了LLM在特定领域（竞赛编程）的脆弱性，缺乏对失败模式的系统研究。

Method: 构建包含315道Codeforces问题的平衡分类体系（7种算法类别×3个难度等级），在严格执行条件下评估GPT-4o和Claude Sonnet 4.6（温度0.2），并进行消融实验对比直接零样本生成与零样本Chain-of-Thought推理。

Result: CoT推理使GPT-4o通过率从46.0%降至36.8%，贪婪逻辑问题加剧；Claude在CoT下保持63.5%的逻辑基线，但编译错误从9增至31（+244%）；错误答案（WA）是主导失败类型（GPT-4o超90%，Claude约70%）。

Conclusion: 标准提示工程技术无法弥补竞赛编程环境中的算法推理差距，CoT可能对某些模型产生反效果。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate increasing proficiency on competitive programming benchmarks, yet technical reports predominantly publish aggregate pass rates, obscuring domain-specific vulnerabilities. We present a systematic empirical study of LLM failure patterns using a balanced taxonomy of 315 Codeforces problems across seven algorithm categories and three difficulty tiers. We evaluate GPT-4o and Claude Sonnet 4.6 under strict execution-based conditions, controlling for temperature (T = 0.2). To isolate the impact of reasoning frameworks on algorithmic correctness, we conduct an ablation study comparing direct zero-shot generation against zero-shot Chain-of-Thought (CoT). Our findings reveal a severe divergence from standard NLP benchmarks: forcing CoT aggressively penalizes GPT-4o, dropping its pass rate from 46.0% to 36.8% and exacerbating a critical weakness in Greedy logic. Conversely, while Claude maintains a higher logical baseline (63.5% under CoT), the expanded text generation severely degrades its markdown instruction adherence, causing its Compile Errors to more than triple (from 9 to 31, a 244% increase). Furthermore, failure-mode analysis indicates that Wrong Answer (WA) is the dominant verdict for both models--accounting for over 90% of GPT-4o's and roughly 70% of Claude's unaccepted solutions. These findings empirically demonstrate that standard prompt engineering techniques fail to bridge the algorithmic reasoning gap in competitive programming environments.

</details>


### [52] [DeployBench: Benchmarking LLM Agents for Research Artifact Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05238)
*Yuanli Wang,Yaoyao Qian,Yue Zhang,Hanhan Zhou,Jindan Huang,Tianfu Fu,Qiuyang Mang,Huanzhi Mao,Wenhao Chai,Wendong Fan,Liqiang Jing*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DeployBench是一个包含51个研究工件部署任务的基准测试，覆盖AI/ML、计算机系统和科学计算领域。评估四个LLM智能体（使用OpenHands）的通过率仅为7.8%-51.0%，失败主要源于智能体过早自我终止（97/154），它们对完成条件的判断比论文要求更弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有环境设置基准未涵盖研究工件部署的完整范围（多语言工具链、GPU/CUDA和内核配置等系统级依赖、遗留工件兼容性），而现有LLM智能体虽在软件工程和ML研究任务上取得进展，但通常假设已具备可运行的执行环境。

Method: 构建51个跨领域（AI/ML、计算机系统、科学计算）的研究工件部署任务基准DeployBench，每个任务通过隐藏管道执行论文指定实验并验证输出；使用OpenHands评估四个SOTA LLM的表现。

Result: 四个LLM的通过率范围为7.8%-51.0%；154次失败中有97次是智能体主动自我终止（自停止），其预完成检查验证的目标弱于论文要求。

Conclusion: DeployBench揭示了当前LLM智能体与自主部署之间的差距，为科学研究智能体提供了现实的测试平台。

Abstract: LLM agents have made rapid progress on software engineering and ML research tasks, but these advances often assume access to a working runnable environment. For research artifacts released alongside published papers, setting up such an environment from a fresh machine remains a major bottleneck. Existing environment setup benchmarks do not cover the full scope of research artifact deployment, which involves multi-language toolchains, system-level dependencies beyond containers (e.g. GPU/CUDA and kernel configurations), and legacy artifact compatibility. We introduce DeployBench, a multi-domain benchmark of 51 research-artifact deployment tasks spanning AI/ML, computer systems, and scientific computing, covering all these dimensions. Each task is verified by a hidden pipeline that executes the paper's designated experiment and checks its outputs. Evaluating four state-of-the-art LLMs with OpenHands yields pass-rates from 7.8% - 51.0% . Failures are dominated by a completion-judgment problem: 97 of 154 are agent-terminated self-stops, where the agent's pre-finish checks validate a different or weaker target than the paper-specific task requires. DeployBench highlights the gap between current agents and autonomous deployment, and offers a realistic testbed for scientific research agents.

</details>


### [53] [SWE-InfraBench: Evaluating Language Models on Cloud Infrastructure Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05249)
*Natalia Tarasova,Enrique Balp-Straffon,Aleksei Iancheruk,Yevhenii Sielskyi,Nikita Kozodoi,Liam H. Byrne,Jack Butler,Dayuan Jiang,Marcin Czelej,Andrew Ang,Yash Shah,Roi Blanco,Sergei Ivanov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SWE-InfraBench，一个面向AWS CDK（TypeScript）基础设施即代码（IaC）的增量代码编辑基准测试集，评估表明当前最先进LLM在该任务上表现有限（最佳模型Sonnet 3.7仅34%成功率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IaC基准主要关注声明式范式（如Terraform）和从零生成完整代码，缺乏对实际企业开发中常见的增量代码编辑场景的评估，且LLM在IaC任务上的能力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 从数十个真实AWS CDK代码库中收集多样化样本，构建包含自然语言指令驱动的代码修改任务，每个任务要求模型对现有代码库进行增量修改，并通过提供测试用例验证修改正确性。

Result: 评估显示当前LLM表现有限：Sonnet 3.7仅34%成功率，DeepSeek R1仅24%成功率，说明即使是顶尖模型在涉及云资源依赖推理和实现模式的任务上仍面临显著困难。

Conclusion: SWE-InfraBench揭示了LLM在IaC增量编辑任务上的严重不足，为未来提升模型在云基础设施代码领域的推理和编辑能力提供了评估基准。

Abstract: Building infrastructure-as-code (IaC) in cloud computing is a critical task, underpinning the reliability, scalability, and security of modern software systems. Despite the remarkable progress of large language models (LLMs) in software engineering -- demonstrated across many dedicated benchmarks -- their capabilities in developing IaC remain underexplored. Unlike existing IaC benchmarks that predominantly center on declarative paradigms such as Terraform and involve generating entire codebases from scratch, our benchmark reflects the incremental code edits common in enterprise development with imperative tools like the AWS CDK. We present SWE-InfraBench, a diverse evaluation dataset sourced from dozens of real-world IaC codebases that challenge LLMs to perform realistic code modifications in AWS CDK repositories. Each example requires models to implement changes to existing codebases based on natural language instructions, with success determined by passing provided test cases. These tasks demand sophisticated reasoning about cloud resource dependencies and implementation patterns beyond conventional code generation challenges. Our evaluation results reveal significant limitations in current LLMs showing that even state-of-the-art systems struggle with many tasks -- the best model, Sonnet 3.7, succeeds in only 34\% of cases, while specialized reasoning models like DeepSeek R1 achieve just 24% success. The SWE-InfraBench dataset is available at: https://www.kaggle.com/datasets/64e59070fd51c0278560b01eb5dc4f3c447d5268cdabe5a350d2969e4413fea5

</details>


### [54] [Towards Persistent Case-Based Memory for Autonomous Data Science: A CBR-Augmented R&D-Agent with a Locally Deployable Small Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05250)
*Felix Stocker*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出将结构化、质量可控的基于案例推理（CBR）层集成到微软R&D-Agent框架中，并首次使用Gemma 4 31B Dense作为本地可部署的小语言模型（SLM）骨干，在Kaggle竞赛上验证了该方法相比无CBR基线具有更高的准确率和更低的方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有顶尖自主数据科学智能体依赖云端大模型且缺乏跨会话持久记忆，同时小语言模型作为本地可部署骨干的可行性尚未被验证，这两个空白亟待填补。

Method: 在R&D-Agent框架中集成持久化CBR层，通过单环境变量切换的子类覆盖三个R&D循环阶段；案例以结构化记录存储，包含可执行代码快照与质量元数据，并引入五门质量过滤器与启发式重用检测机制（结合嵌入相似度、代码指纹重叠与注入溯源）。

Result: 在Spaceship Titanic竞赛中，CBR方法准确率0.8147高于基线0.8098（d=-1.41），方差显著降低；108次检索事件的平均嵌入相似度0.882，平均代码指纹相似度0.305，表明CBR提供概念性指导而非逐字复制。

Conclusion: 结构化的CBR层结合SLM可有效提升自主数据科学智能体的性能与稳定性，且知识传递以概念引导为主，而非代码抄袭。

Abstract: Most top-performing autonomous data-science agents rely on frontier cloud models and lack persistent, cross-session memory. This paper addresses two open gaps: (1) the underexplored use of formally structured, quality-controlled Case-Based Reasoning (CBR) case bases coupling symbolic case records with executable code artefacts; and (2) the untested viability of Small Language Models (SLMs) as locally deployable agent backbones. We present CBR-augmented R&D-Agent, integrating a persistent CBR layer into Microsoft's R&D-Agent framework with a custom backend for Gemma 4 31B Dense -- the first published end-to-end evaluation of Gemma 4 as an autonomous data-science agent backbone. The CBR layer overrides three R&D loop phases via a surgical subclass toggled by a single environment variable. Cases are stored as structured records with executable code snapshots and quality metadata; a five-gate quality filter and a heuristic reuse-detection mechanism assess knowledge transfer by combining embedding similarity, code-fingerprint overlap, and injection provenance. Evaluated on two Kaggle competitions (NOMAD 2018, Spaceship Titanic) with four seeds over eight improvement loops each, CBR achieves directionally higher accuracy than the CBR-disabled baseline on Spaceship Titanic (0.8147 vs. 0.8098, d = -1.41) with substantially lower variance. Heuristic reuse detection across 108 retrieval events shows high semantic relevance (mean embedding similarity 0.882) alongside variable structural proximity (mean code-fingerprint similarity 0.305), consistent with conceptual guidance rather than verbatim code copying.

</details>


### [55] [A Taxonomy of Runtime Faults in Model Context Protocol Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05339)
*Joshua Owotogbe,Indika Kumara,Willem-Jan van den Heuvel,Damian Andrew Tamburri,Antonio Ken Iannillo,Roberto Natella*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次对MCP（模型上下文协议）服务器中的运行时故障进行了实证分类学研究，通过分析473个活跃GitHub仓库中的837个故障线程，构建了一个包含11个顶级类别、27个子类别和73个叶级故障类型的分类体系，并通过55名开发者的调查验证了其外部有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP协议在工具增强型AI工作流中被快速采用，但引入了新的可靠性挑战——配置参数虽被接受却未在运行时强制执行，导致意外默认行为。目前缺乏对这类运行时故障特征的实证研究。

Method: 采用自底向上的开放编码程序，手动分析了来自473个活跃维护的MCP服务器GitHub仓库的837个MCP特定运行时故障线程，并通过调查55名MCP服务器开发者来评估分类学的外部有效性。

Result: 构建的分类学包含11个顶级类别和27个子类别（73个叶级故障类型），覆盖协议交互、工具调用、模式执行、状态管理、模型-提供者集成、安全验证以及超时/取消操作等故障。开发者调查显示，受访者平均经历过27个子类别中的20个，且无任何类别未被观察到。

Conclusion: 该分类学反映了MCP系统中广泛观察到的运行时故障，将有助于未来AI软件的维护和演化。

Abstract: MCP (Model Context Protocol) enables LLMs (Large Language Models) to interact with external tools and data sources via a standardized protocol. Its rapid adoption in tool-augmented Artificial Intelligence (AI) workflows has introduced new reliability challenges, such as configuration parameters that are accepted but not enforced at runtime, leading to unintended default behavior, whose runtime fault characteristics remain empirically unexamined. We present the first empirical taxonomy of runtime faults in MCP servers. We manually analyzed 837 MCP-specific runtime fault threads from 473 actively maintained MCP server GitHub repositories and derived a taxonomy using a bottom-up open coding procedure. The taxonomy comprises 11 top-level categories and 27 subcategories (73 leaf fault types), covering recurrent failures across protocol interactions, tool invocations, schema enforcement, state management, model-provider integration, security validation, and timeouts or explicit cancellations of in-progress operations. To assess the taxonomy's external validity, we surveyed 55 MCP server developers. Respondents reported experiencing an average of 20 of the 27 fault subcategories, and no category remained unobserved. These results indicate that the taxonomy reflects widely observed runtime failures in MCP-based systems and shall assist AI software maintenance and evolution in the future.

</details>


### [56] [Human oversight of agentic systems in practice: Examining the oversight work, challenges, and heuristics of developers using software agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05391)
*Shipi Dhanorkar,Samir Passi,Mihaela Vorvoreanu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过对17位经验丰富的开发者进行访谈，本文探索了开发者在使用自主软件代理时执行的四种新兴监督工作形式（先验控制、协同规划、实时监控和事后审查），揭示了监督不仅是被动和回顾性的，也是预防性和主动性的，并记录了开发者面临的监督挑战及应对策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于自主软件代理监督的研究多为概念性和规范性框架，缺乏对用户实际监督行为的实证了解。本文旨在通过实证研究弥合这一理论-实践差距。

Method: 对17位经验丰富的开发者进行半结构化访谈，采用探索性研究方法分析开发者如何执行监督工作、何时进行以及如何进行，同时记录面临的挑战和应对策略。

Result: 识别出四种新兴监督工作形式：先验控制、协同规划、实时监控和事后审查；发现监督工作不仅是被动回顾性的，还包括预防性和主动性的维度；描述了开发者面临的情境化监督挑战（如难以审查代理生成的代码）及采用的启发式策略（如用测试结果作为代码正确性的保证）。

Conclusion: 研究为代理监督的理论讨论提供了早期实证锚点，提出了高层总结、未来研究方向，以及对以人为中心的软件代理设计和软件工程实践的启示。

Abstract: Autonomous software agents hold promise to increase developer productivity but make mistakes and exhibit novel failure modes, making human oversight central to successful human-agent collaboration. Existing research on agent oversight is largely conceptual; normative frameworks exist, but how users actually oversee agents is less known. In this paper, we bridge this gap by providing early empirical anchors for the theoretical discourse on agent oversight. Drawing on interviews with 17 experienced developers, we conduct an exploratory inquiry examining what forms of emergent oversight work developers perform, when, and how. We also document the oversight challenges developers face and the strategies they have started using to address them. We found at least four forms of emergent oversight work: a priori control, co-planning, real-time monitoring, and post hoc review. We show that oversight work is not only reactive and retrospective, as portrayed in existing research, but also preventative and proactive. We describe situated oversight challenges (e.g., difficulty reviewing agent-generated code) and outline heuristics developers adopt to address such challenges (e.g., using test results as guarantees for code correctness). We conclude with high-level takeaways, future research directions, implications for the human-centered design of software agents and for software engineering practice, and limitations of our research.

</details>


### [57] [REStack: A Large-Scale Dataset of Reverse Engineering Discussions from Stack Exchange](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05493)
*Md Humaun Kabir,Md Rakibul Islam,Farha Kamal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文构建了REStack数据集，包含来自Stack Overflow和Reverse Engineering Stack Exchange的12,000+条逆向工程(RE)相关讨论帖子，覆盖15年以上数据。通过LDA结合遗传算法优化识别出23个RE主题并分为6大类，分析了社区讨论的挑战、难度和知识缺口。RE讨论以实践性和任务导向为主，重点关注调试、反编译和系统级分析，而内存、固件和文件格式分析相关主题难度高且未解决率也高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆向工程在软件工程和网络安全中至关重要，但对RE从业者在真实场景中面临的挑战、主题和知识缺口缺乏系统的实证理解，且缺乏公开可用的RE讨论数据集。

Method: 从Stack Overflow和Reverse Engineering Stack Exchange收集12,000+条RE相关帖子；采用基于遗传算法(GA)优化超参数的LDA主题建模，结合人工主题标注；利用社区交互信号（如未回答率和响应时间）丰富元数据和难度指标。

Result: 识别出23个语义连贯的RE主题，归为6个高层面主题类别；发现RE讨论以实践性和任务导向为主，重点在调试、反编译和系统级分析；内存、固件和文件格式分析相关主题表现出高难度和高未解决率。

Conclusion: REStack为实证研究、教育研究以及AI/LLM辅助RE工具的开发评估提供了可复用的资源；通过开源数据集和脚本推动可复现研究和数据驱动的RE实践支持。

Abstract: Reverse engineering (RE) is a critical activity in software engineering and cybersecurity, supporting tasks such as malware analysis, vulnerability discovery, legacy system maintenance, and firmware inspection. Despite its importance, there is limited empirical understanding of the challenges, topics, and knowledge gaps faced by RE practitioners in real-world settings, and no publicly available dataset has systematically captured RE discussions from developer Q&A forums.
  In this paper, we present REStack, a large-scale dataset of RE discussions collected from Stack Overflow and the dedicated Reverse Engineering Stack Exchange site. The dataset comprises over 12,000 RE-related posts spanning more than 15 years. Using Latent Dirichlet Allocation (LDA) with Genetic Algorithm (GA)-based hyperparameter optimization, followed by manual topic labeling, we identify 23 semantically coherent RE topics grouped into six high-level thematic categories. The dataset is further enriched with metadata and difficulty indicators derived from community interaction signals, such as unanswered rates and response times.
  Our analysis reveals that RE discussions are predominantly practical and task-oriented, with strong emphasis on debugging, decompilation, and system-level analysis, while topics related to memory, firmware, and file format analysis exhibit high difficulty and unresolved rates. Beyond empirical characterization, REStack provides a reusable resource for empirical studies, educational research, and the development and evaluation of AI- and LLM-based developer assistance tools for RE. By releasing the dataset and accompanying scripts, this work aims to facilitate reproducible research and advance data-driven support for RE practice.

</details>


### [58] [STMutants: A Mutation Testing Dataset for Structured Text Programs in Industrial Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05499)
*Md Humaun Kabir,Md Rakibul Islam,Helen H. Lou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了STMutants，首个面向IEC 61131-3结构化文本（ST）程序的公开变异测试基准数据集，包含108个一阶变异体，涵盖7类变异算子，并评估了三种大语言模型在测试用例生成和变异检测中的表现，最高检测准确率达94.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ST程序广泛应用于可编程逻辑控制器（PLC）的实时安全关键工业环境，但目前缺乏支持可重复变异测试研究的公开基准数据集，阻碍了该领域自动化测试、变异分析和故障定位等研究的开展。

Method: 采用四阶段方法论构建数据集：故障类型分析与算子选择、语法变换、可编译性验证、人工等价筛选（kappa=0.87）。数据集包含11个ST程序生成的110个变异体（保留108个），覆盖值、关系、算术、逻辑、否定、操作插入/删除、初始化7类变异算子。评估实验采用两阶段设置：LLM生成测试套件，随后预测变异体kill/survive状态。

Result: 三种LLM在108个保留变异体上的变异检测准确率分别为86.1%、94.4%和86.1%，统计分析证实模型间存在显著性能差异。

Conclusion: STMutants作为首个公开的ST程序变异测试基准，为PLC软件自动化测试生成、变异分析、故障定位和AI辅助质量保证领域提供了可重复研究的基础设施。

Abstract: Mutation testing is widely used to evaluate test-suite effectiveness, yet IEC 61131-3 Structured Text (ST) programs still lack a publicly available benchmark that supports reproducible mutation-based research. This gap is especially important because ST is extensively used in Programmable Logic Controllers (PLCs) that operate in real-time, safety-critical industrial environments, where software faults may cause equipment damage, production loss, or unsafe system behavior. To address this need, we present STMutants, a curated mutation testing dataset for industrial automation software. STMutants contains 110 generated first-order mutants derived from 11 ST programs collected from the OSCAT basic library and industrially relevant sources, of which 108 are retained after observability and equivalence screening. The dataset covers seven mutation operator categories adapted from classical taxonomies for the PLC domain, including value, relational, arithmetic, logical, negation, operation insertion/omission, and initialization faults. Each mutant is constructed through a four-phase methodology: fault-type profiling and operator selection, syntactic transformation, compilability verification, and manual equivalence screening with strong inter-rater agreement (kappa = 0.87). To demonstrate the usefulness of the dataset, we evaluate three large language models (LLMs) in a two-phase setting: test-suite generation followed by mutation kill/survive prediction. Across 108 retained mutants, the models achieve mutation detection accuracies of 86.1%, 94.4%, and 86.1%, respectively, with statistical analysis confirming significant performance differences. By providing the first publicly available mutation benchmark for ST programs, STMutants enables reproducible research on automated test generation, mutation analysis, fault localization, and AI-assisted quality assurance for PLC software.

</details>


### [59] [ADK Arena: Evaluating Agent Development Kits via LLM-as-a-Developer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05548)
*Jintao Huang,Xiaomin Li,Gaurav Mittal,Yu Hu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM-as-a-Developer方法，通过标准化评估流程系统分析了51个Python ADK框架在4个基准上的性能，发现框架间存在显著差异（成本变化5.6倍、中位解决率仅32%），且无单一框架占优，文档、源码和参数知识在框架使用中具有高度可替代性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent开发工具包（ADKs）的快速普及已远超对框架选择如何影响Agent性能的实证理解，亟需一种系统化、可控的评估方法来量化比较不同框架的有效性和可用性。

Method: 提出LLM-as-a-Developer方法论，用LLM编码智能体替代人类开发者，通过从文档学习API、编写代码并迭代修复直至测试通过。实现ADK Arena自动化流水线，包含每个框架的Docker隔离、三级验证管道和4个基准适配器，评估全部51个流行的Python ADK框架。

Result: 57%的生成成功率，成本跨框架变化5.6倍（0.6-3.4美元）；最佳框架解决率达80%，可击败通用前沿编码智能体，但中位框架仅解决32%；信息源消融实验中框架使用率仅28-40%窄区间波动。

Conclusion: 框架间存在显著性能和易用性差异，无单一框架主导；API复杂性成本不能预测成功；文档、源码和参数知识在框架使用中高度可替代，说明学习瓶颈不在于信息来源而在于其他因素。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of Agent Development Kits (ADKs), SDK-level frameworks for building LLM-powered autonomous agents, has outpaced any empirical understanding of how framework choice affects agent performance. We propose \textbf{LLM-as-a-Developer}, a methodology that replaces human developers with an LLM coding agent that learns each framework's API from documentation, writes agent code, and iteratively repairs it through a validate-and-feedback loop until tests pass. By holding the developer constant and varying only the framework, generation effort becomes a quantitative proxy for API usability and the resulting agents provide a controlled measure of framework effectiveness. We implement this in \textbf{ADK Arena}, a fully automated pipeline with per-framework Docker isolation, a three-level validation pipeline, and benchmark adapters for SWE-bench, $τ^2$-bench, Terminal-Bench, and MCP-Atlas. Evaluating all 51 popular Python ADK frameworks (204 agent--benchmark pairs), we find that: (1)~generation succeeds for 57\% of runs, and its cost varies 5.6$\times$ across frameworks (\$0.6 to \$3.4 per agent), a quantitative proxy for API complexity, though cost alone does not predict success; (2)~no single framework dominates: the best single-benchmark ADK agents resolve up to 80\% of tasks and can even \emph{beat} general-purpose frontier coding agents at a fraction of the cost, yet the median framework resolves only 32\%; (3)~across information-source ablations, genuine framework usage stays within a narrow 28--40\% band (highest with raw source access and still 33\% with no reference material at all), indicating that documentation, source code, and parametric knowledge are largely substitutable rather than any one being a hard bottleneck.

</details>


### [60] [SmellBench: Towards Fine-Grained Evaluation of Code Agents on Refactoring Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05574)
*Fake Lin,Binbin Hu,Xi Zhu,Ziwei Zhao,Zhi Zheng,Ziqi Liu,Zhiqiang Zhang,Jun Zhou,Tong Xu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出SmellBench基准，通过主动注入代码异味来评估代码智能体的重构能力。实验显示最佳组合（Qwen Code + Claude Sonnet 4.5）仅获得50.34分的异味消除得分，主要问题在于缺乏跨文件理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要评估代码智能体的功能正确性，而忽略了长期可维护性。代码智能体的滥用常产生臃肿、混乱的代码，但目前缺乏评估其重构能力的标准化基准。

Method: 构建SmellBench基准：从真实仓库的干净代码中主动注入代码异味，生成294个重构案例，涵盖7种异味类型、3个难度级别、2种指令设置。设计3个评估维度（功能正确性、定位能力、重构质量）。

Result: 最佳组合（Qwen Code + Claude Sonnet 4.5）异味消除得分仅50.34。现有智能体倾向于处理局部代码异味，缺乏跨文件理解能力，无法进行全面的异味消除。

Conclusion: 当前代码智能体在代码重构方面存在显著不足，特别是跨文件的异味识别与消除能力薄弱，这揭示了未来改进的方向。

Abstract: Code Agents have achieved remarkable advances in recent years, exhibiting strong capabilities across a wide range of software engineering tasks. However, their misuse often produces bloated and disorganized code that impairing readability, extensibility, and robustness. Despite this risk, existing benchmarks largely evaluate functional correctness rather than long-term maintainability of code agents. In this paper, we propose SmellBench, an extensible code refactoring benchmark that proactively injects code smells into clean code snippets from real-world repositories. This design enables the generation of controlled, high-quality, and diverse refactoring cases with human-written ground truth. Specifically, it contains 294 cases spanning 7 popular smell types, 3 difficulty levels, 2 instruction settings across 7 real-world repositories. We further design 3 evaluation aspects covering functional correctness, localization ability, and refactoring quality assessment. Experiments with 2 popular agents and 6 large langauge models (LLMs) show that the best combination - Qwen Code + Claude Sonnet 4.5 - achieved only a 50.34 score of smell elimination. Further analysis reveals that this gap arises from a focus on local code smells and a lack of cross-file understanding, which hinders comprehensive smell elimination.

</details>


### [61] [The End of Software Engineering: How AI Agents Are Fundamentally Restructuring the Software Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05608)
*Zhenfeng Cao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出AI智能体（以LLM为核心推理引擎、动态生成和废弃代码的系统）正在从根本上重塑软件工程范式，并定义了从传统软件（代码承载决策逻辑）向智能体系统（代码作为LLM推理循环的临时工具）的转变，提出了“智能体即服务”（AaaS）和“智能体工程学”作为新兴学科的概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战软件工程半个多世纪以来的基本前提——即人类工程师分解问题、将决策逻辑编码为静态代码并手动适应需求变化——论证AI智能体的出现不是增量改进，而是对软件范式的根本性重构。

Method: 基于第一性原理的复杂性缩放分析，形式化区分传统软件与智能体系统；追溯从许可软件到SaaS再到AaaS的历史演进；通过SWE-bench Verified、EvoClaw、LangChain多智能体协调研究等基准证据进行分析。

Result: 证明了智能体范式的变革潜力及其当前局限性，展示了各基准研究中的实证证据。

Conclusion: 提出迈向自演化智能体生态系统的四阶段路线图，并为从业人员提供具体建议。

Abstract: For over half a century, software engineering has operated on a foundational premise: human engineers decompose problems, encode decision logic into static code, and manually adapt that code as requirements evolve. This paper argues that the emergence of AI agents -- systems where large language models serve as the primary reasoning engine, dynamically generating and discarding code as an instrumental resource -- constitutes not an incremental improvement but a fundamental restructuring of the software paradigm. Drawing on first-principles analysis of complexity scaling, we formalize the distinction between traditional software (where code is the carrier of decision logic) and agentic systems (where code is ephemeral tooling for an LLM-driven reasoning loop). We trace the historical arc from licensed software to SaaS to what we term Agent-as-a-Service (AaaS), showing that each shift transferred additional complexity away from end-users. We introduce the concept of Agentic Engineering as an emergent discipline -- distinct from software engineering in its core object of study, control model, and human role. Through analysis of recent benchmark evidence including SWE-bench Verified, EvoClaw, and LangChain's multi-agent coordination studies, we demonstrate both the transformative potential of the agentic paradigm and its current limitations. We conclude with a four-stage roadmap toward self-evolving agent ecosystems and concrete recommendations for practitioners navigating this transition.

</details>


### [62] [Enhancing Software Engineering Through Closed-Loop Memory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05646)
*Xuehang Guo,Zora Zhiruo Wang,Qingyun Wang,Graham Neubig,Xingyao Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出MemOp，一个面向软件工程智能体的闭环记忆增强框架，通过将记忆效用锚定在验证过的下游影响上，同时作为任务无关的评估基准和免标注的优化信号，在单轮和跨轮记忆增强中均提升了智能体性能（成功率+5.25%，解析效率+4.63%，计算成本降低≥9.79%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的软件工程智能体本质上是片段式的，无法跨任务保留、精炼和复用经验，每次重建上下文并重复类似错误。即使有记忆支持，也缺乏原则性的、任务无关的记忆效用量化方法，难以严格评估或跨智能体/跨设置推广。

Method: 提出MemOp闭环框架，将记忆效用定义为经过验证的下游影响，具体包括：(1)作为任务无关的评估基准（评估记忆好坏）；(2)作为免标注的优化信号（自动指导记忆更新）。在单轮和跨轮记忆增强两种设置下进行互补评估。

Result: 在单轮和跨轮记忆增强中，MemOp一致提升SE智能体性能，成功率绝对提升最高5.25%，解析效率提升4.63%，同时计算成本降低至少9.79%。

Conclusion: MemOp通过将记忆效用锚定在验证过的下游影响上，为SE智能体提供了一种可评估、可优化的记忆增强方案，有效弥补了现有方法中缺乏原则性记忆效用的缺陷。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have enabled powerful software engineering (SE) agents capable of navigating complex codebases and resolving real-world issues. However, these agents remain fundamentally episodic: they fail to retain, refine, and reuse experiences across tasks, repeatedly reconstructing context from scratch and reproducing similar mistakes. Even with memory support, they offer no remedy for the absence of a principled, task-agnostic \textit{memory utility}, making them difficult to evaluate rigorously or generalize across agents and settings. To tackle these limitations, we introduce \ours, a closed-loop framework for memory augmentation in SE agents. \ours grounds memory utility in \textit{validated downstream impact}, establishing utility as both a task-agnostic \textbf{evaluation benchmark} and an annotation-free \textbf{optimization signal}. Through complementary evaluation on \textit{single-episode} and \textit{cross-episode} memory augmentation, results demonstrate that \ours consistently improves SE agents across settings, achieving absolute gains of up to $\uparrow5.25\%$ in success rate and $\uparrow4.63\%$ in resolve efficiency, while substantially reducing computational cost by $\geq9.79\%$. Our project page: \href{https://xhguo7.github.io/MemOp/}{https://xhguo7.github.io/MemOp/}.

</details>


### [63] [When Surface Form Changes Moderation Decisions: A Paired Study of Code-Mixed Workflow Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05654)
*Suraj Babu Thimma Krishnaram*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了在印地语-英语混合输入下，内容审核工作流（ALLOW/FLAG/REVIEW）相较于纯英语输入产生的决策不稳定性和工作负载变化，发现混合输入导致审查率从0.138升至0.297，非仇恨内容误标率从0.069升至0.104，决策翻转率达0.265，而简单的推迟策略虽能减少错误但增加了审查负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仇恨言论审核评估通常基于纯英语输入的分类准确率，但实际部署系统需处理多种语言混合输入（如泰米尔语-英语混合），且工作流涉及允许、标记、审查等多级动作。现有研究缺乏对混合输入下审核工作流变化的系统评估。

Method: 采用配对评估设置：将相同内容分别用纯英语和泰米尔语-英语混合表达，在基于纯英语开发数据调优的阈值下，比较两种输入下的决策一致性、审核率、误标率等指标，并测试了一种基于分歧的推迟规则。

Result: 混合输入产生0.265的决策翻转率；审查率从0.138升至0.297；非仇恨误标率从0.069升至0.104；纯泰米尔语输入退化更严重；分歧推迟规则虽降低自动错误但增加审查负载。

Conclusion: 工作流级别的评估能揭示标准分类评估无法捕捉的审核失败；混合输入显著破坏基于纯英语调优的审核工作流稳定性；需要针对多语言混合场景设计更鲁棒的审核策略。

Abstract: Hate moderation is often evaluated as classification on clean English inputs, but deployed systems must route content to actions such as ALLOW, FLAG, or REVIEW. We study how this workflow changes under code-mixed inputs using a paired evaluation setting where the same underlying content is expressed as clean English and Tamil-English code-mix. Under thresholds tuned on clean English development data, code-mixed inputs produce substantial action instability, with a paired clean- to-code-mix decision flip rate of 0.265. The main workflow effects are increased review burden and increased false-flagging of non-hateful content: review rate rises from 0.138 to 0.297 and non-hate false-flag rate rises from 0.069 to 0.104. Tamil-only inputs show stronger degradation overall, suggesting a broader language-coverage limitation rather than the same code-mixed instability pattern. A simple disagreement-based deferral rule reduces automatic errors on stressed inputs, but only by increasing review load. These results show that workflow-level evaluation reveals moderation failures that standard classification summaries can miss.

</details>


### [64] [Microskill Architecture: A Modular Skill-Driven Framework for AI-Native Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05720)
*Mohammad Zare,Omid Abdolrahmani*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出MicroSkill架构，一种受微服务启发的模块化设计范式，将知识封装为原子化的技能胶囊，并通过动态路由器选择与任务语义相关的胶囊，从而解决大语言模型和AI编码代理在上下文窗口管理中的效率与精度问题。在15个复杂特性的企业内容管理系统案例中，该架构将token消耗降低超过90%，首次编译成功率提升近一倍，完全消除架构违规，并通过自学习机制自主提取和注册了7个新技能胶囊。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型和AI编码代理在软件开发中面临结构性问题：当开发者将完整的项目文档和代码注入模型记忆时，模型丢失中间序列信息、token成本激增且架构发生漂移。现有方法缺乏有效的上下文窗口管理机制，无法在保持准确性和效率的同时处理大型代码库。

Method: 受微服务架构启发，将知识分解为原子化、边界清晰的技能胶囊，而非将整个代码库供给代理；动态路由器根据任务语义过滤选择相关胶囊；将上下文分配形式化为受token预算约束的语义相关性优化问题。

Result: 企业内容管理系统（15个复杂特性）案例显示：token消耗降低超过90%，首次编译成功率提升近一倍，架构违规完全消除，自学习机制自主提取并注册了7个新技能胶囊。

Conclusion: MicroSkill架构为建设AI原生开发系统提供了可扩展的基础，使系统更高效、更可靠，并具备随时间演化的能力。

Abstract: Large language models and AI coding agents have reshaped software development, but the path to fully AI-native systems faces structural challenges. Chief among them is managing context windows without losing accuracy or efficiency. When developers inject full project documentation and code into a model's memory, the model loses mid-sequence information, token costs spiral, and architecture drifts. This paper presents MicroSkill Architecture: a modular design paradigm inspired by microservices, applied to knowledge encapsulation instead of service decomposition. Instead of feeding an agent the entire codebase, the architecture partitions knowledge into atomic, sharply scoped skill capsules, and a dynamic router selects only semantically relevant capsules for the task. We formally model context allocation as constrained optimization over semantic relevance subject to a token budget. An empirical case study an enterprise content management system with fifteen complex features shows that MicroSkill cuts token consumption by over 90%, nearly doubles first-try compilation success rates, eliminates architectural violations entirely, and enables autonomous extraction and registration of seven new skill capsules via a self-learning mechanism. These findings suggest MicroSkill Architecture offers a scalable foundation for building AI-native development systems that are more efficient, more reliable, and capable of evolving over time.

</details>


### [65] [Human Oversight and Overload: Two Hidden and Costly Burdens of AI-Assisted Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05770)
*Vahid Garousi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文揭示了AI辅助软件工程中两个被忽视的负担：持续的人工监督审查需求，以及大量AI建议导致的认知过载问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在改变软件工程师工作方式的同时，带来了隐藏的成本和负担，但这些负担（尤其是人工监督和认知过载）往往被学界和工业界忽视，需要被系统性地揭示和讨论。

Method: 融合来自从业者的近期观点和证据，对AI辅助软件工程中两项被忽视的负担进行特征化描述和分析。

Result: 识别并描述了两种关键负担：(1) 持续人工监督——工程师必须审查、验证甚至返工AI生成产物，这是不可选择的责任；(2) 认知过载——大量AI建议、提示和解决方案使开发者的心智资源被过度拉伸。

Conclusion: 呼吁学界和工业界关注这些挑战，并开启关于团队如何在日常AI辅助软件工程中应对这些负担的讨论。

Abstract: AI is changing how software engineers work, but it often comes with hidden burdens and costs. In this paper, we characterize two such often-overlooked burdens: (1) the constant need for human oversight and inspection of AI-generated artifacts; and (2) the growing cognitive overload on software engineers from receiving large amounts of suggestions from AI tools. The need for human oversight is not optional-engineers must review, validate, and sometimes rework what AI produces. At the same time, the flood of AI suggestions, prompts, and possible solutions can leave developers mentally stretched. By blending evidence from recent opinions from practitioners, we highlight these often-overlooked challenges and open a conversation about how teams can handle them in day-to-day AI-assisted software engineering.

</details>


### [66] [Asuka-Bench: Benchmarking Code Agents on Underspecified User Intent and Multi-Round Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05920)
*Xin Wang,Liangtai Sun,Yaoming Zhu,Shuang Zhou,Jiaxing Liu,Fengjiao Chen,Lin Qiu,Xuezhi Cao,Xunliang Cai,Licheng Zhang,Zhendong Mao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Asuka-Bench 是一个多轮迭代的代码生成基准测试，模拟真实Web开发中用户逐步反馈的场景。包含50个Web任务、784个评估标准和2402个预期结果。对8个LLM在2个智能体框架下进行基准测试，结果显示模型间任务通过率差异达38个百分点，最强模型三轮后仅完成52%的项目，基准远未饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成基准仅评估单次完整提示到一次性输出的映射，但真实Web开发中用户很少一开始就写出完整规范，许多需求只有在看到中间结果并反馈后才会明确。因此需要能模拟多轮迭代、基于浏览器渲染行为评估的基准。

Method: 构建Asuka-Bench基准，每个任务通过闭环解决：代码智能体生成Web项目，UI智能体在部署网站上执行测试用例，用户LLM将评估结果转化为自然语言反馈用于下一轮迭代。包含50个Web任务，每个任务包含多个未明确指定的用户意图，支持多轮细化。

Result: 对8个LLM在2个智能体框架下进行基准测试。模型间的加权任务通过率差异达38个百分点，模型在从反馈中修复的能力上也存在显著差异。最强模型经过三轮迭代后仅完成52%的项目。

Conclusion: Asuka-Bench能有效区分不同模型的代码生成和迭代修复能力，且远未达到饱和状态，为未来研究提供了充足改进空间。

Abstract: Existing code-generation benchmarks score a single mapping from a complete prompt to a one-shot output. However, real web development is different. Users seldom write a full spec at the start; many requirements only become clear once they look at an intermediate result and react to it. We present Asuka-Bench, a benchmark that pairs underspecified user intent with multi-round refinement, grounded in browser-rendered behavior. Each task is resolved through a closed loop: a Code Agent generates a web project, a UI Agent executes test cases on the deployed site, and a User LLM turns evaluation outcomes into natural-language feedback for the next round. The benchmark comprises 50 web tasks with 784 evaluation criteria and 2402 expected outcomes. We benchmark 8 LLMs across 2 agent frameworks. The results separate models clearly: weighted Task Pass Rate varies by 38 percentage points and models also differ substantially in their ability to repair from feedback. Asuka-Bench is also far from saturated: even the strongest model completes only 52% of projects after three rounds.

</details>


### [67] [Metamorphic Testing with the Rashomon Set: Explanation Faithfulness in Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06056)
*Helge Spieker,Jørn Eirik Betten,Arnaud Gotlieb*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 针对机器学习中的Rashomon效应（多个等性能模型产生不同解释），提出基于蜕变测试的框架，通过五个蜕变关系检验事后解释方法的忠实度，无需真实标签即可评估解释可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习中存在Rashomon效应：多个性能相近的模型对同一任务可能给出截然不同的特征解释，这引发了关于哪些解释值得信任的根本问题。现有方法依赖真实标签评估解释质量，但在实际场景中标签往往不可得或不可靠。

Method: 提出基于蜕变测试的评估框架，定义五个蜕变关系（形式化描述模型行为与特征归因之间应满足的一致性属性），无需真实标签即可检验事后解释方法（如SHAP、LIME）的忠实度。采用模型无关的设计，适用于不同模型和解释器。

Result: 在两个表格回归数据集上应用该框架，结合SHAP和LIME两种事后解释器进行实验，验证了框架的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种实用的、模型无关的工具，帮助选择既具有高精度又具备可靠、可信解释的模型，解决了Rashomon效应下解释可信度评估的难题。

Abstract: Multiple machine learning models can achieve near-equivalent predictive performance on the same task, yet provide divergent feature-based explanations. This is called the Rashomon effect of (explainable) machine learning, and it raises the question of which explanations, if any, are trustworthy. We propose a framework based on metamorphic testing that assesses explanation faithfulness without requiring ground-truth labels by exploring attributed feature importance from post-hoc explanation methods. Five metamorphic relations formalize expected consistency properties between model behavior and feature attributions. We apply this general framework to two tabular regression datasets and two post-hoc explainers (SHAP and LIME) to demonstrate the approach. The framework offers a practical, model-agnostic tool for selecting accurate models with reliable and trustworthy explanations.

</details>


### [68] [TLA-Prover: Verifiable TLA+ Specification Synthesis via Preference-Optimized Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06133)
*Eric Spencer,Arslan Bisharat,Brian Ortiz,Khushboo Bhadauria,TaiNing Wang,George K. Thiruvathukal,Konstantin Laufer,Mohammed Abuhamad*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 TLA-Prover，一个 200 亿参数的 TLA+ 规范合成模型，通过监督微调（SFT）和基于修复的组相对策略优化（GRPO）训练，在 TLC 模型检测器上达到 Gold 和 Diamond 级别 30% 的通过率（pass@1），是未调优基线（8.6%）的约 3.5 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型生成的 TLA+ 规范在语义层面频繁无法通过 TLC 模型检测器（最佳公开基线：语法解析 26.6%，语义模型检测仅 8.6%），亟需一种能够生成语义正确的 TLA+ 规范的方法。

Method: 训练分为两阶段：1) 在已验证示例上进行监督微调（SFT）；2) 基于修复的组相对策略优化（GRPO），模型学习修复自身被拒绝的规范。作为消融实验，还从同一 SFT 检查点训练了直接偏好优化（DPO）变体。使用 TLC 直接提供奖励信号，无需学习奖励模型。输出分为四个等级：Bronze（语法解析通过）、Silver（无警告）、Gold（通过 TLC）、Diamond（正确性属性被微小篡改后 TLC 检测到违例）。

Result: 在 30 个问题的留出基准测试上，TLA-Prover 在 Gold 和 Diamond 级别均达到 9/30（pass@1 = 30%），约为未调优基线（8.6%）的 3.5 倍。DPO 变体在 Diamond 级别达到 20%。所有检查点上 Gold 和 Diamond 指标一致，防止了平凡属性失败模式。

Conclusion: TLA-Prover 通过结合 SFT 和基于修复的 GRPO 训练，显著提升了 LLM 生成语义正确 TLA+ 规范的能力，验证了直接使用 TLC 作为奖励信号的有效性。

Abstract: TLA+ is a formal specification language for verifying distributed systems and safety-critical protocols. Large language models (LLMs) frequently produce TLA+ specifications that fail the TLC model checker for semantic reasons. Across 25 LLMs, the best public baseline is 26.6% syntactic parse and 8.6% semantic model-check. We present TLA-Prover, a 20-billion-parameter model for TLA+ specification synthesis. Training combines supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on verified examples with repair-based group-relative policy optimization (GRPO). In the GRPO stage, the model learns to fix its own rejected specifications. We also train a direct preference optimization (DPO) variant from the same SFT checkpoint as an ablation. TLC provides the reward signal directly, with no learned reward model. Four tiers grade each output: Bronze (parses), Silver (no warnings), Gold (passes TLC), and Diamond. To reach Diamond, the model's correctness property is automatically altered in a small way; TLC must then detect a violation. If TLC still passes, the property was always-true and contributes nothing; the output fails Diamond. TLA-Prover reaches 9/30 (i.e. pass@1 = 30%) at both Gold and Diamond on a held-out 30-problem benchmark. This is roughly 3.5x the 8.6% untuned baseline. The DPO variant reaches 20% at Diamond. Gold and Diamond coincide at every checkpoint; this prevents the trivial-property failure mode.

</details>


### [69] [Towards the Readability of LLM-Generated Codes through Multitask Representation Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06214)
*Huifan Gao,Liuhua He,Yinghui Pan,Shenbao Yu,Yifeng Zeng,Shengchao Qin,Weidi Sun*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出多任务表示工程（RepE）框架，在低数据依赖和低计算成本下提升LLM生成代码的可读性，同时平衡代码正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注提升LLM生成代码的正确性，可读性这一关键质量维度被忽视。由于可读性的主观性，针对性控制具有挑战性。

Method: 采用表示工程（RepE）作为目标控制方法，提出多任务RepE框架，从理论上讨论了多任务引导方法对代码可读性与正确性之间权衡的影响。

Result: 通过综合实验验证了多任务RepE框架在提升代码可读性同时平衡正确性的有效性。

Conclusion: 多任务RepE能以低数据依赖和低计算成本实现代码可读性的定向提升，为解决代码质量中的主观维度控制提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Correctness and readability are key measures of code quality, respectively ensuring functional fidelity and ease of comprehension. While most existing research focuses on improving the correctness of large language models~(LLMs) generated codes, readability remains under-addressed. Enhancing readability through targeted control is challenging due to its subjective nature. In this article, we employ representation engineering~(RepE) as the targeted control method given its characteristics of low data dependency and low computational cost. Prior work on RepE has primarily focused on the targeted control for a single task, but improving the code readability requires the control across multiple tasks. Accordingly we proposes the multitask RepE framework and theoretically discuss the impact of the multitask steering method on the tradeoff between the code readability and correctness. We further provide comprehensive experiments in support. All the relevant implementations are open-source and available upon request.

</details>


### [70] [Domain Diversity, Motivation, Inclusion, and Feedback in Software Modelling Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06275)
*Isabella Graßl,Christopher Lazik,Shalini Chakraborty,Grischa Liebel,Miguel Goulão*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过90名学生和22名教师的平行调查，发现学生在软件建模教育中对社会相关领域最感兴趣并偏好自主选择，而教师高估了学生对学习相关领域的兴趣，揭示了问题领域选择对动机、参与度和包容性的重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究集中于建模工具、符号和评估，但问题领域的选择及其多样性、相关性和文化视角如何塑造学生学习体验尚未得到充分关注。

Method: 对90名学生和22名教师进行平行问卷调查，对比分析学生偏好与教师假设之间的差异。

Result: 学生对与社会相关的领域动机最强并偏好选择权；教师高估了学生对学习相关领域的兴趣；细微的设计选择可能导致学生被排斥；学生认为只有当反馈被明显采纳时才是有意义的。

Conclusion: 包容性的领域选择是学生动机的核心，建议采用以学生为中心的领域选择方法。

Abstract: Student engagement is critical for effective learning in software modelling, yet fostering motivation and inclusivity remains a challenge. While existing research has focused on modelling tools, notations, and assessment, little attention has been given to how the choice of problem domains and the diversity, relatability, and cultural perspectives they bring shape students' learning experiences. This study explores how problem domains and teaching methods influence motivation, engagement, inclusiveness, and feedback in modelling education. To investigate these dimensions, we conducted parallel surveys with 90 students and 22 educators. Our findings reveal disconnects between educator assumptions and student preferences: Students show greatest motivation for socially relevant domains and prefer choice in selection, while educators overestimate interest in study-related domains. The study identifies how minor design choices can exclude students. Students perceive feedback as meaningful when visibly acted upon. These findings suggest inclusive domain selection is central to student motivation; thus, we recommend student-centred domain selection.

</details>


### [71] [More than a Judge: An Empirical Study of Agent-Human Interaction in Crowdsourced Testing Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06301)
*Yue Wang,Yuan Zhao,Shengcheng Yu,Zhenyu Chen,Qing Gu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过受控人类实验（20名测试人员，3个真实应用），验证了基于多智能体评估系统生成的反馈不仅能评估众包测试报告质量（文本性、充分性、竞争性），还能作为工作流中的反馈提供者，显著提升测试人员修订报告质量、后续任务表现及跨应用迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作证明多智能体评估系统能可靠地自动评判众包测试报告，但尚不清楚智能体输出嵌入工作流后能否实际改善人类工作。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究评估驱动的可操作反馈能否提升测试人员的报告修订、后续任务表现及跨应用实践迁移。

Method: 基于LLM-as-a-Judge范式的多智能体评估框架，从文本性、充分性、竞争性三个维度评估报告；设计四阶段受控人类实验（20名测试人员，3个真实应用），通过比较反馈前后的报告质量、新任务首次提交质量及跨应用迁移效果进行验证。

Result: 智能体生成的反馈支持：1）修订报告的即时改进；2）接触反馈后在新任务上的首次提交质量提升；3）部分但有意义的跨应用迁移效果。17名参与者的问卷显示反馈总体可理解、被用于修订并延续至后续任务，但存在特异性和执行力方面的剩余摩擦。

Conclusion: 在众包测试场景中，评估智能体不仅可作为事后评判者，还可作为工作流集成的反馈提供者，支持上游报告质量改进，为智能体AI在软件工程工作流中的实际价值提供了实证证据。

Abstract: Agentic AI is increasingly being integrated into software engineering workflows. In crowdsourced testing, however, the large volume and uneven quality of submitted reports still create a substantial review burden for developers. In prior work, we developed and validated a multi-agent assessment backbone based on the LLM-as-a-Judge paradigm. That backbone assesses reports along three dimensions--textuality, adequacy, and competitiveness--and was shown to align well with human consensus while substantially reducing assessment effort. Yet reliable automated judging does not by itself show whether agent outputs can improve human work when embedded into workflow. This paper studies that missing question in the context of crowdsourced testing. We investigate whether assessment-derived, actionable feedback can improve how testers revise reports, perform on later tasks, and transfer reporting practices across applications. To do so, we conducted a controlled four-stage human-subject study with 20 testers across three real-world applications. The results show that agent-generated feedback supports immediate improvements in revised reports, better first submissions on a new task after prior feedback exposure, and evidence of partial but meaningful transfer to a later application. A post-task questionnaire completed by 17 participants complements these artifact-based findings by suggesting that the feedback was generally understandable, acted upon in revision, and carried into later tasks, while also revealing remaining friction in specificity and execution. Overall, the study provides empirical evidence that, in the studied crowdsourced testing setting, assessment agents can serve not only as post-hoc judges but also as workflow-integrated feedback providers that support upstream report-quality improvement.

</details>


### [72] [From Failed Trajectories to Reliable LLM Agents: Diagnosing and Repairing Harness Flaws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06324)
*Mengzhuo Chen,Junjie Wang,Zhe Liu,Yawen Wang,Qing Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: HarnessFix是一种基于跟踪引导的框架，通过将执行轨迹和代码编译为中间表示(HTIR)，归因故障到具体步骤和层，生成并验证补丁，在多个基准上将初始系统性能提升15.2%-50.0%，超越人类设计和自我进化基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自改进智能体和自动框架进化方法主要依赖运行时监督、提示优化、工作流搜索或基于最终结果的框架修改，但无法诊断失败轨迹中责任证据的位置以及哪个框架层导致了不可靠行为，导致修改宽泛、间接或范围不当。

Method: HarnessFix包含三阶段：(1)将原始执行轨迹和框架代码编译为HTIR，归一化碎片化轨迹证据并捕获步骤级溯源和控制流关系；(2)将故障归因到责任轨迹步骤和框架层，将重复诊断合并为可操作的缺陷记录，并映射到范围化修复算子；(3)在缺陷特定的修复规范下生成并验证框架补丁，以减少目标缺陷且不引入不可接受的回归。

Result: 在SWE-Bench Verified、Terminal-Bench 2.0 Verified、GAIA和AppWorld基准上，HarnessFix将初始框架的测试性能提升15.2%-50.0%，优于人类设计的基线和自我进化基线，并揭示了跨ETCLOVG层的重复框架缺陷模式。

Conclusion: HarnessFix通过细粒度的故障归因和范围化的修复机制，有效解决了现有方法无法精准定位失败原因的问题，为智能体框架的自动诊断与修复提供了一种系统化的解决方案。

Abstract: LLM-based agents increasingly rely on harnesses that provide execution environments, tool interfaces, context, lifecycle orchestration, observability, verification, and governance. Existing self-improving agents and automatic harness evolution methods mainly improve agents through runtime supervision, prompt optimization, workflow search, or harness modification based on final outcomes. However, they often fail to diagnose where the responsible evidence lies in failed trajectories and which harness layer causes the unreliable behavior, resulting in broad, indirect, or poorly scoped changes. This paper proposes HarnessFix, a trace-guided framework for diagnosing agent failures and repairing agent harnesses. HarnessFix compiles raw execution traces and harness code into a Harness-aware Trace Intermediate Representation (HTIR), which normalizes fragmented trajectory evidence and captures step-level provenance and control-flow relations. It then attributes failures to responsible trajectory steps and harness layers, consolidates recurring diagnoses into actionable flaw records, and maps them to scoped repair operators. Finally, HarnessFix generates and validates harness patches under flaw-specific repair specifications to reduce target flaws without introducing unacceptable regressions. We evaluate HarnessFix on SWE-Bench Verified, Terminal-Bench 2.0 Verified, GAIA and AppWorld. Across these benchmarks, HarnessFix improves held-out test performance over the initial harnesses by 15.2%--50.0%, outperforms human-designed and self-evolution baselines, and reveals recurring harness-flaw patterns across ETCLOVG layers.

</details>


### [73] [Scaffold, Not Vocabulary? A Controlled, Two-Tier, Pre-Registered Study of a Popperian Code-Generation Skill](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06454)
*Mehmet Iscan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过消融实验发现，提示词中Popperian证伪主义技能对代码生成的改进效果并非来自其方法论内容，而是来自提示词本身的结构框架。在前沿模型上所有条件无显著差异（天花板效应），在小模型上完整技能与仅保留标签的框架无显著区别，对照安慰剂仅差2.4个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在检验LLM提示词中流行的"Popperian证伪主义者"技能是否真正因Popperian方法论内容带来代码生成改进，还是仅因提示词的结构性框架（任何支架都会产生增益）。

Method: 采用两层消融实验设计：长度匹配的安慰剂对照、仅保留Popperian标签但剥离具体步骤的标签框架对照，搭配执行Oracle（HumanEval+单元测试）作为客观评估指标，同时设置词汇光环哨兵和同模型自评审计。

Result: 前沿模型（Claude Sonnet 4.6, N=163）上所有条件接近基准天花板，未分离；小模型（Qwen2.5-Coder-0.5B, N=164）上有结构框架的条件提升20-22个百分点，但完整技能与仅标签框架无显著差异（F@8=L@8 vs V@8=34.8%），安慰剂仅落后2.4个百分点。0.5B自评模型应用Popperian评分不优于随机选择，60%选择集中于同一索引。

Conclusion: Popperian技能的程序性内容在测试场景中未带来超越仅标签框架的执行正确性增益，增益源于支架结构本身。本文贡献了校准后的负结果和可复用的消融协议，结论限定了特定提示技能族的工程主张，并非对Popperian方法论本身的评价。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly write, review, and judge code, and a fast-growing practice equips them with prompt 'skills' that ask the model to reason like a scientist. A prominent example tells the model to act as a Popperian falsificationist, and such skills are reported to improve generated code. But these gains are almost always read off an LLM-as-a-judge, an instrument with documented positional, self-preference, and stylistic biases. We ask: if it appears to help, is the gain from the skill's Popperian content, or from the structure any scaffold imposes? We pre-register a two-tier ablation with three controls: a length-matched placebo, a labels-only scaffold that keeps the Popperian headers but strips the procedure, and an execution oracle (HumanEval+ unit tests), plus a vocabulary-halo sentinel and a same-model self-judge audit. On a frontier model (Claude Sonnet 4.6, N=163) all conditions sit near the benchmark ceiling and do not separate, so the pre-registered +5-point improvement is not supported (a ceiling-limited non-detection). On a small model (Qwen2.5-Coder-0.5B, N=164) structured arms lift best-of-eight correctness by 20-22 points, but the full skill shows no separable benefit over a labels-only scaffold (aggregate F@8=L@8 vs V@8=34.8%), and the placebo trails by only 2.4 points. A 0.5B self-judge applying the Popperian rubric does not beat random selection and concentrates 60% of its picks on one index. In the two settings tested, the skill's Popperian procedural content adds no separable execution-correctness benefit beyond a labels-only scaffold, so the gains track scaffold structure. We contribute a calibrated negative result and a reusable disambiguation protocol; the finding bounds an engineering claim about one prompt-skill family and is not an evaluation of Popperian methodology in general.

</details>


### [74] [Code2LoRA: Hypernetwork-Generated Adapters for Code Language Models under Software Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06492)
*Liliana Hotsko,Yinxi Li,Yuntian Deng,Pengyu Nie*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Code2LoRA是一个超网络框架，通过生成仓库特定的LoRA适配器来注入仓库级代码知识，无需推理时的token开销。支持静态（单快照）和演化（按diff更新）两种场景，在包含604个Python仓库的RepoPeftBench基准上，静态任务达到63.8%跨仓库和66.2%仓库内精确匹配，演化任务达到60.3%跨仓库精确匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的代码语言模型需要仓库级上下文来解决导入、API调用和项目约定等问题。现有方法（通过RAG或依赖分析注入长文本输入，或通过逐仓库微调和LoRA）在仓库规模扩展时成本高昂，且对演化的代码库脆弱。作者希望提出一种既能注入仓库知识、又无需推理时额外token开销的方法。

Method: Code2LoRA是一个超网络（hypernetwork）框架，能够生成仓库特定的LoRA适配器。支持两种使用场景：1) Code2LoRA-Static：将单个仓库快照转换为适配器，适用于稳定代码库的理解；2) Code2LoRA-Evo：维护一个由GRU隐藏状态支持的适配器，该隐藏状态按代码diff更新，适用于活跃开发的演化代码库。基准方面，构建了RepoPeftBench，包含604个Python仓库，分为静态轨道（40K训练/12K测试断言补全任务）和演化轨道（215K训练/87K测试提交衍生的任务）。

Result: 静态轨道上，Code2LoRA-Static达到63.8%跨仓库精确匹配和66.2%仓库内精确匹配，与逐仓库LoRA上界持平；演化轨道上，Code2LoRA-Evo达到60.3%跨仓库精确匹配，比单一共享LoRA高出5.2个百分点。

Conclusion: Code2LoRA能够有效注入仓库知识，在推理时无额外token开销，且支持静态和演化两种代码库场景。实验表明其性能与逐仓库LoRA上界相当，在演化场景中显著优于共享LoRA基线。代码、模型和数据集均已开源。

Abstract: Code language models need repository-level context to resolve imports, APIs, and project conventions. Existing methods inject this knowledge as long inputs (retrieved through RAG or dependency analysis) or through per-repository fine-tuning and LoRA -- costly at repository scale and brittle to evolving codebases. We introduce Code2LoRA, a hypernetwork framework that generates repository-specific LoRA adapters, effectively injecting repository knowledge with zero inference-time token overhead. Code2LoRA supports two usage scenarios: Code2LoRA-Static converts a single repository snapshot into an adapter, suitable for comprehension of stable codebases; while Code2LoRA-Evo maintains an adapter backed by a GRU hidden state updated per code diff, suitable for active development of evolving codebases. To evaluate Code2LoRA against parameter-efficient fine-tuning baselines, we build RepoPeftBench, a benchmark of 604 Python repositories with two tracks: a static track with 40K training and 12K test assertion-completion tasks, and an evolution track with 215K commit-derived training and 87K commit-derived test tasks. On the static track, Code2LoRA-Static achieves 63.8% cross-repo and 66.2% in-repo exact match, matching the per-repository LoRA upper bound; on the evolution track, Code2LoRA-Evo achieves 60.3% cross-repo exact match (+5.2 pp over a single shared LoRA). Code2LoRA's code can be found at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/code2lora-6857; the model checkpoints and RepoPeftBench datasets can be found at https://huggingface.co/code2lora.

</details>

### [75] [The Evaluation Blind Spot: A Stereological Theory of Benchmark Coverage for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05169)
*Jason Z Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LLM基准覆盖的体视学理论，揭示了基准套件的有效维度(2.86-4.80)，发现结构盲点比跑分差距大两个数量级，并证明了通过子模贪心算法可在4个基准上实现稳定覆盖、7个基准达到90%覆盖率。同时独立解决了Gardner Problem 1.5，建立了C^2支撑函数在一般维度下的极小极大率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM基准评测缺乏对基准套件覆盖能力的理论理解，无法量化评测盲区的大小，也难以判断哪些基准是冗余的、哪些是必不可少的。作者希望建立一套严格的体视学理论来刻画基准对能力空间的覆盖程度。

Method: 采用体视学（stereological）理论框架，引入有效维度d_eff概念，用可见Hausdorff距离刻画能力轮廓间的差异界。实证上对三个独立排行榜进行有效维度估计，并通过卡方投影模型、贝叶斯先验模拟、子模贪心算法（含Nemhauser (1-1/e)保证）以及反事实验证等方法进行验证。同时通过最优恢复理论在S^(D-1)上解决了Gardner问题1.5。

Result: 三个独立排行榜的有效维度在[2.86, 4.80]区间；结构盲点比分差大两个数量级、是统计噪声的52-127倍；模拟中前两名互换率稳定在[0.38,0.49]；500次随机分割中92%会改变第一名排序，平均2.83/5个前五模型变动；4个基准构成稳定核心，7/12可达90%覆盖率，跨时间迁移保持93-97%保留率；反事实验证r=-0.69（移除)和r=+0.38（新信息）；Gardner问题的极小极大率为Θ(R/(κ m^(2/(D-1))))。

Conclusion: LLM基准套件存在巨大的结构盲区，当前排行榜的排序具有高度不确定性；通过有效维度分析和子模选择可以显著减少冗余、提高评测效率；体视学理论可预测哪些评测不可替代、哪些外部评测带来新信息。此外独立的理论贡献解决了Gardner的开放问题。

Abstract: We give a stereological theory of LLM benchmark coverage. For any suite with effective dimensionality d_eff, the visible Hausdorff distance between two convex capability profiles consistent with the same scores is bounded by epsilon + C R m^(-1/(d_eff-1)), with matching Lipschitz lower bound. Empirically, three independent leaderboards (Open LLM v2, an extended 12-benchmark suite, LiveBench) all have d_eff in [2.86, 4.80] on their competitive frontier; the structural blind spot exceeds the observed runner-up score gap by two orders of magnitude and dominates statistical noise by 52-127x. Under a chi-squared projection model, the isotropic prior is the optimistic case; across six hidden-capability priors and four ambient dimensions the simulated half-split swap rate of the top two models stays in [0.38, 0.49], and a 500-trial random visible/held-out split shows that 92% of trials swap the top-1 ranking with on average 2.83 of 5 top-5 models changing. A submodular greedy algorithm with the Nemhauser (1 - 1/e) guarantee finds a stable core of 4 benchmarks; 7 of 12 suffice for 90% coverage, and the trained subset transfers across temporal quarters with 93-97% retention. A counterfactual validation across 12 internal benchmarks and 27 Chatbot Arena categories confirms that the eigenstructure predicts which evaluations are irreplaceable (rho = -0.69, p = 0.013 for removal disruption) and which external evaluations bring new information (rho = +0.38). As a second, independent theoretical contribution, we resolve Gardner's Problem 1.5 (1995) for C^2 support functions, establishing the minimax rate Theta(R/(kappa m^(2/(D-1)))) in general dimension via optimal recovery theory on S^(D-1).

</details>


### [76] [ERRORQUAKE: Heavy-Tailed Error Severity Distributions in Open-Weight Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05170)
*Jason Z Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Errorquake-10k基准测试，对21个开放权重LLM的错误严重性分布进行连续评分（0-4级），发现即使准确率匹配，不同模型的错误严重性分布形态存在显著差异。通过统计检验、人类验证、非冗余性定理证明和严重性机制分类，论证了严重性分布与错误率携带相互不可约的信息，应作为独立指标报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉基准将所有错误等同对待（仅报告标量错误率），但实际中"错误日期"与"捏造法庭裁决"的严重性相差数个数量级。为揭示准确率背后模型在错误严重性分布上的结构性差异，需要引入连续严重性评分体系。

Method: 构建Errorquake-10k（10,000查询，8领域5难度层级，0-4严重性连续评分）；拟合21个开放权重模型的严重性分布，估计Gutenberg-Richter上尾斜率b（含95%自举置信区间）；进行519项三人人类验证研究（ICC(2,k=3)=0.85），验证LLM评判排名（rho=0.89）及稠密模型规模相关性（rho_s=-0.86）；证明非冗余性定理；建立严重性机制分类（kappa=0.83）。

Result: 210个模型对中，85对在匹配准确率（|Δε|<0.05）下b的95%置信区间不重叠；非冗余性定理表明严重性分布与错误率信息非冗余（I(b;模型|ε)=1.56比特，64.5%的跨模型b方差无法被ε解释）；低严重性错误71%为检索错误，高严重性错误39%为捏造，且此构成随模型规模显著变化（p<0.0001）。

Conclusion: 严重性分布应作为准确率的补充指标报告，它携带错误率无法捕捉的判别性信息。

Abstract: At matched accuracy, open-weight LLMs differ substantially in the shape of their error severity distribution -- a difference invisible to the scalar error rate. Hallucination benchmarks report a single error count and treat all errors as equivalent, yet a wrong date and a fabricated court ruling differ by orders of magnitude. We introduce Errorquake-10k, a 10,000-query benchmark scoring each response on a continuous 0-4 severity scale across 8 domains and 5 difficulty tiers, and we fit per-model severity distributions for 21 open-weight models. For each model we estimate a severity distribution index (b, the Gutenberg-Richter upper-tail slope) with 95% bootstrap confidence intervals. Headline: across the 210 model pairs, 85 have disjoint 95% b confidence intervals at matched accuracy (|Delta epsilon| < 0.05) on human-consensus scoring, e.g. deepseek-v3.2 vs. ministral-14b at epsilon = 0.586 and Delta b = 0.47. A 519-item three-rater human validation study confirms measurement reliability (ICC(2,k=3) = 0.85), validates the LLM-judge ranking (rho = 0.89), and confirms the dense-model scaling correlation on human data (rho_s = -0.86). We prove a Non-Reducibility Theorem showing that severity profile and error rate are informationally non-redundant (I(b; model | epsilon) = 1.56 bits; 64.5% of cross-model b variance is unexplained by epsilon). A severity mechanism taxonomy (kappa = 0.83) reveals that error type shifts categorically with severity: low-severity errors are retrievals (71%); high-severity errors are fabrications (39%) -- and this composition differs by model size (p < 0.0001). Severity distribution should be reported alongside accuracy; it carries discriminative information that the error rate cannot.

</details>


### [77] [From Prediction to Self: Developmental Conditions for Agency in Minimal Neural Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05605)
*Evan Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过40个受控实验，追踪了一个192维GRU系统从纯预测到区分自我因果与世界因果的发展路径，发现了四个必须严格顺序满足的条件，并提出了“能动性增益”指标来量化这一过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究一个仅能预测世界的系统如何逐步发展出区分自身因果影响与外部世界因果影响的能力，即自我表征的产生机制。

Method: 通过40个受控实验构成的发育序列，逐步添加组件，追踪192维GRU能否区分自我引起的变化与世界引起的变化，并使用能动性增益（A = Err_world - Err_self）作为度量指标。

Result: 发现了四个必须严格顺序满足的条件：持久状态形成稳定吸引子、因果动作循环、本体感觉反馈使隐性因果知识显性化、异步觉醒（感知学习先于动作学习）。自感知预测器在周期性和混沌环境中均优于自盲预测器。前向采样动作选择产生有意义的能动性增益，而梯度方法则退化。同时识别出12个被证伪的假设。

Conclusion: 自我表征的产生需要特定的发育顺序条件，能动性增益可作为量化自我意识发展的有效指标，纯预测编码不足以产生自我表征。

Abstract: How does a system that merely predicts the world come to distinguish its own causal influence from everything else? We trace this transition in a minimal 192-dimensional GRU through 40 controlled experiments arranged as a developmental sequence, adding components one at a time and tracking whether the system can distinguish self-caused from world-caused changes.
  The developmental path reveals four conditions that must be satisfied in strict order: (1) persistent state forming stable attractors, (2) a causal action loop linking output to input, (3) proprioceptive feedback that makes implicit causal knowledge explicit, and (4) asynchronous awakening - perceptual learning must consolidate before action learning begins. We propose agency gain (A = Err_world - Err_self), the predictive advantage of knowing one's own action, as a metric to track this process. The self-aware predictor consistently outperforms the self-blind predictor across periodic (sinusoidal) and chaotic (Lorenz) environments, and the metric survives ablation of all auxiliary components. Only forward-sampled action selection produces meaningful agency gain; two gradient-based alternatives degenerate.
  Equally significant are 12 falsified hypotheses mapping where development stalls: predictive coding alone does not produce self-represent

</details>


### [78] [Staged Factorial Screening for Budget-Constrained Micro-Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05186)
*Felipe Chavarro Polania*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了预算受限的微预训练场景下，采用分阶段分式析因工作流来识别稳定早期效应结构的方法。通过613次实验（包括不同时间预算的预筛选和跟进筛选、多次重复运行、锚点检查、基线对比、桥接实验及多平台长时间锚点延续），发现批次大小、深度和宽度等因子在短预算下惩罚最大，随预算增加而缓解。部分因子在矫正后保持显著，随机搜索能找到强候选但缺乏归因能力。最终提出基于桥接模型的有界方法建议，强调硬件敏感性而非通用超参数优化优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在预算有限的微预训练中，需要在共享加速器上对大量候选配方进行筛选，然后才投入更大搜索预算。目前缺乏一种能够在该场景下稳定恢复早期效应结构的工作流方法。

Method: 采用分阶段分式析因工作流，在固定单GPU训练循环上运行613次实验，包括2/5/10分钟预筛选和跟进筛选、5/10分钟完整16条件种子重复运行、靶向锚点检查、同主机贪心和匹配成本随机基线、60分钟桥接包，以及Windows A100和Linux L40S上24小时内的有界锚点延续。

Result: 总批次、深度和宽度在短预算下惩罚最大且随预算增加而缓解；预声明完整筛选族中D/A/B/C在5和10分钟经Benjamini-Hochberg矫正后保持非零估计，E则不然；随机搜索可触及强候选但重复在低惩罚区域且缺乏因子归因；60分钟桥接锚点均值最低但无法分离工作流优化与模型容量优势；12/24小时三锚点延续中桥接仍最低，非桥接排序依赖主机。

Conclusion: 提出有界方法建议：使用短设计屏幕识别高惩罚方向，通过重复运行确认有前景的锚点，在缩减空间内局部优化。证据支持24小时内桥接中心推荐，但非硬件无关排名或通用超参数优化优越性。

Abstract: Budget-constrained micro-pretraining often requires triaging many candidate recipes on a shared accelerator before larger search budgets are spent. We study whether a staged fractional-factorial workflow can recover stable early effect structure in this setting. On a fixed autoresearch-derived single-GPU training loop, we run 613 experiments across pilot and follow-up screens at 2, 5, and 10 minutes; full 16-condition seeded reruns at 5 and 10 minutes; targeted seeded anchor checks; same-host greedy and matched-cost random baselines; a 60-minute bridge package; and bounded Windows A100 and Linux L40S anchor continuations through 24 hours. Main penalties from total batch, depth, and width are largest at short budgets and relax as budget increases. Within the predeclared seeded full-screen families, D, A, B, and C retain non-zero estimates at 5 and 10 minutes after within-budget Benjamini-Hochberg correction, while E does not. Random search can reach strong incumbents in this 32-condition space, but repeatedly in the same low-penalty region and without factor attribution. The 60-minute bridge anchor has the lowest mean, although that package does not separate workflow refinement from the larger bridge model's capacity advantage. In bounded 12-hour and 24-hour three-anchor continuations on both hosts, the bridge has the lowest sample mean while the non-bridge ordering stays host-sensitive. We therefore present a bounded methods result: use short designed screens to identify high-penalty directions, confirm promising anchors under repeated runs, and refine locally inside the reduced space. The evidence supports a bridge-centered recommendation through 24 hours on two hosts, not hardware-invariant ranking or general hyperparameter-optimization superiority.

</details>


### [79] [Adaptive Oscillatory-State Alignment for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06010)
*Zhangyao Song,Ziqiong Li,Xiangfei Qiu,Chao Zha,Yinfei Xu,Tao Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AOSNET框架，通过希尔伯特变换提取解析信号描述符，将周期预测从固定模板匹配转变为自适应振荡状态对齐，在8个基准上取得SOTA或竞争性结果，且对非平稳性鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实时间序列的振荡行为存在幅度调制、相位漂移和局部频率变化，固定模板的周期建模与底层时间状态存在根本性不匹配，需要更灵活的自适应周期建模方法。

Method: 利用希尔伯特变换从观测序列和可学习的全局振荡先验中提取解析信号描述符，通过描述符条件门控自适应对齐局部状态，选择性保留可靠观测并软校正不匹配区域。

Result: 在8个基准数据集上达到SOTA或高度竞争性的预测精度，且推理速度快。合成实验表明，随着非平稳性增强，振荡状态对齐的优势持续增加。

Conclusion: AOSNET通过振荡状态对齐替代固定模板匹配，有效处理了真实世界时间序列中非平稳周期模式，验证了该范式在周期预测中的优越性。

Abstract: Long-term time series forecasting benefits from inductive biases that expose recurring temporal structure. Existing periodic forecasting methods typically model recurrence through predefined periods, global spectral components, or fixed learnable templates. However, real-world temporal dynamics are rarely rigidly periodic: oscillatory behavior often evolves through amplitude modulation, phase drift, and local frequency variation. Under these conditions, fixed-template periodic modeling can become fundamentally mismatched to the underlying temporal states. We propose AOSNET, a Hilbert-guided forecasting framework that reformulates periodic forecasting from fixed template matching to adaptive oscillatory-state alignment. AOSNET extracts analytic-signal descriptors from both the observed sequence and a learnable global oscillatory prior, then adaptively aligns local states through a descriptor-conditioned gate that selectively preserves reliable observations while softly correcting mismatched regions. The learned prior serves not as a rigid repeated template but as a flexible oscillatory reference interpreted through local state dynamics. Experiments on eight benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art or highly competitive accuracy with fast inference speed. Controlled synthetic studies isolating amplitude modulation, phase drift, and local frequency variation confirm that the advantage of oscillatory-state alignment consistently increases as non-stationarity intensifies.

</details>


### [80] [PyCC.id: A package for hypothesis-driven equation discovery with structural identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05191)
*Federico J. Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PyCC，一个基于特征曲线（CC）骨架假设驱动方法的Python库，用于从时间序列数据中发现常微分方程，该库通过整合领域知识来缓解逆问题的不适定性，并支持多种方程发现范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动方程发现本质上是病态逆问题，常产生多个拟合效果相当的数学模型。现有方法虽通过假设约束缩小搜索空间，但仍需人工后验筛选。本文旨在提供一个灵活的工具，将基于特征曲线骨架的假设驱动方法论系统化。

Method: 提出PyCC库，基于特征曲线（CC）骨架的假设驱动方法。用户定义与ODE族关联的骨架结构，并基于领域知识添加假设和先验以迭代优化模型。该库具有模块化特性，支持神经网络、符号回归和稀疏回归等多种方程发现范式。

Result: 开发了PyCC Python库，具有结构可辨识性验证能力，能高效整合领域假设，支持多种方程发现范式，为从时间序列数据中发现ODE提供灵活工具。

Conclusion: PyCC是一个有效的工具，通过结构化骨架和领域知识融合，解决了方程发现中的病态逆问题，其模块化设计使其适用于多种方程发现场景。

Abstract: Data-driven equation discovery is fundamentally an inverse problem that seeks to infer the governing differential equations of a system directly from time-series measurements. A known issue is the ill-conditioned nature of the inverse problem, which frequently produces multiple mathematical models that fit the data similarly well. One path to address this issue is by incorporating known hypotheses and constraints into the training phase beforehand. While this approach effectively reduces the search space, it still results in multiple candidate models, forcing practitioners to rely on post-hoc manual filtering based on their own domain expertise. A recent approach incorporates structural `skeletons' inspired by characteristic curves (CCs), defining a hypothesis-driven methodology. In this methodology, practitioners define a skeleton, which is associated with a family of ordinary differential equations (ODEs), and then add their hypotheses and priors based on their domain knowledge to refine the obtained model iteratively. An important advantage of this approach is that some skeletons have demonstrable structural identifiability properties, which are useful for checking whether the skeleton is correct or should be discarded. Furthermore, this formalism enables the use of multiple equation discovery paradigms due to its modularity (such as neural networks, symbolic regression, and sparse regression). In this work, we present the Python library PyCC, which condenses these efforts into a flexible tool that allows researchers and engineers to seamlessly define their skeletons and hypotheses to discover ODEs from time-dependent data.

</details>


### [81] [Double Preconditioning (DoPr): Optimization for Test-Time Performance, not Validation Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06418)
*Thomas T. Zhang,Alok Shah,Yifei Zhang,Vincent Zhang,Nikolai Matni,Max Simchowitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为双预条件（DoPr）的优化范式，针对深度学习中训练和部署目标不一致导致的测试时反馈（TTF）问题，通过结合梯度预条件和激活预条件来缓解误差累积，在多种TTF场景中提升下游性能，且该提升不一定伴随验证损失的改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在深度学习的许多现代应用中，模型通过单步预测损失（如L²回归、交叉熵）训练，但在部署时沿着自身预测进行自回归滚动，导致训练/验证损失与下游指标（如任务成功率、生成质量）之间存在显著 mismatch，且随任务长度增长而加剧。现有方法主要从数据、架构和目标函数角度缓解该问题，本文提出从优化视角出发进行干预。

Method: 提出双预条件（DoPr）优化范式，将梯度预条件（如Adam、Muon中的做法）与激活预条件（如KFAC中的做法）相结合，专门针对TTF场景中的误差累积问题进行优化。

Result: DoPr在多种TTF设置（自回归语言建模、流式生成建模、机器人策略学习）中均能提升下游模型性能，且这种提升并不一致地伴随验证损失的改善，揭示了现有评估方式的局限性。

Conclusion: 优化方法可作为缓解TTF问题的新设计维度；激活预条件的加入是一种有效的即插即用干预手段；验证损失的改善与否不能可靠预测TTF场景下的下游性能，需重新思考评估标准。

Abstract: Many modern applications of deep learning involve training a neural network via a one-step prediction loss (e.g., $L^2$ regression, cross-entropy), but deploy the network by rolling out along its own predictions. Key examples include autoregressive language modeling, flow-based generative modeling, and robot policy learning. It is well-documented that these settings induce a phenomenon we call test-time feedback (TTF): the mismatch between the training/validation loss and downstream metrics of interest, such as task success rate and generation quality, which grows with task length. While data curation, architecture, and objective design have been proposed to combat train-test shift in TTF settings, this paper proposes optimization as a new design axis to mitigate error accumulation. Specifically, we introduce a new optimization paradigm called double-preconditioning (DoPr) uniquely tailored to the challenges of TTF. DoPr combines gradient-wise preconditioning, as in Adam and Muon, with activation-wise preconditioning (AP), such as in KFAC. We show that the addition of AP yields a drop-in intervention for increasing downstream model performance across a range of TTF settings. Interestingly, these gains in test-time performance do not consistently accompany improvements in validation loss, opening new questions about how to properly evaluate models trained with one-step supervised objectives.

</details>


### [82] [Temporal Preference Concepts and their Functions in a Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05194)
*Ian Rios-Sialer,Shantanu Darveshi,Shuai Jiang,Avigya Paudel,Anastasiia Pronina,Ipshita Bandyopadhyay,Justin Shenk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过因果定位方法，在蒸馏LLM中识别出负责时间偏好决策的神经子图，发现LLM对未来的折现比人类更平缓，且这种偏好不稳定，可通过引导向量进行调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在需要权衡短期收益与长期后果的决策场景中日益重要，但其内部如何表征和解决这种时间权衡尚不清楚，缺乏可解释性和可控性。

Method: 利用梯度归因和激活修补的汇聚证据，在蒸馏后的Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507模型中因果定位了时间偏好的底层子图；通过残差流分析时间跨度的几何编码；进行行为分析比较LLM与人类的时间折现特征；尝试通过引导向量调控时间偏好。

Result: 识别出中上层层的节点构成时间偏好子图；LLM对未来折现的陡度比人类低数倍；时间偏好跨上下文不稳定；引导向量能初步改变时间偏好。

Conclusion: 可解释性机制分析有助于实现对LLM规划与推理中时间偏好的可靠控制，应追求显式控制而非隐式依赖训练。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being deployed to make decisions that require trading off near-term gains against long-term consequences, yet little is known about how they internally represent or resolve these tradeoffs. In this work, we causally localize an underlying subgraph for temporal preference in a distilled LLM (Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507), identifying mid-to-upper-layer nodes through converging evidence from gradient-based attribution and activation patching. We find that the geometry of time horizon is encoded in the residual stream at the expected localized layers. A behavioral analysis reveals that unintervened LLMs discount the future several times less steeply than humans, yet this preference is unstable across contexts, motivating explicit control rather than implicit reliance on training. Finally, we find suggestive evidence that steering vectors can shift temporal preference. Our work demonstrates how mechanistic interpretability can bring us closer to reliable control over how LLMs plan and reason

</details>


### [83] [State commitment learning: training language models to distinguish computation from memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05201)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng,Huiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出状态承诺学习（State Commitment Learning）训练目标，通过反事实擦除强化学习（CERL）使语言模型区分持久状态与临时计算，在数学、长链逻辑、科学问答和多轮工具使用等任务中，显著减少答案对隐藏思想的依赖，同时保持准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前推理语言模型无法区分用于计算的令牌和构成持久状态的令牌，所有隐藏思考（包括失败的尝试、死胡同和私人草稿）都留在上下文中影响后续预测，导致下游推理可能依赖不应被可靠依赖的信息。

Method: 提出状态承诺学习（State Commitment Learning）训练目标，定义反事实标准——持久状态充分性（persistent-state sufficiency），使擦除隐藏思想后答案是否仍可用可训练可测量。提出反事实擦除强化学习（CERL），在相同前缀下同时评估保留隐藏思想和擦除隐藏思想两条路径，仅当擦除路径正确时给予奖励。引入擦除依赖协议（Erasure Dependence Protocol）。

Result: 在数学、长链逻辑、科学QA和多轮工具使用评估中，CERL显著减少了答案对隐藏思想的依赖，同时未牺牲准确性，一致优于仅正确性强化学习和长答案SFT基线。

Conclusion: 通过状态承诺学习和CERL，可以有效训练模型区分应被提交为持久状态的信息与可丢弃的临时计算，减少推理对隐藏思考的不当依赖，提升推理可靠性和安全性。

Abstract: Reasoning language models do not distinguish tokens used for computation from tokens that constitute persistent state: once generated, all hidden thoughts remain in context and influence future predictions. As a result, downstream reasoning may depend on failed attempts, dead ends, and private scratch work that should not be safely relied on later. We recast this phenomenon as a new training objective, state commitment learning: training models to explicitly distinguish information that should be committed as persistent state from temporary computation that can be discarded. We define a counterfactual criterion, persistent-state sufficiency, which makes it trainable and measurable whether an answer remains usable after hidden thoughts are erased. We then propose Counterfactual Erasure RL (CERL), which evaluates, under the same prefix, both a path that keeps hidden thoughts and a path that erases them, and gives reward only when the erasure path remains correct. We also introduce the Erasure Dependence Protocol and show across mathematics, long-chain logic, scientific QA, and multi-turn tool-use evaluation that CERL substantially reduces answer dependence on hidden thoughts without sacrificing accuracy, consistently outperforming correctness-only RL and long-answer SFT baselines.

</details>


### [84] [Gradient Descent with Large Step Size Restores Symmetry in Deep Linear Networks with Multi-Pathway](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05219)
*Hee-Sung Kim,Sungyoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度线性网络中，大步长离散梯度下降(GD)如何通过边缘稳定性驱动的震荡，推翻梯度流(GF)预测的“赢家通吃”单路径专业化，促使信号在多路径间重新平衡分布，从而偏好共享表征而非单路径主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究（基于梯度流）预测多路径深度线性网络会走向“赢家通吃”的专业化，但实际训练中离散梯度下降的行为与这一预测存在差异，需要重新审视大步长GD下的路径竞争机制。

Method: 理论证明了单路径解是尖锐最小值，而跨路径分布信号可降低尖锐度（降幅随路径数和深度增加）；通过分析边缘稳定性(Edge of Stability)阶段的震荡行为，揭示早期深度驱动的对称性破缺如何被后续震荡覆盖并进入重新平衡阶段。

Result: 大步长GD下，早期训练遵循GF预测的深度驱动对称性破缺，但随后边缘稳定性震荡会覆盖这一趋势，驱动信号在各路径间重新分布，形成多路径共享表征。

Conclusion: 深度塑造了路径竞争的方式，大步长GD倾向于共享表征而非持续的单路径主导，这解释了实践中深度网络为何偏好分布式而非集中式表征。

Abstract: Recent analyses of multi-pathway Deep Linear Networks use Gradient Flow to predict a "winner-takes-all" specialization in which path symmetry breaks and each feature concentrates in a single pathway. In this work, we show that discrete Gradient Descent (GD) with a large step size tells a different story. We prove that single-path solutions are sharp minima, whereas distributing signals across pathways reduces sharpness by a factor that decreases with both the number of pathways and depth. Consequently, while early training reproduces the depth-driven symmetry breaking predicted by GF, oscillations at the Edge of Stability subsequently override this tendency and drive the network into a re-balancing phase, where signals redistribute across pathways. Together, these results clarify how depth shapes pathway competition and explain why large-step GD favors shared representations rather than persistent single-pathway dominance.

</details>


### [85] [Differentiable Efficient Operator Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05232)
*Xiaohuan Pei,Jiyuan Zhang,Yuanfan Guo,Weiguo Feng,Tao Huang,Cho-Jui Hsieh,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出可微分的算子搜索框架(EOS)，将多种手工设计的token缩减算子统一到共享算子空间中，自动搜索缩减位置、保留数量和算子行为，在多模态基准上实现了竞争性的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态基础模型依赖手工设计的token缩减算子（剪枝、合并、池化、自适应重加权），这些算子看似不同但缺乏统一视角，且手工设计难以探索最优组合。

Method: 提出可微分的 Efficient Operator Search (EOS)框架，构建包含层激活、保留预算和算子行为的参数化搜索空间，通过单边预算和成本约束优化任务性能，联合搜索缩减位置、保留数量和算子行为。

Result: 在多种多模态基准上，搜索得到的算子在精度-效率权衡上具有竞争力，特别是在激进的视觉token缩减场景下表现优异；该方法能恢复代表性手工设计baseline，并能发现超越孤立手工设计的混合算子。

Conclusion: 高效多模态推理可以从手工算子设计转向可微分的算子搜索，该框架统一了多种现有算子并为自动化设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Efficient multimodal foundation models often rely on manually designed token-reduction operators, such as pruning, merging, pooling, and adaptive reweighting. Although these operators appear different, we show that they can be interpreted as distinct regimes of a shared operator space. Based on this view, we introduce Efficient Operator Search, a differentiable framework that jointly searches where to reduce tokens, how many tokens to retain, and how reduced token information should be processed. The proposed search space parameterizes layer activation, retention budget, and operator behavior, while the search policy optimizes task performance under one-sided budget and cost constraints. This formulation recovers representative hand-designed baselines as special cases and further discovers hybrid operators beyond isolated manual designs. Experiments on multimodal benchmarks show that the searched operators achieve competitive accuracy-efficiency trade-offs, especially under aggressive visual-token reduction. These results suggest that efficient multimodal inference can be reframed from manual operator design to differentiable operator search.

</details>


### [86] [DiffSlack: Learning under Nonlinear Inequality Constraints via Learnable Slack Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05247)
*Ziqian Wang,Chenxi Fang,Zhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffSlack，一种可微投影层，通过可学习的松弛变量将非线性不等式约束转化为等式，并利用阻尼高斯-牛顿投影将网络输出映射到可行流形上，在车辆路径规划中实现了更高的成功率和约束满足度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在神经网络中强制执行非线性不等式约束仍然具有挑战性，尤其是当输出受多个耦合约束时。现有硬约束方法要么对约束集施加结构限制，要么在大规模非线性问题中引入大量计算开销。

Method: DiffSlack将不等式约束转化为带有可学习松弛变量的等式约束，松弛变量作为网络增广输出的一部分进行预测并为阻尼高斯-牛顿投影提供数据驱动的暖启动。投影层将原始预测映射到增广可行流形上，同时保持端到端可微性。两阶段课程学习进一步稳定训练并改善约束满足。

Result: 在包含200个非线性不等式约束（碰撞避免、曲率限制、航点间距）的车辆路径规划任务中，DiffSlack相比现有学习基线方法实现了更高的规划成功率和更强的几何约束满足度，推理开销相当。消融实验表明硬投影层降低了对监督质量的敏感性。CARLA闭环跟踪和实车实验验证了生成轨迹的可执行性。

Conclusion: DiffSlack提供了一种实用且可扩展的方法，将硬不等式约束嵌入到面向工程应用的神经网络中。

Abstract: Enforcing nonlinear inequality constraints in neural networks remains challenging, especially when the output is subject to many coupled constraints.
