The final answer can only be verified, and the reward assigned, after the CoT trace is complete, making it a delayed reward problem. GRPO and its modifications correspond to Monte Carlo methods in standard RL, which are known to suffer from high variance. A possible solution to this problem is the redistribution of rewards through credit assignment, where segments of the CoT trace that are important for arriving at the desirable solution are emphasized by assigning a higher reward. While Monte Carlo sampling can be used to provide an unbiased estimate of intermediate state values, its computational overhead makes it unsuitable for train-time credit assignment in long contexts at high granularity. We introduce RREDCoT (Reward REDistribution for Chain of Thoughts), which utilizes the model itself to approximate the optimal reward redistribution without additional generation. We investigate the advantages of our method compared to MC sampling and several attribution methods. We further analyze several aspects relevant to the construction of the redistribution such as segmentation of CoT traces and state value estimation.

</details>


### [197] [Pretraining Recurrent Networks without Recurrence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06479)
*Akarsh Kumar,Phillip Isola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出监督记忆训练（SMT），通过将RNN训练转化为一步记忆转换标签的监督学习，实现时间并行训练且梯度路径长度为O(1），无需展开计算图，在语言建模和像素序列建模任务上优于BPTT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准BPTT在训练RNN时存在时间顺序执行导致并行性受限、长程梯度消失/爆炸等问题，难以有效学习长距离依赖。

Method: SMT通过Transformer编码器基于预测状态目标生成记忆标签(m_t, x_{t+1}) → m_{t+1}，将记忆内容（记住什么）与记忆更新（如何更新）解耦，实现时间并行训练，梯度路径在任何两个token之间恒为O(1)。

Result: 在语言建模和像素序列建模等预训练任务中，SMT训练的各类RNN架构性能优于BPTT，能更好地捕捉长程依赖关系并支持并行训练。

Conclusion: SMT为非线性RNN的训练提供了替代方案，有望推动构建时间抽象能力的模型规模化发展。

Abstract: Training recurrent neural networks (RNNs) requires assigning credit across long sequences of computations. Standard backpropagation through time (BPTT) addresses this problem poorly: it is sequential in time, limiting parallelism, and suffers from vanishing or exploding gradients, making long-range associations difficult to learn. We propose Supervised Memory Training (SMT), a method for training nonlinear RNNs that sidesteps recurrent credit propagation entirely by reducing RNN training to supervised learning on one-step memory transition labels $(m_t, x_{t+1}) \rightarrow m_{t+1}$. SMT acquires these memory labels by training a Transformer-based encoder on a predictive state objective--retaining only information from the past necessary to predict the future. By decoupling what to remember from how to update memory, SMT enables time-parallel RNN training with a stable $O(1)$ length gradient path between any two tokens--without ever unrolling the RNN. We find that SMT outperforms BPTT when pretraining various RNN architectures on tasks like language modeling and pixel sequence modeling. SMT enables nonlinear RNNs to better capture long-range dependencies and train in parallel, potentially unlocking the scaling of models that build temporal abstractions of past experience.

</details>


### [198] [Regret Minimization with Adaptive Opponents in Repeated Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06486)
*Mingyang Liu,Asuman Ozdaglar,Tiancheng Yu,Kaiqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于自适应对手的重复博弈新遗憾度量——Repeated Policy Regret (RP-Regret)，并研究了在非凸策略空间中实现次线性遗憾的必要条件和可行算法，证明了所有玩家最小化该遗憾时可学习到子博弈完美均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的外部遗憾(external regret)无法有效衡量重复博弈中自适应对手（能够基于历史响应）的对抗性行为，缺乏对玩家反事实推理(counterfactual reasoning)的刻画。现有遗憾概念在比较器强度和对手约束方面存在局限。

Method: 提出了RP-Regret这一博弈论指标，衡量实际累积效用与所有玩家都能基于历史响应的最佳事后累积效用之差。识别了实现次线性RP-Regret的必要条件（关于策略变分和记忆长度），并提出了三种算法：(i) 基于优化oracle的算法；(ii) 最小化凸线性化代理遗憾的算法；(iii) 在对手策略缓慢变化时直接最小化RP-Regret的算法。

Result: 证明了所提算法能够实现次线性RP-Regret；当所有玩家都最小化RP-Regret（或其线性化变体）时，可以学习到重复博弈的某些子博弈完美均衡；实验表明该指标在Stag-Hunt等博弈中能引导出更合作的、效用更高的解。

Conclusion: RP-Regret作为重复博弈原生的遗憾度量，能够支持更强的比较器和更少约束的对手，同时保持寻找更优均衡的可能性，为自适应对手环境下的在线学习提供了新的理论基础和实用算法。

Abstract: In this paper, we study regret minimization in repeated games with \emph{adaptive} opponents who can respond based on histories of play. The standard metric of \emph{external regret} in online learning is known to fail to capture such adaptivity. To account for players' counterfactual reasoning, we introduce {\tt Repeated Policy Regret (RP-Regret)}, a game-theoretic metric that measures the difference between the \emph{realized} and the \emph{best-in-hindsight} accumulated utility when all players can \emph{respond} to the history of play. Compared to existing regret notions in this setting, ours is native to repeated game playing, enabling stronger comparators and opponents with fewer constraints, while maintaining the possibility of finding better equilibria when all players minimize it. We first identify necessary conditions for obtaining {\tt RP-Regret} sublinear in time, on the variation of the player's comparator strategies in the regret definition and on the memories of both the comparator and opponents' strategies. We then study additional conditions and provable algorithms to minimize {\tt RP-Regret}, which is by definition \emph{non-convex} in the strategy space. To address this challenge, we propose three algorithms: (i) one based on an optimization oracle, as assumed in some prior work in online non-convex learning; (ii) one that minimizes a convex and \emph{linearized} surrogate of {\tt RP-Regret} at each iteration; (iii) one that directly minimizes {\tt RP-Regret} when opponents change strategies slowly. Furthermore, when all players can run algorithms to minimize the {\tt RP-Regret} (or its linearized variant), certain subgame perfect equilibria of the repeated game can be learned. We also provide experiments showing that minimizing our regret notions can lead to more cooperative solutions with higher utility in games such as Stag-Hunt.

</details>


### [199] [TailLoR: Protecting Principal Components in Parameter-Efficient Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06494)
*Marius Dragoi,Ioana Pintilie,Alexandra Dragomir,Antonio Barbalau,Florin Brad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TailLoR 是一种基于谱分解的参数高效持续学习方法，通过固定预训练权重的奇异基作为参考框架，仅更新奇异值矩阵，并对主导奇异方向施加软惩罚以减少干扰，将细粒度适应引导到长尾谱坐标中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于谱分解的参数高效微调方法在持续学习中取得了进展，但现有方法在更新时容易与预训练权重的主导奇异方向产生干扰，影响模型在连续任务上的表现。

Method: 利用预训练权重的奇异基 U 和 V 作为固定参考框架，学习对奇异值矩阵进行低秩更新；同时引入软谱惩罚机制，抑制对主导奇异方向的更新，从而将细粒度适应路由到高度灵活的长尾谱坐标中。

Result: 减少了更新与主导奇异方向之间的干扰，实现了更有效的细粒度适应，提升了持续学习性能。

Conclusion: TailLoR 通过将更新限制在奇异值矩阵并利用软谱惩罚，有效降低了任务间的干扰，为持续学习中的参数高效微调提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient finetuning methods based on spectral decomposition have enabled progress in Continual Learning. In this paper we introduce TailLoR, which utilizes the singular bases U and V of the pre-trained weights as a fixed reference frame to learn a low-rank update applied to the singular value matrix. A soft spectral penalty discourages updates aligned with dominant singular directions, reducing interference while routing fine-grained adaptation into the highly flexible, long-tail spectral coordinates.

</details>


<div id='cs.SC'></div>

# cs.SC [[Back]](#toc)

### [200] [A Finite Certificate for the Positive $n=9$ Vasc Inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06136)
*Dakai Guo,Ruichen Qiu,Yichuan Cao,Ruyong Feng*

Main category: cs.SC

TL;DR: 使用AI辅助（MechMath Agent团队）证明了Vasc循环不等式n=9的正实数情况，通过人类数学归约和计算机证书验证相结合的方式完成证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vasc循环不等式是一个著名的循环不等式猜想，n=9的情况尚未被证明。该工作旨在通过人机协作的方式攻克这一难题。

Method: 方法分为两部分：1）人类部分：将有理不等式归约为齐次多项式不等式，固定循环最大值，通过累积间隙参数化每个排序的固定最大值锥面；2）有限部分（AI生成）：覆盖所有8!=40320个排序锥面的证书，包含36815个系数叶子节点、2236个普通Polya乘子叶子节点和1269个AM-GM中点叠加叶子节点。

Result: 成功证明了Vasc循环不等式n=9的正实数情况，生成了包含40320个排序锥面全覆盖的验证证书。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了AI辅助数学证明的有效性，通过人机协作的方式解决了此前未知的循环不等式问题，并提供了可独立验证的证书和工具链。

Abstract: We prove the positive-real $n=9$ case of the Vasc cyclic inequality. The proof was obtained with human-guided assistance from the AI agent MechMath Agent Team: the human-readable part reduces the rational inequality to a homogeneous polynomial inequality, fixes a cyclic maximum, and parametrizes each sorted fixed-maximum cone by cumulative gaps; the finite part is a certificate covering all $8!=40320$ sorted cones. MechMath Agent Team generated the certificate verification workflow through Python tool calls, including the case split, verification programs, and terminal classifications. The published certificate has $36815$ coefficient leaves, $2236$ ordinary Polya multiplier leaves, and $1269$ AM-GM midpoint overlay leaves. Human authors audited the mathematical reductions and verification logic, and a separate artifact contains the certificate, an independent verifier, and a from-source rebuild route.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [201] [OLIVE: Online Low-Rank Incremental Learning for Efficient Adaptive Exoskeletons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05234)
*Dong Liu,Yanxuan Yu,Ben Lengerich,Tony Geng,Ying Nian Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OLIVE提出了一种参数高效的在线自适应框架，通过低秩残差分解（ΔW = AB^T）实现外骨骼控制的持续个性化，在约1800步内收敛，在步态平滑度、运动努力度和运动稳定性上分别提升13、22和15个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可穿戴外骨骼控制器依赖静态步态策略，无法适应动态真实环境或个体用户特征，缺乏在线自适应能力。

Method: 将控制策略的自适应部分分解为低秩残差形式ΔW = AB^T（r ≪ min(d,k)），使在线更新成本从O(dk)降至O(r(d+k))；通过基于奖励的策略梯度（仅依赖EMG、IMU、振动等体感反馈）更新参数；引入门控机制调节个性化强度，以及动态秩调度器根据地形复杂度调整更新维度。

Result: 在可穿戴平台上，OLIVE在步态平滑度、运动努力度和运动稳定性上分别比最强基线提升13、22和15个百分点，约1800步收敛，端到端延迟7.4ms。

Conclusion: OLIVE证明了在线低秩自适应框架能有效实现外骨骼控制的实时个性化，在多种地形（平地、楼梯、斜坡、不平整地面）上均表现出稳健性能，且计算效率高。

Abstract: Wearable exoskeleton systems hold promise for restoring mobility in individuals with physical impairments, yet most existing controllers rely on static gait policies that lack the ability to adapt to dynamic real-world environments or individual user characteristics. We present \olive (\underline{O}nline \underline{L}ow-rank \underline{I}ncremental Learning for Efficient Adapti\underline{ve} Exoskeletons), a parameter-efficient online adaptation framework that continuously personalizes exoskeleton control during deployment. \olive decomposes the adaptive component of the control policy into a low-rank residual form~$\dW = \At\Bt^\top$ with rank~$r!\ll!\min(d,k)$, reducing online update cost from $\mathcal{O}(dk)$ to $\mathcal{O}(r(d{+}k))$ while preserving the stability of a pretrained base controller~$\Wz$. Parameters are updated via a reward-shaped policy gradient driven purely by on-body sensor feedback (EMG, IMU, vibration), eliminating dependence on offline reference trajectories. A gating mechanism modulates the strength of personalization based on contextual state, and a dynamic rank scheduler adapts the update dimensionality to terrain complexity -- allocating minimal capacity on simple flat terrain and expanding to higher-rank updates on demanding uneven surfaces -- enabling robust performance across diverse activities: flat walking, stair navigation, slopes, and uneven terrain. Experiments on the wearable platform demonstrate that \olive achieves +13, +22, and +15 percentage-point improvements in gait smoothness, effort reduction, and motion stability over the strongest baseline, converging within $\sim$1{,}800 walking steps at 7.4,ms end-to-end latency. Our code implementation is available at https://github.com/FastLM/OLIVE.

</details>


### [202] [Dynamic Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery in Robotic Cellular Warehousing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05669)
*Cheng Ren,Ming Li,Xinping Guan,George Q. Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对机器人仓储系统中订单动态演化的实际需求，首次形式化了考虑订单内部演化的动态多智能体拾取与配送问题，并提出了两种基于token传递的事件触发在线重规划算法——动态Token传递和协作Token传递，仿真结果表明能显著降低订单流转时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAPD问题假设任务是静态的，但实际仓库运营中订单会动态演化（新SKU追加到正在执行的订单），现有方法无法处理这种订单内部演化场景。

Method: 基于Token传递范式，提出两种事件触发在线重规划算法：(1) 动态Token传递——通过添加订单分解和基于优先级的Token调度执行局部重规划，保证无碰撞执行；(2) 协作Token传递——进一步允许空闲机器人主动协助新添加的拾取任务，提升系统效率。

Result: 在RCWS环境中的仿真结果表明，与静态和非协作基线相比，所提方法显著降低了订单流转时间。

Conclusion: 首次形式化并解决了考虑订单内部演化的动态MAPD问题，所提算法能有效处理动态订单变更，提升仓储系统效率。

Abstract: Robotic Cellular Warehousing Systems (RCWS) give rise to multi-agent pickup and delivery (MAPD) processes in which robots sequentially collect multiple stock-keeping units (SKUs) for each order. Unlike classical MAPD formulations that assume static tasks, real warehouse operations often involve dynamic order evolution, where new SKUs may be appended to an order while it is being executed. Motivated by this practical requirement, this letter formulates the Dynamic Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery problem considering internal order evolution for the first time. Building on the token passing paradigm, we propose two event-triggered online replanning algorithms. The first, Dynamic Token Passing, performs localized replanning upon order updates through add-order decomposition and priority-based token scheduling while preserving collision-free execution. The second, Cooperative Token Passing, further enables idle robots to opportunistically assist newly added pickups, improving system-level efficiency. Simulation results in RCWS environments demonstrate that the proposed methods significantly reduce order flowtime compared with static and non-cooperative baselines.

</details>


### [203] [A New Quaternion-Joint Cable-Driven Redundant Manipulator Configuration and its Control Through FABRIK and Residual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05236)
*Tanapath Pornthisan,Thanapat Kemthong,Thanyapisit Kangsathien,Pasut Aranchaiya,Paulo Garcia,Viboon Sangveraphunsiri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种4段8关节的电缆驱动冗余机械臂，采用四元数关节设计，在更大工作空间和更低硬件成本下，利用残差强化学习实现了比FABRIK算法高出三个数量级的位置和方向控制精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业领域亟需能在高度障碍环境下穿越任意空间路径的机械臂；四元数关节虽减少了每自由度所需电机数量，但其运动学模型复杂，给构型先验决策、控制计算负担以及制造误差放大带来挑战。

Method: 设计了4段8关节的四元数关节机械臂构型，采用残差强化学习（Residual Reinforcement Learning）进行控制，并与FABRIK算法进行对比。

Result: 该构型比现有设计具有更高效的工作空间和更低的硬件成本；残差强化学习在位置和方向精度上比FABRIK算法高出三个数量级，实现精确控制；控制实现也更简单。

Conclusion: 所提方法与构型适用于新型机械臂系统的设计，为开发此类机械臂及对应控制系统提供了新的设计工具。

Abstract: Robotic arms capable of traversing arbitrary spatial paths, especially in highly obstructed workspaces, are highly desired across several industries. Quaternion-joints have recently empowered a specific class of robotic arms -- cable-driven redundant manipulators -- beyond its prior capabilities. Specifically, quaternion-joints reduce the number of required motors per degree of freedom, paving the way for more compact solutions.An ongoing challenge is that the complexity of the kinematic model of quaternion joints challenges a priori decisions on manipulator configurations and imposes higher computational demands on the control system and its non-linearities amplify all discrepancies between design and physical artifact arising from fabrication imprecision. Here we show a that a 4-segment, 8-joint manipulator can achieve a broader workspace than extant configurations, at lower hardware cost, and that Residual Reinforcement Learning outperforms extant state-of-the-art methods -- specifically, the FABRIK algorithm -- on the control of such manipulator. Our results show that this configuration is more workspace-effective than prior designs, and that Residual Reinforcement Learning outperforms FABRIK by three orders of magnitude on positional and orientational accuracy, effecting precise control of the novel 4-segment, 8-joint manipulator. Additionally, the control implementation is simpler: we describe the complete FABRIK process for control and corresponding learning implementation. Our methodology is applicable to the design of new systems, providing designers with further tools for the development of this class of manipulators and corresponding control systems for novel configurations.

</details>


### [204] [Accelerating and Scaling MPC-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Humanoid Locomotion and Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05687)
*Junheng Li,Liang Wu,Sergio A. Esteban,Lizhi Yang,Ján Drgoňa,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MPC-RL框架，通过并行化质心动力学MPC求解器（πⁿMPC）在训练阶段高效引导强化学习，无需繁琐问题构建和过高训练开销，在人体机器人 locomotion 和操作任务上取得优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPC和RL在人体运动控制中各有优势，但将MPC嵌入RL训练过程需要耗时的问题构建或过高的训练开销，使得这类框架在实践中难以证明其合理性。

Method: 提出MPC-RL框架：1）基于质心动力学的MPC奖励公式，利用MPC轨迹在训练阶段引导RL；2）开发πⁿMPC，一种并行化、无构建的批处理GPU MPC求解器，直接在时变动力学上运行，避免高内存使用和预编译。

Result: 通过多种对比研究和硬件验证，MPC-RL在 locomotion 和操作技能上均取得优越性能。

Conclusion: MPC-RL框架有效实现了高效的训练阶段MPC指导，显著提升了人体机器人运动与操作能力，且代码已开源。

Abstract: In humanoid motion control, model predictive control (MPC) offers physically grounded prediction and constraint handling, while reinforcement learning (RL) enables robust whole-body skills through large-scale simulation. However, using MPC inside RL often requires time-consuming problem construction or excessive training overhead, making such frameworks difficult to justify in practice. This work studies efficient training-time MPC guidance for humanoid locomotion and manipulation, termed MPC-RL. We introduce a centroidal-dynamics MPC reward formulation that leverages guidance from MPC trajectories in training time. To make this practical in massively parallel RL, we develop $π^n$MPC, a parallel-in-horizon and construction-free batched GPU MPC solver that operates directly on time-varying dynamics to avoid high memory usage and pre-compilation. Through a variety of comparative studies and hardware validations, we have found that MPC-RL achieves superior performance in locomotion and manipulation skills. The code base is available at https://github.com/junhengl/mpc-rl.

</details>


### [205] [Inverse Manipulation through Symbolic Planning and Residual Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05248)
*Yigit Yildirim,Giuseppe Rauso,Riccardo Caccavale,Alberto Finzi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合框架，通过从演示中自动提取STRIPS-like算子，结合符号逆规划和残差强化学习，实现机器人操作任务的逆技能学习。在PushCube任务上，符号逆规划实现粗粒度恢复，残差SAC策略精调物体位姿以满足逆谓词条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作任务的逆问题不能仅通过反转符号状态转换或重放运动轨迹来解决，需要在连续交互动力学下实现物理可行的逆技能。

Method: ①从演示中通过软几何谓词自动提取STRIPS-like算子；②为每个算子构建逆恢复目标（保留前提、恢复删除效果、否定添加效果）；③任务规划器尝试用现有动作原语满足逆目标；④未解决的符号谓词通过强化学习（SAC）学习残差策略进行精调。

Result: 在ManiSkill3 PushCube任务上，符号逆规划执行粗粒度的拾取-放置恢复，残差SAC策略精调立方体位姿，使近似符号逆规划转化为物理可行的逆技能。

Conclusion: 谓词导出的残差控制可以将近似的符号逆规划转化为物理上可行的逆技能，验证了符号推理与连续控制结合的有效性。

Abstract: Inverting a robotic task requires more than reversing symbolic state transitions or rewinding motor trajectories. In robot manipulation tasks, symbolic inverse plans often fail to fully restore the effects of forward executions under continuous interaction dynamics. We present a hybrid framework for inverse manipulation that derives inverse-skill objectives from STRIPS-like operators automatically extracted from demonstrations through soft geometric predicates. For each extracted operator, we construct an inverse restoration objective that preserves preconditions, restores delete effects, and negates add effects. A task planner first attempts to satisfy this objective using available action primitives. Unresolved symbolic predicates then induce a residual operator learning problem solved through Reinforcement Learning (RL). We evaluate the framework on the ManiSkill3 PushCube task. For a forward pushing skill, the symbolic inverse performs a coarse pick-and-place restoration, while a residual Soft Actor-Critic policy refines the cube pose to satisfy the remaining inverse predicates. Our results show that predicate-derived residual control can turn an approximate symbolic inverse into a physically grounded inverse skill.

</details>


### [206] [Sample-efficient Low-level Motion Planning for Robotic Manipulation Tasks via Zero-shot Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06041)
*Yuanzhi He,Victor Romero-Cano,José J. Patiño,Juan David Hernández,William Sawtell,Gualtiero Colombo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出iCEM+TL框架，通过迁移学习将iCEM参数从简单任务迁移到复杂任务，并结合奖励重塑进行任务分解，在仿真和真实机器人上分别取得高达23%的成功率提升和实际部署可行性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人系统日益复杂，运动规划模型的计算复杂度和训练时间大幅增加，现有iCEM方法在堆叠、滑动和货架放置等复杂场景中性能受限。

Method: 提出iCEM+TL框架，利用迁移学习将上游简单任务中关键的iCEM参数迁移到下游复杂任务；同时通过任务分解进行奖励重塑，针对堆叠和货架放置任务优化性能。

Result: 仿真中成功率提升高达23%；在Franka Emika机器人上完成堆叠任务的实际部署验证。

Conclusion: iCEM+TL框架结合迁移学习和奖励重塑能有效提升复杂运动规划任务的成功率，并具备实际机器人部署的可行性。

Abstract: As robotic systems become more sophisticated, the growing complexity of their motion planning models and the longer training times pose substantial challenges. Evolutionary algorithms such as the Sample-efficient Cross-Entropy Method (iCEM) have recently demonstrated promising potential for low-level real-time planning by leveraging efficient knowledge reuse strategies to improve performance. Although effective in many control tasks, iCEM's performance can be constrained in more complex scenarios, particularly those requiring stacking, sliding, and shelf placement. In this work, we propose a novel iCEM+TL framework that explicitly leverages Transfer Learning (TL), where key iCEM parameters are transferred from simpler upstream tasks to guide more complex downstream tasks. Additionally, we applied Reward Redesign (RR) through task decomposition for stacking objects and shelf placement to optimize task-specific performance. Results from the simulation show that our framework achieves success rate improvements of up to 23%. The framework is further validated on a real Franka Emika robot in a stacking task, demonstrating its practical feasibility for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [207] [Gotta Grow Fast: Design and Benchmarking of a Tip Mount for High-Speed Vine Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06040)
*Antonio Alvarez Valdivia,Robert Reeve,Ankush Dhawan,Ciera McFarland,Chad Council,Margaret McGuinness,Nathaniel Hanson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种三角形滚轮尖端安装座，通过滚动而非滑动减少藤蔓机器人生长时的内部阻力，首次在TPU涂层防撕裂尼龙藤蔓机器人上实现一致外翻，并通过定制测试台验证了其最低尾张力和最稳定的生长性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 藤蔓机器人通过尖端外翻生长，但尖端传感器集成面临材料持续更新、内层摩擦、尖端重量增加和织物收缩等挑战，现有安装方式限制了其在检测和搜索任务中的应用。

Method: 设计三角形滚轮尖端安装座，以滚动接触替代滑动接触降低内部阻力；通过迭代故障分析优化设计；搭建定制测试台，通过测量外翻时的尾部张力来量化安装座性能。

Result: 与先前设计相比，三角形滚轮安装座实现了最低的尾部张力和最可重复的生长性能，并在TPU涂层防撕裂尼龙材料上首次实现一致外翻。

Conclusion: 该工作既提供了一个经过验证的尖端安装座设计，也建立了一个可重复的基准测试框架，有助于推进软体生长机器人的传感器和工具集成。

Abstract: Soft, growing vine robots extend through tip eversion, a mechanism that enables navigation through cluttered environments. However, integrating cameras and other sensors at the tip is uniquely challenging because the material forming the tip is constantly renewed as the robot grows. This continual material turnover, combined with friction between internal layers, added tip weight, and fabric constriction, complicates sensor and tool mounting. These limitations hinder the deployment of vine robots for inspection and search tasks, where rapid growth while carrying tip-mounted sensors is essential. In this work, we present a triangular roller tip mount that reduces internal resistance during growth by rolling rather than sliding against the robot body. The design was refined through iterative failure analysis, enabling, for the first time, consistent eversion on a TPU-coated ripstop nylon vine robot. To quantitatively evaluate mount performance, we introduce a custom testbed that isolates tip mounting effects by measuring tail tension during eversion. Comparative experiments across multiple mount variants, including prior designs, show that our triangular roller mount achieves the lowest tail tension and most repeatable growth performance. These results establish both a validated tip mount design and a repeatable benchmarking framework for advancing sensor and tool integration in soft growing robots. CAD for the mount and testbed is available at: https://sprout-mitll.github.io/tip_mounts/.

</details>


### [208] [Efficient Computation of Distance Functions for Navigation Vector Fields in Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05372)
*Vinicius M. Gonçalves,João Baião,Felipe Bartelt,Douglas G. Macharet,Gustavo M. Freitas,Héctor Azpúrua,Luciano C. A. Pimenta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效计算矩阵李群上G-多项式曲线与点之间距离的方法，通过将问题转化为少量多项式求根计算，显著降低了计算时间，并在SE(3)群和机器人操作臂上进行了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于向量场的机器人控制方法在处理李群上的曲线时，需要反复计算机器人位姿与曲线之间的距离和最近点，这在嵌入式平台高控制频率下计算开销巨大，亟需一种高效的计算方法。

Method: 利用G-多项式曲线的结构特性，将距离计算问题归结为少量多项式求根问题，取代了原有的优化迭代方法。

Result: 仿真结果表明该方法相比传统优化方法显著缩短了计算时间，同时保持了计算精度；在SE(3)群上给出了实用公式，并在机器人操作臂上进行了实验验证。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效降低了李群曲线距离计算的计算复杂度，适用于高频率实时控制场景，并已开源实现。

Abstract: Vector-field-based methods are widely used for robot control and are often applied to the path-tracking problem. Some vector field approaches require repeatedly computing the distance between the robot configuration and the curve, as well as the corresponding closest point. Recently, vector fields have been extended to Lie Groups. In this case, this computation can be expensive, especially when performed at high control frequencies on embedded platforms. This paper proposes a method for efficiently computing the distance between a point and a curve represented as what is called a G-polynomial curve, which is a curve representation that generalizes polynomial curves to matrix Lie groups. The proposed approach exploits the structure of these curves to reduce the problem to a small number of polynomial root-finding computations. Simulation results show that the method significantly reduces computation time while maintaining accuracy compared to existing optimization-based approaches. Practical formulas are also provided for the case of the group SE(3), and the method is validated experimentally on a robotic manipulator. The methodology is implemented in a computational package, available online.

</details>


### [209] [VASO: Formally Verifiable Self-Evolving Skills for Physical AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05395)
*Yunhao Yang,Neel P. Bhatt,Kevin Wang,Samuel Tetteh,Zhangyang Wang,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VASO 是一个验证引导的自我进化框架，用模型检查器对 LLM 生成的机器人技能合约进行形式化验证，将反例轨迹转化为文本梯度来更新技能合约，在 Clearpath Jackal 和 PX4 四旋翼任务上实现 97.2% 的规范合规率，且无需微调基础模型权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人技能进化循环依赖执行反馈、单元测试、环境奖励或 LLM 自我批评等信号，但这些仅提供"痕迹级"证据——只能表明技能在采样执行中有效，无法保证技能驱动的规划在未测试条件下满足时序安全契约。基础模型虽降低了技能创建成本，但信任成本并未降低。

Method: VASO 将每个技能表示为具有双接口的语义契约：1) 形式化接口——将机器人状态、观测和控制命令对齐到逻辑命题以支持模型检查；2) 规划器接口——指导可执行行为生成。模型检查器先过滤逻辑不一致的技能契约，再验证技能生成的规划是否满足全局和局部时序规约。验证失败时，将反例轨迹转化为文本梯度来更新可复用的技能合约，同时冻结基础模型权重。

Result: 在 Clearpath Jackal 和 PX4 四旋翼任务上，VASO 以不到 100 个优化样本达到 97.2% 的形式化规范合规率，优于执行反馈、提示优化和微调基线方法。

Conclusion: VASO 是首个将形式化验证与 LLM 生成技能自我进化闭环的框架——形式化反例成为可复用机器人技能合约的优化反馈，而非仅验证一次性规划、调优规划器提示或微调模型权重。

Abstract: Reusable robot skills are becoming the basic units through which embodied agents turn open-ended instructions into long-horizon physical behavior. We argue that, while foundation models have collapsed the cost of creating these skills, the cost of trusting them has not. Existing skill-evolution loops refine skills through execution feedback, unit tests, environment reward, or LLM self-critique, but these signals provide only trace-level evidence: they show that a skill worked on sampled executions, not that skill-induced plans satisfy temporal safety contracts under untested conditions. We introduce VASO, a framework for verification-guided self-evolution of LLM-generated robot skill contracts. In VASO, each skill is represented as a semantic contract with two coupled interfaces: a formal interface that aligns robot states, observations, and control commands with logical propositions for model checking, and a planner-facing interface that guides executable behavior generation. A model checker first filters logically inconsistent skill contracts, then verifies plans induced by the skill against global and local temporal specifications. When verification fails, VASO translates the counterexample trace into a textual gradient that updates the reusable skill contract while keeping foundation-model weights frozen. On Clearpath Jackal and PX4 quadcopter tasks, VASO reaches 97.2% formal-specification compliance using fewer than 100 optimization samples, outperforming execution-feedback, prompt-optimization, and fine-tuning baselines. To our knowledge, VASO is the first framework that closes the loop between formal verification and self-evolving LLM-generated skills for physical AI agents: formal counterexamples become optimization feedback for reusable robot skill contracts, rather than merely verifying one-off plans, tuning planner prompts, or fine-tuning model weights.

</details>


### [210] [MoDex: A Diffusion Policy for Sequential Multi-Object Dexterous Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05407)
*Haofei Lu,Hongjia Liu,Yifei Dong,Florian T. Pokorny,Jens Lundell,Danica Kragic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MoDex，一种基于扩散策略的灵巧手多物体顺序抓取方法，通过对手空间和点云条件预测抓取位姿，使手部能分次利用自由度抓取多个物体而不释放已抓取物体，仿真和实物实验均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧手抓取方法通常将所有自由度用于单一物体，未充分利用手的灵巧性，无法实现多物体顺序抓取。

Method: 提出MoDex扩散策略，以对手空间和点云为条件，从观测直接预测下一抓取位姿；采用两阶段训练：先模仿学习专家示范，再通过强化学习微调以促进仿真到现实的迁移。

Result: 在MuJoCo仿真和真实硬件平台上，MoDex成功率分别比基线方法高2.92-17.92%和6.67-17.78%。

Conclusion: MoDex通过对手空间条件实现灵巧手部分自由度用于当前抓取、保留其余自由度用于后续抓取，有效解决了多物体顺序抓取问题，且两阶段训练策略提升了实际部署性能。

Abstract: This work addresses sequentially grasping multiple objects with a single dexterous hand without releasing those already held. Most dexterous grasping methods commit all of the hand's degrees of freedom to a single object, underutilizing its dexterity and leaving no redundancy for subsequent grasps. The proposed solution, MoDex, is a diffusion policy that predicts the next gripper pose directly from observations, conditioned on an opposition space and point cloud. The opposition space condition specifies which fingers participate in the current grasp, enabling the gripper to use only a subset of its available degrees of freedom while reserving the remaining degrees of freedom for subsequent grasps. To facilitate sim-to-real transfer, MoDex is trained in two stages: first through imitation learning on expert demonstrations, and subsequently through reinforcement learning fine-tuning, which consistently improves success rates over the pre-trained policy. We evaluate MoDex in simulation on a MuJoCo-based Franka Emika Panda robot equipped with an Allegro Hand and on the corresponding real-world hardware platform. Across both simulation and real-world experiments, MoDex achieves higher success rates than the evaluated learning-based baselines, improving performance by 2.92-17.92% and 6.67-17.78%, respectively. Project page: https://modex2026.github.io/.

</details>


### [211] [Learning from Demonstrations over Riemannian Manifolds using Neural ODEs: An Extended Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05422)
*Diana Cuervo Espinosa,Mahathi Anand,Angela P. Schoellig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用神经常微分方程在黎曼流形上高效估计测地线，用于机器人学习从演示中生成同时编码位置和朝向的自然运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的从演示学习通常在欧氏空间中进行，而机器人状态（如朝向）自然演化于弯曲空间（黎曼流形）。现有方法在流形上计算测地线的计算开销大，需要更高效的方法。

Method: 提出通过神经常微分方程（Neural ODEs）数值估计黎曼流形上的测地线，以降低计算成本；测地线随后被解码回原始任务空间并部署到机器人上。

Result: 通过仿真实验获得了初步结果，并与其它测地线计算方法进行了比较。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在黎曼流形上高效编码位置和朝向数据，生成自然的复杂运动，但仍有挑战需要在未来工作中解决。

Abstract: Learning from demonstratins (LfD) is usually performed over Euclidean spaces, while the robot state, e.g. orientation, naturally evolves over curved spaces. Therefore, to ensure natural, complex motion generation, we investigate learning from demonstrations over Riemannian manifolds that are capable of encoding both position and orientation data. Here, geodesic paths provide for natural motion between two arbitrary points within the manifold. We propose to numerically estimate geodesics via neural ordinary differential equations, mitigating large computational overhead of existing approaches. Finally, these geodesics can be decoded back into the original task space before deploying on the robot. In this extended abstract, we discuss the architecture of our framework, provide some initial insights from our simulation experiments, including comparison to other geodesic computation mechanisms, and discuss the challenges and prospects for future work.

</details>


### [212] [Uncertainty-Aware Adaptive Sensor Fusion for Autonomous Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05437)
*Simegnew Yihunie Alaba,Yuichi Motai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合深度学习框架，结合无迹卡尔曼滤波（UKF）与视觉Transformer（ViT）+多尺度CNN（MCNN），通过自适应传感器融合和不确定性感知损失函数，在KITTI数据集上以155 FPS实现卓越的VIO位姿估计精度（ATE/RPE优于基线）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VIO方法在噪声、不完整或不可靠传感器输入下鲁棒性不足，缺乏有效的不确定性建模与自适应融合机制。

Method: (1) ViT网络捕获IMU时序依赖；(2) MCNN学习视觉光流运动线索；(3) 基于估计不确定性的自适应传感器融合模块动态加权IMU与视觉特征；(4) 不确定性感知损失函数将预测不确定性显式融入学习过程；(5) 结合UKF进行状态估计。

Result: 在KITTI数据集上，ATE和RPE显著优于基线方法；模型轻量化，在NVIDIA A100 GPU上达到155 FPS的处理速度。

Conclusion: 所提方法在精度、鲁棒性和计算效率上均具有优势，适用于资源受限的自主导航系统。

Abstract: This work introduces a hybrid deep learning approach integrated with an Unscented Kalman Filter (UKF) to enhance pose estimation accuracy in Visual-Inertial Odometry (VIO) for autonomous navigation. The proposed model employs a Vision Transformer (ViT) network to effectively capture temporal dependencies from inertial measurement unit (IMU) data and utilizes a Multiscale Convolutional Neural Network (MCNN) to learn optical flow-based motion cues from visual data. An adaptive sensor fusion module dynamically weights IMU and visual features by leveraging estimated uncertainty, thus improving robustness in diverse and challenging environmental conditions. Additionally, a novel uncertainty-aware loss function is proposed to explicitly incorporate prediction uncertainty into the learning process, enabling robust and accurate navigation under noisy, incomplete, or unreliable sensor inputs. Comprehensive evaluations of the KITTI dataset demonstrate that the proposed method significantly outperforms baseline approaches, achieving superior performance in terms of Absolute Trajectory Error (ATE) and Relative Pose Error (RPE). The lightweight and computationally efficient model processes data at 155 FPS on an NVIDIA A100 GPU, making it highly suitable for deployment in resource-constrained autonomous systems.

</details>


### [213] [FlowPRO: Reward-Free Reinforced Fine-Tuning of Flow-Matching VLAs via Proximalized Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05468)
*Yihao Wu,He Zhang,Junbo Tan,Xueqian Wang,Zhengyou Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlowPRO是一个针对流匹配VLA模型的免奖励离线强化微调框架，通过RPRO偏好优化目标和干预-回滚数据收集范式，在四类长周期双臂任务中取得最高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练VLA模型为可部署策略存在瓶颈：SFT和DAgger仅间接利用失败信号，基于奖励的RL受限于真实世界奖励设计困难和可靠评论家训练难度。

Method: 提出RPRO目标（流匹配偏好优化），结合对比优化器和显式近端正则化器消除奖励黑客问题；采用遥操作干预-回滚范式生成成对轨迹，通过平滑插值和批量混合将稀疏修正转换为稠密逐状态监督。

Result: 在四个长周期双臂任务中，FlowPRO取得最高成功率，优于四个基线方法，消融实验证实各损失分量的贡献。

Conclusion: FlowPRO通过免奖励偏好优化和数据增强策略有效提升了VLA模型在实际机器人上的部署性能。

Abstract: Post-training Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models into policies that can be reliably deployed on real robots remains a major bottleneck. SFT and DAgger exploit failure signals only indirectly, and reward-based RL is bottlenecked by the difficulty of real-world reward design and of training reliable critics. We present FlowPRO, a reward-free offline reinforced fine-tuning framework for flow-matching VLAs. Algorithmically, we propose RPRO (Robotic Flow-matching Proximalized Preference Optimization), a preference-optimization objective tailored to the flow-matching action head of VLA models. RPRO pairs a contrastive optimizer with an explicit proximal regularizer that anchors the absolute magnitude of the implicit reward, thereby eliminating the reward-hacking failure mode of plain Flow-DPO. On the data side, a teleoperated intervention-and-rollback paradigm produces naturally paired positive and negative trajectories $(τ^w, τ^l)$ on a real robot from a single operator action; a Smooth Interpolation procedure, combined with batch mixing, then converts these sparse corrections into dense per-state supervision while preserving the base policy's capabilities. On four long-horizon bimanual tasks, FlowPRO attains the highest success rate, outperforming four representative baselines, and ablations confirm the contribution of each loss component.

</details>


### [214] [Learning Contact Representation for Leg Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05501)
*Emre Girgin,Cagri Kilic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自监督表示学习框架，仅利用关节编码器标准传感器集实现足式机器人接触检测，无需力传感器增强，并通过概率建模区分支撑相和摆动相，实验证明其性能优于监督方法和基线概率方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足式机器人里程计估计依赖于足部与地面接触时速度为零的假设，而基于力传感器的接触检测方法并非普遍可用，且对滑移等扰动不敏感。现有监督方法需要传感器增强和标注，成本高且泛化性差，因此需要一种仅依赖标准传感器集的自监督接触检测方法。

Method: 提出自监督表示学习框架，仅使用关节编码器标准传感器集；通过学习到的表示对支撑相和摆动相进行概率建模；无需力传感器或额外标注。

Result: 实验证实了该自监督接触检测器的有效性；性能优于需要传感器增强和标注的监督方法，以及基线概率方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的自监督框架可有效替代基于力传感器的接触检测方法，降低硬件依赖和标注成本，代码已开源。

Abstract: The estimation of odometry in legged robots depends on the assumption that the velocity of the foot with respect to the world remains zero during the stance phase. Feedback for the main body velocity is derived from the kinematic serial chain of the feet making accurate leg phase detection is a critical subproblem. A considerable number of studies employ ground reaction force sensors mounted at the tip of the foot to classify, yet these sensors may not be universally available for all legged robots. Additionally, these sensors are often unresponsive to unaccounted disturbances, such as slippage, while the foot remains in contact with the ground. In this study, we propose a self-supervised representation learning framework for contact detection that utilizes the standard sensor set of joint encoders without reliance on force sensor augmentations. We employ learned representations to model the stance and swing phases probabilistically. The experimental results obtained confirm the efficacy of the proposed self-supervised contact detector. Our framework exhibited superior performance in comparison to supervised methods which necessitate sensor set augmentation and labeling, as well as baseline probabilistic approaches. Additionally, we make our code available to the public.

</details>


### [215] [Auditing Demonstration Curation Metrics: Action-Only Scorers Fail on the Structural Defects That Degrade Imitation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05588)
*Aarav Bedi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 现有模仿学习策略评估指标在识别低质量演示数据方面存在严重缺陷：对于结构性错误（关键时刻的错误动作），所有仅基于动作的指标均失效甚至反向评分，只有状态轨迹指标能部分检测，且高检测精度并不保证下游性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习策略的质量受限于训练演示数据的质量，虽然已有多种自动筛选指标用于过滤低质量演示，但这些指标在不同数据集和协议下分别验证，缺乏统一的比较基准，因此不清楚哪些指标能有效识别有害演示。

Method: 构建可控测试平台，向演示数据注入两类已知缺陷（微小扰动：相关动作噪声、震颤、截断；结构性错误：关键时刻错误动作），评估七种筛选指标在两个维度上的表现：区分缺陷演示与干净演示的能力，以及基于各指标筛选后的子集训练行为克隆策略能否提升任务成功率。

Result: 微小扰动可通过多变量离群点检测有效识别，移除后可完全恢复下游性能差距；结构性错误对所有仅基于动作的指标均不可见，其中两个指标出现反向评分（将缺陷演示评为更高质量），用于筛选后反而使策略性能低于或等于未筛选基线。仅状态轨迹指标能检测结构性错误，但最佳指标也只能恢复三分之一的下游差距。高检测精度并不保证下游性能提升。

Conclusion: 当前演示筛选指标存在根本性局限，尤其是无法有效处理结构性错误，且检测准确率与下游策略性能之间存在脱节，需要开发更有效的筛选方法。

Abstract: Imitation-learning policies inherit the quality of the demonstrations they are trained on, and a growing set of curation metrics promise to score and filter low-quality demonstrations automatically. These metrics are each validated on different data with different protocols, so it is unclear which of them actually identify the demonstrations that harm a policy. We build a controlled testbed in which demonstration defects are injected with known type, and audit seven curation metrics along two axes: how well each separates defective from clean demonstrations, and whether training a behavior-cloning policy on each metric's curated subset improves task success. We study two defect regimes. Subtle perturbations (correlated action noise, tremor, truncation) are detectable by multivariate outlier scoring and, once removed, recover the full downstream gap. Structural errors, where the demonstration executes a wrong action at a key moment, are invisible to every action-only metric we test, and two of them are inverted: they score defective demonstrations as higher quality and, used for curation, tend to leave the policy at or below the uncurated baseline rather than above it. Only metrics that examine the state trajectory detect structural errors, and even the best of them recovers just a third of the downstream gap. High detection accuracy does not guarantee downstream improvement. We release the testbed and all curation implementations.

</details>


### [216] [Discrete-WAM: Unified Discrete Vision-Action Token Editing for World-Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05645)
*Ziyang Yao,Haochen Liu,Yuncheng Jiang,Zeyu Zhu,Zibin Guo,Jingru Wang,Tianle Liu,Jianwei Cui,Kuiyuan Yang,Hongwei Xie,Jingwei Zhao,Guang Chen,Hangjun Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Discrete-WAM，一种统一离散潜变量视觉-动作世界策略，将未来视觉状态和自车动作表示为对齐的离散token，通过共享离散扩散框架统一世界模型、世界动作策略和分层决策策略，实现自动驾驶中的组合因果推理和反事实推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶方法仅建立状态到动作的直接映射，缺乏对动作条件动力学的显式建模；连续潜变量世界模型缺乏组合结构，难以进行跨反事实未来的因果推理。

Method: 将未来视觉状态和自车动作编码为对齐的离散token，构建统一的离散扩散框架，联合建模世界模型（预测未来状态）、世界动作策略（动作条件生成）和分层决策策略（高层决策支持），支持组合泛化。

Result: 在大规模自动驾驶基准上取得竞争性性能，同时支持可控生成和反事实推理，为更可靠的决策提供路径。

Conclusion: Discrete-WAM通过离散对齐和统一扩散框架，有效实现了自动驾驶中的组合因果推理能力，在性能和可控性方面均具优势。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires reasoning about how ego actions shape the evolution of the surrounding world. However, most end-to-end methods rely on direct state-to-action mappings, capturing correlations without explicitly modeling action-conditioned dynamics. Conversely, continuous-latent world models often lack compositional structure for causal reasoning across counterfactual futures. We introduce Discrete-WAM, a unified latent vision-action world policy that represents future visual states and ego actions as aligned discrete tokens, enabling compositional causal reasoning across alternative futures. Built upon this unified discrete alignment, Discrete-WAM establishes a shared discrete diffusion framework with unified generative tasks, jointly formulating world modeling, world-action policy, and hierarchical decision-enabled policy, supporting compositional generalization across diverse driving scenarios. Experiments on large-scale autonomous-driving benchmarks show that Discrete-WAM achieves competitive performance while supporting controllable generation and counterfactual reasoning, offering a principled path toward more reliable decision-making.

</details>


### [217] [Safe Embodied AI for Long-horizon Tasks: A Cross-layer Analysis of Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05660)
*Dabin Kim,Daemin Park,Sangyub Lee,Jinsik Kim,Yeongtak Oh,Jongho Shin,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文从具身AI视角综述了长程机器人操作中的安全问题，将文献按规划时、策略时和执行时的干预节点分类，分析了各类证据强度，并指出了当前研究中的关键空白与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着具身AI系统在物理环境中进行长程推理和行动的能力增强，安全性问题日益突出，但相关文献分散于规划、策略设计和运行时执行等多个领域，缺乏系统性的整合梳理。

Method: 采用结构化综述方法，按干预节点（规划时、策略时、执行时）组织文献，并分析每类工作的证据强度（形式化保证、统计支持、经验性安全启发式），同时区分骨干能力论文、直接安全机制和基准评估研究。

Result: 揭示了当前安全声明的支撑情况：有些证据充分，有些仍不充分。识别出若干持续存在的空白，包括策略时安全的证据有限、接触丰富的长程操作缺乏形式化支持、不确定性触发的干预机制不成熟、以及缺少操作专用的安全基准。

Conclusion: 提出跨层保障、评估设计和长程机器人智能体在真实环境中更安全部署的研究方向。

Abstract: Embodied AI systems are increasingly expected to reason and act over extended horizons in physical environments. This growing capability brings safety to the foreground, because failures in the physical world can harm people, damage objects, and disrupt workplaces. Although safe embodied AI has attracted substantial attention, the literature remains fragmented across planning, policy design, and runtime execution. Long-horizon robotic manipulation is a particularly revealing anchor domain for this problem because semantic misgrounding, subtask-level error propagation, execution drift, and contact-rich physical risk can accumulate within the same closed-loop system. This survey therefore provides a structured review of safety in long-horizon robotic manipulation from an embodied AI perspective. We organize the literature by intervention locus, covering planning-time, policy-time, and execution-time safety, and we analyze the strength of the evidence that each line of work provides, distinguishing formal guarantees, statistical support, and empirical safety heuristics. This framework clarifies the distinct roles of backbone capability papers, direct safety mechanisms, and benchmark or evaluation studies, while exposing where current safety claims are well supported and where they remain indirect. We identify persistent gaps, including limited evidence for policy-time safety, weak formal support for contact-rich long-horizon manipulation, immature uncertainty-triggered intervention, and a shortage of manipulation-specific safety benchmarks. We conclude by outlining research directions for cross-layer assurance, evaluation design, and safer deployment of long-horizon robotic agents in real-world settings.

</details>


### [218] [Preserving Full 6-DOF Actuation Under Abrupt Total Rotor Failures: Passive Fault-Tolerant Flight Control Using a Biaxial-Tilt Hexacopter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05663)
*Yipeng Yang,Yiqiao Tang,Hao Zhang,Jinqi Jiang,Jianfeng He,Rumo Chen,Xinghu Yu,Zhan Li,Huijun Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对双轴倾转冗余驱动六旋翼飞行器（BTO）在完全未知的转子突发失效情况下的被动容错控制问题，提出了两种无需故障检测与在线优化的高效控制方案，通过扩展可达力旋量空间的度量方法并引入瞬态力旋量跳变项，实现了在多达三个转子同时失效情况下的稳定悬停与六自由度轨迹跟踪，实验验证了该方法相较于传统单轴倾转和共面六旋翼设计具有更大的恢复裕度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多旋翼在转子突然完全失效时会面临可达力旋量空间（AWS）的急剧坍塌，导致六自由度完全恢复在物理上不可行。现有方法多依赖故障检测与隔离或模式切换，但实际中故障模式先验未知，因此需要一种无需明确故障检测的被动容错控制方案。

Method: (1) 扩展AWS内接球度量，引入瞬态力旋量跳变项，实现对多达三个同时转子失效的定量可行性评估；(2) 提出两种被动容错方案：方案一在控制器层结合高阶全驱动（HOFA）控制器与线性扩展状态观测器（LESO）进行集总扰动抑制；方案二在分配器层采用模型参考自适应控制分配（MRACA）结合基于动量矩的力旋量估计以补偿控制分配偏差。

Result: 仿真与飞行实验验证了单转子及多转子失效下的稳定悬停与六自由度轨迹跟踪能力。系统性比较表明BTO相比单轴倾转和共面六旋翼设计具有更大的恢复裕度。额外实验（含风扰室内跟踪、极限条件室外跟踪、窄框穿越及接触式空中书写）进一步验证了框架的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: BTO在转子突发失效下具备显著的被动容错优势，所提两种方案无需故障检测即可实现有效容错，为过驱动多旋翼在极端故障条件下的安全飞行提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Conventional multirotors suffer from a rapid collapse of attainable wrench space (AWS) under abrupt total rotor failures, rendering full 6-DOF recovery physically impossible. This paper addresses passive fault-tolerant flight of a biaxial-tilt overactuated hexacopter (BTO) under abrupt total rotor failures that are a priori unknown to the controller. The control design and analysis focus on representative abrupt rotor-failure cases for which the post-failure system remains fully actuated, while no explicit fault detection, isolation, or fault-mode switching is assumed. First, we extend the inscribed-sphere metric of the AWS by incorporating the transient-wrench-jump term, enabling quantitative feasibility assessment under up to three simultaneous rotor failures and benchmarking against uniaxial-tilt and coplanar hexacopters. Second, we develop two computationally efficient passive schemes without relying on fault detection or online optimization. One scheme operates at the controller layer by combining a high-order fully actuated (HOFA) controller with a linear extended state observer (LESO) for lumped-disturbance rejection. The other scheme operates at the allocator layer by using model-reference adaptive control allocation with momentum-based wrench estimation to compensate for control-allocation biases. Simulations and flight experiments validate stable hovering and 6-DOF trajectory tracking under single and multiple rotor failures. Further systematic comparisons confirm that the BTO provides larger recovery margins than uniaxial-tilt and coplanar designs. Additional onboard-sensor-only experiments, including indoor tracking under wind disturbance, outdoor tracking under extreme conditions, narrow-frame traversal, and contact-based aerial writing, further validate the robustness of the proposed framework in complex operational environments.

</details>


### [219] [DexFuture: Hierarchical Future-State Visuomotor Targeting for Bimanual Dexterous Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05699)
*Runfa Blark Li,Kuang-Ting Tu,Nikola Raicevic,Dwait Bhatt,Xinshuang Liu,Keito Suzuki,Ki Myung Brian Lee,Nikolay Atanasov,Truong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DexFuture分层系统，通过高层未来状态预测器生成手-工具-物体多步目标轨迹，再由低层策略进行跟踪，在60Hz下达到特权oracle性能的90%，且比基于未来动作条件世界模型的CEM规划快约250倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧双手机器人工具使用策略依赖示教提供的未来位姿参考或需要基于未来动作条件世界模型的慢速在线规划，缺乏一种无需示教特权状态或慢速反事实规划即可生成动态一致未来参考轨迹的方法。

Method: 提出DexFuture分层系统：(1) 高层Future-State Visuomotor Target Predictor，基于RGB、本体感知和几何历史，通过horizon-conditioned transformer生成多步未来目标轨迹；(2) 低层Target-Conditioned Structured Dexterous Policy，通过target-conditioned per-link transformer跟踪目标轨迹。分层架构将粗粒度未来参考生成与细粒度动作控制解耦，将慢速长程语义预测与高频执行解耦。

Result: 在OakInk2双人工具使用任务中达到特权oracle性能的90%（无参考策略仅7%），运行频率60Hz，比DexWM风格CEM规划快约250倍。

Conclusion: 分层预测-跟踪范式可有效解决灵巧双手工具使用中高维动作空间和复杂接触动力学带来的未来轨迹生成难题，实现高效且高性能的机器人控制。

Abstract: Bimanual dexterous tool use remains challenging for robots due to high-dimensional hand configurations and complex hand-tool-object dynamics and contact. Most existing control policies depend on future configuration references provided from demonstrations, while future action-conditioned world models require slow online planning over high-dimensional action sequences. A significant challenge is generating a dynamically consistent future reference trajectory without relying on privileged states from demonstrations or slow counterfactual planning. We propose DexFuture, a hierarchical system that couples a high-level Future-State Visuomotor Target Predictor with a low-level Target-Conditioned Structured Dexterous Policy. Conditioned on egocentric RGB, proprioceptive and geometric history, the high-level predictor constructs structured hand-tool-object visuomotor embeddings and uses a horizon-conditioned transformer to generate a multi-step future target trajectory. Then, the low-level policy tracks them with a target-conditioned per-link transformer. This hierarchy decouples coarse future reference generation from fine-grained action control, and slow long-horizon semantic prediction from high-frequency execution. On OakInk2 bimanual tool-use tasks, DexFuture achieves 90% of the privileged-oracle performance, compared to 7% for a no-reference policy. DexFuture operates at 60 Hz, approximately 250 times faster than DexWM-style Cross-Entropy Method (CEM) planning with a future action-conditioned world model.

</details>


### [220] [PiL-World: A Chunk-Wise World Model for VLA Policy-in-the-Loop Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05773)
*Chong Ma,Taiyi Su,Jian Zhu,Jianjun Zhang,Zitai Huang,Yi Xu,Hanli Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PiL-World是一个面向闭环VLA策略评估的块级世界模型，通过动作条件视频生成和失败轨迹学习，在无需真实机器人执行的情况下实现高保真度的多视角未来观测预测，将VLA成功率评估误差从63.2%降至12.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型仅支持沿预采集轨迹的开环预测，无法为需要基于执行结果观测进行条件决策的闭环VLA策略提供评估支持。

Method: 提出PiL-World块级世界模型：1）以当前观测和VLA策略动作块为输入，生成多视角未来观测；2）通过交替执行VLA推理和世界模型预测实现闭环评估；3）利用动作驱动的头部视角视觉控制和潜历史编码提升生成保真度；4）联合预测互补多视角观测；5）同时从成功遥操作和失败执行轨迹中学习。

Result: 在三个真实双臂操作任务上，PiL-World生成的想象 rollout 与真实执行高度一致；相比基线，将VLA成功率在真实执行与闭环世界模型评估之间的误差从63.2%降至12.0%。

Conclusion: PiL-World通过闭环世界模型有效弥合了VLA策略在模拟评估与真实执行之间的性能鸿沟，为无实物部署的机器人策略评估提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies operate in a closed loop in real-world robot tasks: a robot observes the scene, executes an action chunk, and conditions its next decision on the resulting observation. However, most existing world models for robot action evaluation are limited to open-loop prediction along pre-collected action trajectories. This prevents them from supporting closed-loop VLA evaluation, where each action chunk must be conditioned on the observation generated by the previous execution. To address this gap, we propose PiL-World, a chunk-wise world model designed for policy-in-the-loop VLA evaluation. Given the current observation and the action trajectory rolled out by a VLA policy, PiL-World generates multi-view future observations that are consistent with the VLA rollout and match the image inputs required by the policy. By alternating between VLA inference and world-model prediction, PiL-World enables closed-loop evaluation without real robot execution at every step. To improve rollout fidelity, PiL-World conditions video generation on action-derived visual control from head-view robot motion and latent histories that encode task execution context, while jointly predicting complementary multi-view observations. Beyond successful teleoperated demonstrations, it also learns from failed execution trajectories, helping the imagined rollouts better match the distribution of real policy executions. We evaluate PiL-World on three real dual-arm manipulation tasks. PiL-World generates imagined rollouts that are highly consistent with real robot executions. More importantly, compared with the baseline, it reduces the error between VLA success rates measured in real-world rollouts and those estimated through closed-loop world-model evaluation from 63.2% to 12.0%.

</details>


### [221] [Visuotactile and Explicitly Force-Controlled Robotic Ultrasound for Abdominal Volumetric Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05848)
*Adrian Piedra,R Brooke Jeffrey,Oussama Khatib*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种集成立体视觉、触觉反馈和专家策略的自主超声采集机器人系统，通过复现专家操作数据、生成腹部三维地形图并融合刚度测量，实现了自适应扫描路径规划与闭环力控制，在获得与专家相当成像质量的同时，还支持三维容积采集等超越人类能力的诊断功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人超声系统缺乏自适应患者解剖结构差异的能力，且未能有效融合专家操作知识与自动化控制策略。本文旨在通过整合专家经验、视觉感知与力觉反馈，实现高质量、可复现且具有自主适应性的腹部超声扫描。

Method: 1) 采集放射科专家的自由操作运动与力数据，建立专家扫描框架；2) 利用立体视觉生成腹部三维地形图；3) 在关键点进行刚度测量以界定肋骨边界；4) 规划两种扫描路径：肋骨下向上倾斜扫查和垂直于软组织的扫查；5) 采用七自由度柔性机械臂，通过闭环力控制实现探头与不同解剖表面的恒压接触。

Result: 实验表明，该系统在动态适应患者个体解剖差异的前提下，实现了与专家扫描相媲美的高质量成像。此外，机器人系统在三维容积数据采集方面超越了人类专家能力，为高级诊断分析提供了更丰富的体数据。

Conclusion: 将专家知识与感知驱动的自主性相结合，配合物理推理（力控与刚度感知），可显著提升机器人超声系统的诊断性能和自适应能力，为自主医疗成像系统的发展提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a robotic ultrasound acquisition system that integrates stereo vision, touch-based feedback, and expert-informed strategies to perform autonomous and adaptive abdominal scans. The system records freehand motion and force data from expert radiologists, creating a framework to capture transducer motion, applied forces, and anatomical scanning strategies. This expert data is replayed to replicate characteristic scans with the robot, forming a foundation for further autonomous capabilities. Using stereo vision, the system generates three-dimensional topography maps of the patient's abdomen, which are refined through stiffness measurements at key points to delineate the rib cage boundary. These combined techniques enable the robot to execute two distinct scanning paths: an upward-angled sweep beneath the rib cage to visualize structures near the upper abdomen and a perpendicular sweep across soft tissue regions. A compliant, torque-controlled seven degree-of-freedom robotic manipulator is controlled to maintain consistent probe contact through closed-loop force control over the varied anatomical surfaces. Physical experiments demonstrate that the system achieves high-quality imaging comparable to expert scans while dynamically adapting to patient-specific topographies. Furthermore, the robotic system surpasses expert capabilities by enabling three-dimensional volume acquisition, which enhances diagnostic potential and provides volumetric data for advanced analyses. This work highlights the integration of expert knowledge into autonomous robotic systems and underscores the potential of combining perception-based autonomy with physical reasoning for enhanced diagnostic performance.

</details>


### [222] [LadderMan: Learning Humanoid Perceptive Ladder Climbing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05873)
*Siheng Zhao,Yuanhang Zhang,Ziqi Lu,Pieter Abbeel,Rocky Duan,Koushil Sreenath,Yue Wang,C. Karen Liu,Guanya Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LadderMan是一个使类人机器人能够稳健攀爬各种梯子并在梯子上执行操作任务的统一系统，采用两阶段学习框架（混合运动追踪+专家蒸馏）和视觉基础模型弥合仿真到真实的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类人机器人攀爬梯子因稀疏的脚/手支撑点、复杂的全身协调以及感知与控制误差敏感性而极具挑战性，现有方法缺乏统一且可实际部署的解决方案。

Method: 两阶段学习流水线：1) 从单个参考运动通过混合运动追踪学习多个攀爬专家；2) 通过混合模仿学习和强化学习将专家蒸馏为统一的基于深度视觉的运动策略。使用视觉基础模型解决深度感知的仿真到真实差距。操作策略采用双智能体公式化训练，支持遥操作。

Result: LadderMan在多种几何形状的梯子上实现稳健攀爬，零样本迁移到真实硬件，并支持在梯子约束下的多种操作任务。

Conclusion: 提出的统一系统有效解决了类人机器人攀爬梯子的核心挑战，具有较好的泛化性和实际部署能力。

Abstract: Humanoid robots hold great promise for operating in human-centered environments, yet ladder climbing remains one of the most challenging tasks due to sparse footholds and handholds, complex whole-body coordination, and sensitivity to perception and control errors. We present \textbf{LadderMan}, a unified system that enables humanoid robots to robustly climb diverse ladders and perform manipulation under such constrained conditions. Our climbing policy is built on a scalable two-stage learning pipeline, where we use hybrid motion tracking to learn multiple climbing experts from a single reference motion, and distill these experts into a unified depth-based visuomotor climbing policy via hybrid imitation and reinforcement learning. To enable real-world deployment, we leverage vision foundation models to bridge the sim-to-real gap in depth perception. Building on the learned climbing policy, we further train a separate manipulation policy using a dual-agent formulation, allowing stable on-ladder manipulation via teleoperation. Experiments demonstrate that LadderMan achieves robust ladder climbing across a wide range of geometries, successfully transfers to real-world hardware in a zero-shot manner, and supports various manipulation tasks under challenging ladder constraints. Video results are available at https://ladderman-robot.github.io .

</details>


### [223] [TAGA: Terrain-aware Active Gaze Learning for Generalizable Agile Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05880)
*Peizhuo Li,Hongyi Li,Mingfeng Fan,Fangzhou Xu,Shuhao Liao,Yuxuan Ma,Zicheng Zeng,Ze Wang,Yongbin Jin,Yuhong Cao,Hongtao Wang,Guillaume Sartoretti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TAGA框架，通过主动注视机制（Active Gaze）让双足机器人学习关注地形关键区域，在有限计算资源下提升感知密度，实现鲁棒且可泛化的复杂地形行走，包括1.2米的最大实机跨越距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的双足机器人感知运动方法要么依赖全局地形信息导致计算负担重，要么感知覆盖有限；人类在行走时会选择性关注关键地形区域，受此启发，希望在有限计算资源下实现对大尺度复杂地形的精细感知运动控制。

Method: 提出TAGA（Terrain-aware Active Gaze learning framework）框架，融合视觉、本体感知和运动指令，引导模型学习预期线索并主动关注高度扫描图的特定区域，选择性利用信息丰富区域供下游网络处理；通过强化学习自然涌现注视行为，无需额外监督。

Result: 在仿真和实机上实现了鲁棒且可泛化的运动，包括地形感知的落脚点选择、高台跨越、稀疏落脚点穿越，以及1.2米的实机最大跨越距离（同类系统中最大）；在严重感知干扰和环境干扰下仍保持稳定。

Conclusion: 主动注视机制可通过强化学习自然涌现，显著提升训练效率，使双足机器人在有限计算资源下实现大尺度复杂地形的精细感知运动，具备强鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Agile humanoid locomotion across diverse challenging terrain demands both wide perceptual coverage and precise local geometry understanding. Motivated by the way humans selectively look at relevant terrain during locomotion, we introduce TAGA, a Terrain-aware Active Gaze learning framework for Attention-based humanoid control. By fusing vision, proprioception, and motion commands, our framework guides the model to learn anticipatory cues and actively attend to specific areas of the height scan, selectively using these informative regions for the downstream network. This adaptively increases the information density of observations under tight onboard computational constraints, thus enabling fine-grained perceptive locomotion over larger-scale terrains. We find that such gaze behaviors can naturally emerge through reinforcement learning alone, without requiring additional supervision or explicit guidance, significantly improve training efficiency. As a result, the trained policy demonstrates robust and generalizable locomotion in simulation and on hardware, including reliable terrain-aware foothold selection, elevated-platform traversal, competitive sparse-foothold traversal, and the largest reported real-world gap traversal distance of 1.2m among perceptive humanoid locomotion systems, while maintaining stability under severe perceptual disturbances and environmental interference.

</details>


### [224] [A Novel Method with Encoder-Decoder for Cross-Sensor Adaptation in Surface Shape Sensing with Sparse Strain Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05903)
*Shuo Wang,Heng Luo,Dian Jin,Xiaoming Tao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于编码器-解码器架构的表面形状感测方法，结合元学习和少样本自适应策略，使稀疏应变传感器阵列在不同安装条件下快速适应，仅需不到5%的新标注数据和1秒适应时间，即可将误差从23.0mm降至约4.0mm，错误低于5.0mm的点数增加超65%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器阵列因固有差异或安装条件导致的性能变化，使得形状感测结果不一致；传统方法需要大量数据和为每个传感器阵列重新训练独立模型，增加了数据采集、传输和计算的时间和成本。

Method: 采用编码器-解码器架构进行表面形状感测，并引入元学习和少样本自适应策略，实现跨不同传感器阵列组的快速适应。

Result: 跨传感器自适应后，新部署传感器阵列的感测误差约4.0mm，仅需不到5.0%的新标注数据和1秒适应时间；相比无自适应时23.0mm的误差和20分钟的数据采集时间显著提升，误差低于5.0mm的点数增加超65%。

Conclusion: 所提方法显著降低了表面形状感测的成本和训练负担，在软体机器人和可穿戴设备领域具有广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: Performance variations in sensor arrays, caused by intrinsic differences or installation conditions, can lead to inconsistent results during shape sensing. To obtain accurate results, a large amount of data is usually required, and a separate model must be retrained for each sensor array, thereby increasing the cost and time of data acquisition, transmission, and computation. To address this issue, this work proposes an encoder-decoder architecture for surface shape sensing based on sparse strain sensors and further incorporates meta-learning and few-shot adaptation strategies to enable adaptation across different groups of sensor arrays. Experimental results demonstrate that, after the cross-sensor adaptation, a newly deployed sensor array achieves a sensing error of approximately 4.0 mm relying on less than 5.0% newly labeled data and requiring an adaptation time of under 1 second, which represents a substantial improvement from 23.0 mm error without adaptation and 20-minute data collection time required to train a new model. Moreover, the number of points with errors below 5.0 mm increased by more than 65.0%. These results indicate that the proposed method can substantially reduce the cost and training burden of surface shape sensing, and it has broad potential applications in soft robotics and wearable devices.

</details>


### [225] [Learning of Robot Safety Policies via Adversarial Synthetic Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05952)
*Nikolai Dorofeev,Alexey Odinokov,Rostislav Yavorskiy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于对抗博弈的智能体游戏化框架，通过红蓝两队（红队构建危险场景，蓝队优化安全策略）的迭代对抗，高效发现高风险边缘案例，用于机器人安全策略的合成场景学习。目前为问题定义与方案架构的进行中工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机仿真或人工枚举难以有效捕获机器人系统中高风险边缘案例，亟需一种可扩展的方法来系统性地发现潜在故障场景并学习相应的安全策略。

Method: 将场景生成建模为红队（探索失败空间并构建危险情境）与蓝队（逐步优化安全策略以防范风险）之间的对抗博弈，结合经典风险建模、对抗性场景生成与现代学习范式，形成迭代式安全学习框架。

Result: 目前为进行中工作，主要成果为问题形式化定义与所提出的解决方案架构设计。

Conclusion: 该框架为在复杂真实环境中嵌入物理AI系统的安全性提供了一条可扩展的路径，通过对抗性博弈机制实现了对高风险边缘案例的高效发现。

Abstract: In this work, we propose an agentic gamification framework for hazard-informed learning of robot safety policies through synthetic scenarios. We model scenario generation as an adversarial game between two agents: a Red Team that explores the space of potential failures by constructing hazardous situations, and a Blue Team that incrementally refines safety policies to prevent them. This iterative process enables efficient discovery of high-risk edge cases that are unlikely to be captured through random simulation or manual enumeration. By combining classical risk modeling with adversarial scenario generation and modern learning paradigms, this work provides a scalable pathway for embedding safety into Physical AI systems operating in complex real-world environments. The paper describes ongoing work. The contribution is a problem formulation and a proposed solution architecture.

</details>


### [226] [Towards a Data Flywheel for Embodied Intelligence in Logistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05960)
*Anlan Yu,Zaishu Chen,Zhiqing Hong,Daqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向工业具身智能的数据中心化框架，通过构建物流数据飞轮，将日常操作转化为可重用的数据资产，利用世界模型生成长尾包裹操作的可靠监督，并将部署反馈循环用于策略改进。作为初步成果，WM-DAgger框架通过世界模型生成分布外恢复数据用于鲁棒模仿学习，并正在探索如何对齐大规模多模态数据以实现持续策略改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能正从实验室演示走向工业部署，物流行业是关键技术场景。基于学习的策略相比传统感知-规划-控制流水线更具前景，但其可扩展性取决于具身数据的收集、组织和复用方式。现有方法缺乏系统化的数据中心化框架来有效利用工业环境中的多模态数据。

Method: 构建物流数据飞轮框架：(1) 将日常操作转化为可复用数据资产；(2) 使用世界模型为长尾包裹操作生成可靠监督信号；(3) 将部署反馈循环输入策略改进。初步成果WM-DAgger采用基于世界模型的数据聚合框架，合成分布外（OOD）恢复数据用于鲁棒模仿学习。进一步探索大规模野生多模态数据（包括标注的人类演示、未标注的操作视频和系统级机器人日志）的对齐与反馈转换。

Result: 提出了WM-DAgger框架作为初步成果，能够通过世界模型合成分布外恢复数据来增强模仿学习的鲁棒性。展示了将工业物流环境中的日常操作数据转化为可复用数据资产的可行性，并建立了从数据收集到策略改进的闭环流程。

Conclusion: 该数据中心化框架为工业具身智能的规模化部署提供了可行路径，通过数据飞轮机制实现持续的系统改进。未来工作将聚焦于大规模多模态数据的对齐与反馈转化，以进一步提升物流场景下的操作鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence is moving from laboratory demonstrations toward industrial deployment, with the logistics industry serving as a key application scenario. Learning-based policies offer a promising path beyond traditional perception-planning-control pipelines, but their scalability depends on how embodied data can be collected, organized, and reused. This research studies a data-centric framework for industrial embodied intelligence by constructing a logistics data flywheel. Our framework converts daily operations into reusable data assets, uses World Models to generate reliable supervision for long-tail parcel manipulation, and feeds deployment feedback back into policy improvement. As an initial result, \textit{WM-DAgger} introduces a World-Model-based data aggregation framework that synthesizes out-of-distribution recovery data for robust imitation learning. Building on this result, ongoing work explores how large-scale in-the-wild multimodal data, including labeled human demonstrations, unlabeled operational videos, and system-level robot logs, can be aligned for policy learning and transformed into feedback for continual system improvement.

</details>


### [227] [World-Language-Action Model for Unified World Modeling, Language Reasoning, and Action Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05979)
*Yi Yang,Zhihong Liu,Siqi Kou,Yiyang Chen,Yanzhe Hu,Jianbo Zhou,Boyuan Zhao,Zhijie Wei,Xiao Xia,Xueqi Li,Pengfei Liu,Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出世界-语言-动作（WLA）模型，一种结合世界建模与语言推理能力的具身基础模型，通过自回归Transformer联合预测文本子任务、子目标图像和机器人动作，在2B参数规模下实现40ms推理速度，在多项仿真和真实世界任务中达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型（WAM）擅长从大规模自我中心视频中学习物理动态，但缺乏语言推理能力来求解复杂长时任务；而视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型具备语言推理能力，但难以从无动作标注的视频中学习。WLA旨在融合两者的优势，同时实现世界建模与语言推理。

Method: 采用自回归（AR）Transformer替代WAM中的双向扩散Transformer，联合预测下一状态（包括语义级文本意图和细粒度物理动态）。物理动态由世界专家（World Expert）的世界建模目标监督，并用于简化动作专家（Action Expert）的状态-动作相关性建模。使用元查询（meta-queries）使世界预测隐式影响动作生成，推理时可禁用世界预测，也可激活以实现测试时扩展（test-time scaling）。

Result: WLA-0（2B活跃参数）在NVIDIA RTX 5090上实现40ms/次推理；RoboTwin2.0 Clean任务成功率达92.94%，RMBench任务成功率达56.5%，均达到SOTA水平；并能直接从跨本体机器人视频中学习新任务，无需动作标注。

Conclusion: WLA模型通过统一世界建模与语言推理，在保持高效推理的同时实现了多任务和长时任务的SOTA性能，并具备从无动作标注视频中学习的能力，为具身基础模型提供了新的范式。

Abstract: We propose world-language-action (WLA) models as a new class of embodied foundation models. WLA takes textual instructions, images, and robot states as inputs to jointly predict textual subtasks, subgoal images, and robot actions, conjoining the \emph{world modeling interface} to learn from extensive egocentric videos as in the world-action model (WAM) and the \emph{language reasoning} capacities to solve complex long-horizon tasks as in vision-language-action (VLA) models. At the core of WLA lies an \emph{autoregressive (AR)} Transformer backbone, instead of a bidirectional diffusion Transformer as in WAMs, to predict the \emph{next state}, comprising the \emph{semantic-level} textual intention and complementary \emph{fine-grained} physical dynamics. The physical dynamics are supervised by the world modeling objective based on a dedicated World Expert, and are leveraged to ease the characterization of the state-action correlation for the Action Expert. WLA leverages meta-queries to make the world prediction \emph{implicitly} impact the action generation so that the former can be disabled during inference. The world prediction can also be activated to enable test-time scaling for improved robot control. Our WLA-0 prototype, with 2B active parameters, achieves 40 ms per inference on an NVIDIA RTX 5090. Evaluations across simulated and real-world environments demonstrate that WLA-0 achieves state-of-the-art multi-task and long-horizon learning abilities, e.g., 92.94\% success rate on RoboTwin2.0 Clean and 56.5\% success rate on RMBench. WLA-0 also holds the promise to learn novel tasks directly from \emph{cross-embodiment robot videos} without action annotations.

</details>


### [228] [Merging model-based control with multi-agent reinforcement learning for multi-agent cooperative teaming strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06011)
*Christian Llanes,Spencer W. Jensen,Samuel Coogan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合多智能体强化学习（MARL）与模型预测控制（MPC）的框架MA-AC-MPC，用于实现多智能体协作任务中的安全、动态可行动作。在追逃场景和异构环境（无人机+全向轮机器人）中，MA-AC-MPC在硬件上实现了100%的成功率，优于基线方法MA-AC-MLP的60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体强化学习擅长从离散非可微奖励中学习长期合作策略，但难以保证短期安全性和动态可行性；模型预测控制鲁棒性强、能提供安全动态可行动作，但仅限于短时域。两者的优势互补尚未在多智能体协作场景中得到有效结合。

Method: 将actor-critic模型预测控制（AC-MPC）扩展到多智能体场景，提出多智能体actor-critic模型预测控制（MA-AC-MPC）算法，使每个智能体在长时域通过MARL学习合作策略，同时在短时域利用MPC进行快速重规划以保证安全与动态可行性。

Result: 在追逃场景中，使用MA-AC-MPC的逃避者团队策略优于MA-AC-MLP；在异构环境（无人机+全向轮机器人）中，MA-AC-MPC硬件实验成功率为100%，而MA-AC-MLP仅为60%。同时在两种环境中均验证了算法的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MA-AC-MPC成功融合了MARL的长期策略学习能力与MPC的短期安全可行性保证，在多智能体协作任务中显著提升了性能与鲁棒性，尤其在实际硬件部署中表现突出。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a framework that combines multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) with model-based control to achieve safe, dynamically feasible actions in cooperative multi-agent tasks. Multi-agent reinforcement learning provides the advantage of learning cooperative policies for multi-agent teams from discrete non-differentiable rewards in a long planning horizon. Model-predictive control is robust and offers safe, dynamically feasible actions in a fast replanning framework for short horizons. We propose an algorithm that extends actor-critic model predictive control for MARL which we refer to as multi-agent actor-critic model predictive control (MA-AC-MPC). We demonstrate the capabilities of this algorithm by applying it to a multi-agent pursuit-evasion scenario. Specifically, we compare the evader team's strategy using the MA-AC-MPC model and a multi-layer perceptron model (MA-AC-MLP). The pursuer team uses augmented proportional navigation as it is accepted as an advanced adversarial control law. We also provide an example with a heterogeneous environment where a drone and omni-wheeled rover cooperate to achieve repeatable and successful landing with 100% success rate in hardware for MA-AC-MPC compared to 60% for MA-AC-MLP. We demonstrate the robustness of the proposed MA-AC-MPC algorithm in hardware for both environments.

</details>


### [229] [RealDexUMI: A Wearable Universal Manipulation Interface for Dexterous Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06033)
*Chaoyi Xu,Yixuan Jiang,Jiahui Huan,Yuhui Fu,Haoyu Zhou,Weitian Yuan,Jiayi Yu,Wanpeng Zhang,Haoqi Yuan,Zongqing Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RealDexUMI是一种基于可穿戴通用操作接口的灵巧操控数据采集与部署系统，通过共享末端执行器模块（轻量化灵巧手、手内视觉、指尖触觉）和手掌侧同构遥操作手套，实现了无需重定向、零间隙的灵巧操控数据采集，在8个真实机器人任务上取得88.75%的平均成功率，并支持跨三款实体迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧操控数据采集管线要么因重定向或具身转换而丢失可部署的灵巧性，要么依赖成本高昂且缺乏直观接触感知能力的机器人专用遥操作，难以规模化扩展。

Method: 设计了一个共享灵巧末端执行器模块（轻量化手+手内视觉+指尖触觉），配合手掌侧同构遥操作手套，直接将人类手指输入映射为机器人手关节指令，实现无需重定向的实时、直观、精确控制，确保采集与部署阶段的手内观察、触觉信号、接触和手部动作完全匹配。

Result: 在8个真实机器人任务（涵盖精细操作、接触丰富、长时域和双臂操作）上，基于RealDexUMI数据训练的策略平均成功率达88.75%，能泛化到未见初始位姿，并迁移至三种不同实体。

Conclusion: RealDexUMI通过消除重定向和具身转换带来的信息损失，提供了一种可规模化的灵巧操控数据采集方案，显著提升了策略的部署性能与泛化能力。

Abstract: Learning dexterous manipulation requires demonstrations that preserve fine hand-object interactions while remaining executable at deployment. Existing pipelines either lose deployable dexterity through retargeting or embodiment conversion, or rely on robot-specific teleoperation that is costly to scale and often lacks intuitive, contact-aware control for dexterous data collection. We present RealDexUMI, a wearable universal manipulation interface built around a shared dexterous end-effector module that integrates a lightweight dexterous hand, in-hand vision, and fingertip tactile sensing. A palm-side isomorphic teleoperation glove maps human finger inputs to robot-hand joint commands, enabling real-time, retargeting-free, intuitive, and precise hand control. The shared hand and sensing modules yield zero-gap end-effector data, with matched in-hand observations, tactile signals, contacts, and hand actions between collection and deployment. Across eight real-robot tasks spanning fine-grained, contact-rich, long-horizon, and bimanual manipulation, policies trained on RealDexUMI data achieve an average success rate of 88.75%, generalize to unseen initial poses, and transfer across three embodiments. Website: https://research.beingbeyond.com/realdexumi

</details>


### [230] [L-SDPPO: Policy Optimization of Spiking Diffusion Policy for Intra-vehicular Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06049)
*Liwen Zhang,Dong Zhou,Guanghui Sun,Yifei Zheng,Yuhui Hu,Kaihong Ouyang,Zuoquan Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对航天器内机器人操作中微重力环境导致的物体非约束漂移和能源限制问题，提出L-SDPPO框架，将尖峰扩散策略（SDP）与强化学习（RL）优化结合，并引入状态依赖延迟注入（SDLI）机制模拟生物神经延迟以增强动态时空特征感知，在五个典型舱内任务上实现了更高成功率和更低能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法在微重力环境下难以应对物体不受约束的漂移和复杂的多模态动作分布；扩散策略虽能建模复杂动作但迭代采样能耗过高，不适用于航天器有限的能源预算。

Method: 提出L-SDPPO框架：1) 使用尖峰扩散策略（SDP）建模复杂多模态动作；2) 通过强化学习（RL）优化SDP；3) 提出状态依赖延迟注入（SDLI）机制，通过模拟生物神经延迟动态调节输入信息的时序，增强对微重力下动态时空特征的感知。

Result: 在舱门开启、精密容器封盖等五个代表性舱内日常任务中，该方法相较于最先进的机器人操作方法，持续实现了更高的成功率和更低的能耗。

Conclusion: L-SDPPO是一种可行的舱内机器人操作方法，在能效和任务成功率方面均优于现有方法，适用于航天器内受限能源环境下的机器人操作。

Abstract: Intra-vehicular robots in spacecraft help reduce astronaut workload and improve mission efficiency. Recent research focuses on using deep learning methods to achieve the acute control required for operations in these complex environments. However, objects exhibit unpredictable, unconstrained drift without gravitational damping. These factors demand robustness against complex multimodal action distributions. Diffusion policies (DP) can model these complex actions, but their iterative sampling process consumes too much energy for the limited power budgets of spacecraft. We therefore propose a low-energy intra-vehicular robotic manipulation framework, L-SDPPO, in which the Spiking Diffusion Policy (SDP) is optimized with a reinforcement learning (RL) algorithm. Furthermore, to address the insufficient perception of dynamic spatiotemporal features in microgravity, we propose the statedependent latency injection (SDLI) mechanism, which mimics biological neural delays to dynamically regulate the timing of input information. Evaluation on five representative intra-vehicular daily tasks (e.g., hatch opening and precision container capping) shows that our method consistently achieves higher success rates and lower energy consumption, compared to the state-of-the-art robotic manipulation methods. These results demonstrate our method is a viable intra-vehicular robotic manipulation method.

</details>


### [231] [A Conversational Framework for Human-Robot Collaborative Manipulation with Distributed Generative AI models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06061)
*Arash Ghasemzadeh Kakroudi,Roel Pieters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于ROS 2的分布式对话框架，通过集成轻量级语言和视觉-语言模型实现人机协作操作，支持自由形式指令输入、视觉目标定位以及操作前的人工确认机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人机协作操作面临自然语言理解、视觉目标定位与实时执行之间的集成挑战，现有方案在灵活性、部署效率和安全性方面存在不足。

Method: 采用ROS 2节点化架构，将语言理解、视觉定位、任务编排和运动执行解耦为独立节点；使用VLM生成图像空间目标，通过深度信息和标定转换为机器人坐标系目标；前端仪表盘展示中间意图和定位覆盖层，并要求操作员显式确认后才执行运动。

Result: 在Franka FR3平台上进行了端到端任务可靠性实验，评估了不同场景模糊度下的延迟表现，并对比了多种LLM/VLM配置在相同流水线中的性能。

Conclusion: 该分布式框架能够有效整合自然语言指令与视觉定位，保障操作安全性，并支持灵活部署；代码和文档已开源。

Abstract: This paper presents a distributed conversational framework for human-robot collaborative manipulation that integrates local language and vision-language models (VLMs) with a Robot Operating System 2 (ROS 2)-based execution stack. Language understanding, visual grounding, orchestration, and motion execution run as separate ROS 2 nodes, enabling flexible deployment across distributed hardware while maintaining a responsive control loop. From free-form user commands, the system generates structured action requests for pick, place, and handover. It uses a VLM to return image-space targets, which are converted into metric robot-frame goals using depth and calibration. A web dashboard exposes intermediate intent and grounding overlays (pixel, depth, and robot-frame) and requires explicit operator confirmation before any motion is executed. Experiments on a Franka FR3 platform evaluate end-to-end task reliability and latency under increasing working table scene ambiguity and compare alternative LLM/VLM configurations in the same pipeline. Code and full documentation are available at [github.com/cogrob-tuni/franka-llm](https://github.com/cogrob-tuni/franka-llm).

</details>


### [232] [3D Underwater Path Planning via Generative Flow Field Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06077)
*Zachary Cooper-Baldock,Paulo E. Santos,Russell S. A. Brinkworth,Karl Sammut*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出用条件生成对抗网络（cGAN）替代高成本RANS CFD仿真，为自主水下航行器（AUV）在宿主平台螺旋桨尾流中的发射与回收提供实时三维流场预测，并集成到能量加权的A*路径规划框架中。在550种流动条件下生成19,800条轨迹进行基准测试，cGAN代理模型恢复约45-60%的CFD能量收益，推理时间仅28-146微秒，首次系统量化了cGAN预测水动力场在三维海洋机器人路径规划中的下游价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUV在行进宿主平台船体中进行发射与回收需穿越复杂三维螺旋桨尾流，其水动力结构无法用均匀流模型表征。高保真RANS CFD虽能精确解析该结构用于路径规划，但计算成本过高无法机载实时使用，因此需要低延迟且高精度的替代方案。

Method: 集成两种cGAN架构（正则化PatchGAN和带自注意力的2D3DGAN）作为RANS CFD数据的即插即用替代品，构建层次化流水线从标量操作条件输入合成完整128³体素流场体积，结合能量加权A*路径规划框架在三维空间中进行轨迹生成。

Result: 在550种流动条件下生成19,800条轨迹进行基准测试，全CFD尾流知识相比均匀流规划降低能耗5.7-12.5%，高速度尾流核遭遇减少高达77.8%。cGAN代理模型恢复约45-60%的CFD能量收益和高速度单元规避收益，推理时间28-146微秒，适合边缘设备使用。

Conclusion: 首次系统量化了cGAN预测水动力场在三维海洋机器人路径规划中的下游价值，证明cGAN可在保持边缘设备推理速度的同时提供有意义的路径规划收益，为实时机载水动力场预测与路径规划结合提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater vehicle (AUV) launch and recovery (LAR) into the hull of an advancing host platform requires traversal of a complex, three-dimensional propeller wake whose hydrodynamic structure cannot be characterised by a uniform current model. High-fidelity Reynolds-Averaged Navier-Stokes (RANS) Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) simulations resolve this structure with sufficient accuracy for path planning, but their computational cost renders them impractical for onboard use. We address this gap by integrating two conditional generative adversarial network (cGAN) architectures -- a regularised PatchGAN and a 2D3DGAN with self-attention -- as drop-in replacements for RANS CFD data within a three-dimensional, energy-weighted A* path planning framework. Both generators are driven by a hierarchical pipeline that synthesises full $128^3$ voxel flow field volumes from scalar operating condition inputs alone, with end-to-end inference times of approximately 28-146 $μ$s, compared to hours for a single RANS computation. We benchmark all four environmental knowledge levels: uniform current, ground-truth CFD, PatchGAN, and 2D3DGAN~SA across 19,800 independently generated trajectories spanning 550 distinct flow conditions. Full CFD wake knowledge reduces energy expenditure by 5.7-12.5% and high-velocity wake-core encounters by up to 77.8% relative to uniform-current planning, with both benefits scaling with operating severity. The cGAN surrogates recover approximately 45-60% of the CFD energy benefit and high-velocity cell avoidance benefit while operating at inference speeds compatible with edge device use. These results provide the first systematic quantification of the downstream path planning value of cGAN-predicted hydrodynamic fields in a three-dimensional maritime robotics application.

</details>


### [233] [Towards Realistic 3D Sonar Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06130)
